# Site variables own furnace condition and paid receipts across control changes.
SHL_apply_starting_economy = {
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_debt value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_inflation value = 5 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_deficit_pressure value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_fiscal_stress value = 8 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_price_shock value = 6 }
	ADISCORD_economy_sync_starting_accounting = yes
}

SHL_receive_250 = {
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 250 }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 250 }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

SHL_initialize_furnaces = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SHL
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_furnaces_initialized }
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_fresh_campaign_contract_v1
		}
		set_country_flag = SHL_furnaces_initialized
		set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_recipient value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_regular_supply value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_supply_settings_open value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_floor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 20 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_stock value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_number value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pp_gain value = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_course_stability value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_treasury_capacity_bonus value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_cartel_pp value = -0.20 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_cartel_consumer_goods value = 0.10 }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_cartel_influence }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_guard_org value = -0.10 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_guard_planning value = -0.20 }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_shahrabad_army }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_workshop_output value = -0.10 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_workshop_construction value = -0.10 }
		SHL_initialize_programmes = yes
		# The book of furnaces is read by a human player only; its dispatcher
		# requeues itself every 90 days until the fifth chapter.
		set_variable = { var = SHL_book_next value = 1 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_book.99 days = 4 }
		SHL_initialize_tensions = yes
		set_power_balance = {
			id = SHL_conclave_balance
			set_value = -0.20
		}
		287 = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 12 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 }
		}
		288 = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 15 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 }
		}
		289 = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 18 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 }
		}
		290 = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 36 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 }
		}
		291 = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 24 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 }
		}
		292 = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 27 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 }
		}
		293 = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 30 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 }
		}
		294 = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 33 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 }
		}
		295 = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 36 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 }
		}
		remove_ideas = SHL_nine_furnaces_compact
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		SHL_schedule_cycle = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.2 days = 2 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.110 days = 168 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.120 days = 14 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.121 days = 70 }
	}
}

SHL_schedule_cycle = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_cycle_pending }
		}
		set_country_flag = SHL_cycle_pending
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.1 days = 28 }
	}
}

# A 28-day event rescheduled from its own immediate block fires one hour early
# each cycle; once that hour crosses midnight the flag age reads 27 days.
# Callbacks queued by an older clock are still younger than this threshold.
SHL_open_ballot = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 0 compare = equals }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_conclave_ballot }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_ballot_due }
			SHL_count_ballot = yes
		}
		else = { activate_mission = SHL_conclave_ballot }
	}
}

SHL_count_ballot = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = SHL_ballot_due
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 0 compare = equals }
		}
		clr_country_flag = SHL_ballot_due
		remove_ideas = SHL_hereditary_seats
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_ballot_house_majority = yes }
			SHL_commit_house_course = yes
			complete_national_focus = SHL_admit_tenth_house
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SHL_ballot_city_majority = yes }
			SHL_commit_city_course = yes
			complete_national_focus = SHL_rights_without_fire
		}
		else = {
			SHL_commit_state_course = yes
			complete_national_focus = SHL_state_furnaces
		}
	}
}

SHL_run_cycle = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = { flag = SHL_cycle_pending days > 26 } }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cycle_pending
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_country_operable = yes }
			SHL_produce_period = yes
			SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
			SHL_update_tensions = yes
			SHL_check_obligation_deadline = yes
			SHL_check_crises = yes
			SHL_open_ballot = yes
			ADISCORD_south_crisis_cycle = yes
			SHL_crisis_draw = yes
			SHL_life_draw = yes
		}
		# A capitulated country keeps its clock; stale callbacks cannot advance it.
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			set_country_flag = SHL_cycle_pending
			SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.1 days = 28 }
		}
	}
}

SHL_produce_period = {
	clear_variable = SHL_convoy_dispatch
	SHL_refresh_delivery_terms = yes
	SHL_ai_allocate_deliveries = yes
	SHL_reconcile_obligation = yes
	clear_variable = SHL_tenth_crest_delivered
	set_variable = { var = SHL_last_cycle_income value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_cycle_lease value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_idle_sites value = 0 }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_number value = 1 }
	# The metal exchange quotes one price per cycle; the cartel keeps 60.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_export_mode value = 1 compare = equals } }
		SHL_roll_exchange_price = yes
	}
	else = { set_variable = { var = SHL_exchange_price value = 60 } }
	# A lease without an overlord ends before the pour, so no share leaves the cash.
	SHL_seal_release_without_overlord = yes
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_has_priorities value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_regular_supply value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_stock value = SHL_reserve_floor compare = greater_than }
			SHL_reserve_has_packet = yes
			SHL_has_reserve_priorities = yes
		}
		SHL_plan_priority_deliveries = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_has_priorities value = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_grant_1 value = SHL_planned_transfer_1 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_grant_2 value = SHL_planned_transfer_2 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_grant_3 value = SHL_planned_transfer_3 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_grant_4 value = SHL_planned_transfer_4 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_grant_5 value = SHL_planned_transfer_5 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_grant_6 value = SHL_planned_transfer_6 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_grant_7 value = SHL_planned_transfer_7 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_grant_8 value = SHL_planned_transfer_8 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_grant_9 value = SHL_planned_transfer_9 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_grant_10 value = SHL_planned_transfer_10 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_has_priorities value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_site_grant value = SHL_cycle_grant_1 }
	}
	287 = { SHL_produce_site = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_has_priorities value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_site_grant value = SHL_cycle_grant_2 }
	}
	288 = { SHL_produce_site = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_has_priorities value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_site_grant value = SHL_cycle_grant_3 }
	}
	289 = { SHL_produce_site = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_has_priorities value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_site_grant value = SHL_cycle_grant_4 }
	}
	290 = { SHL_produce_site = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_has_priorities value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_site_grant value = SHL_cycle_grant_5 }
	}
	291 = { SHL_produce_site = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_has_priorities value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_site_grant value = SHL_cycle_grant_6 }
	}
	292 = { SHL_produce_site = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_has_priorities value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_site_grant value = SHL_cycle_grant_7 }
	}
	293 = { SHL_produce_site = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_has_priorities value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_site_grant value = SHL_cycle_grant_8 }
	}
	294 = { SHL_produce_site = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_has_priorities value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_site_grant value = SHL_cycle_grant_9 }
	}
	295 = { SHL_produce_site = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_has_priorities value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_site_grant value = SHL_cycle_grant_10 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 4 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			294 = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_running value = 1 compare = equals }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_period_cost value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_period_wear value = 6 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_period_income value = 60 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_period_cost value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_period_wear value = 10 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_period_income value = 90 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 2 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_period_wear value = 3 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_period_income value = 25 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_period_income value = SHL_income_deduction }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_period_income min = 0 max = 90 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_regular_supply value = 1 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_stock value = SHL_tenth_period_cost compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_missing value = SHL_tenth_period_cost }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_missing value = SHL_tenth_stock }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve value = SHL_reserve_stock }
			SHL_settle_furnace_order = yes
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_stock value = SHL_order_delivered }
			clear_variable = SHL_order_delivered
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_stock value = SHL_tenth_period_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_stock value = SHL_tenth_period_cost }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_wear value = SHL_tenth_period_wear }
			SHL_deliver_tenth_heat = yes
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_tenth_period_income }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_tenth_period_income }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_last_cycle_income value = SHL_tenth_period_income }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_shortages value = 0 }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_shortages value = 1 }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_wear value = 12 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_wear value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_shortages value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_running value = 0 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = SHL_tenth_stopped_tt
		}
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_wear min = 0 max = 100 }
	}
	# Kerdan's paid Veyr duty covers this production of all ten furnaces; the
	# second countdown ends the exemption before the next cycle is priced.
	if = {
		limit = { 292 = { check_variable = { var = SHL_duty_paid_cycles value = 0 compare = greater_than } } }
		292 = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_duty_paid_cycles value = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_duty_paid_cycles value = 0 compare = less_than_or_equals } }
				clear_variable = SHL_duty_paid_cycles
			}
		}
		SHL_refresh_income_deduction = yes
	}
	# Deliver relief after all nine sites, so recipient order cannot grant a free cycle.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_progress value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		SHL_complete_obligation = yes
	}
	# House credits repay 25 per cycle for six cycles each.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_credit_cycles value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 25 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 25 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_last_cycle_income value = 25 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_credit_cycles value = 1 }
	}
	# Metal contracts settle after the furnaces, inside the same cycle report.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_metal_clients value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		SHL_metal_settle_clients = yes
	}
	# A war indemnity is paid from the furnaces lit in this cycle.
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_indemnity_due }
		SHL_pay_indemnity = yes
	}
	# District, treaty and retainer income of the second-row focuses: once per
	# cycle, in country scope, never through the nine site scopes above. May be negative.
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_cycle_bonus_income }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_bonus_income value = 0 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_cycle_bonus_income }
	}
	else = {
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_cycle_bonus_income }
	}
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_last_cycle_income value = SHL_cycle_bonus_income }
	# The defeat chapter settles its terms, loan and underground after the furnaces.
	SHL_seal_cycle = yes
}

# COUNTRY, PREV is the furnace district. The quote is shared by production and the forecast.
SHL_quote_site_wear = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_site_wear_quote value = 6 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_site_wear_quote value = 10 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_site_wear_quote value = 3 }
	}
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_site_wear_quote value = PREV.SHL_furnace_training }
	if = {
		limit = { PREV = { SHL_has_cooling_loop = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_site_wear_quote value = 2 }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_site_wear_quote min = 0 max = 100 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			PREV = { SHL_site_on_qanat_border = yes }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 1 compare = equals }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_site_wear_quote value = 4 }
	}
}

# Country scope. Both the live order and the forecast quote the same shortfall
# against the budget and reserve available when this furnace is reached.
SHL_quote_furnace_order = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_buy_packets value = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_order_missing value = 1 compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_buy_packets value = 2 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_price value = SHL_order_buy_packets }
	multiply_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_price value = SHL_auto_packet_price }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve_packets value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_order_budget value = SHL_order_price compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve_packets value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_order_buy_packets value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_order_budget value = SHL_auto_packet_price compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve_packets value = 2 }
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_buy_packets value = SHL_order_reserve_packets }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_price value = SHL_order_buy_packets }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_price value = SHL_auto_packet_price }
	}
}

SHL_apply_reserve_floor = {
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve value = SHL_reserve_floor }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve min = 0 }
}

# The district reads the delivery in state scope; X reads it in country scope.
# Each caller credits its own bunker before the next furnace places an order.
SHL_settle_furnace_order = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_budget value = ADISCORD_economy_treasury }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve value = 1 compare = equals } }
		SHL_apply_reserve_floor = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_has_priorities value = 1 compare = equals } }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve max = SHL_cycle_site_grant }
		}
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve value = 0 } }
	SHL_quote_furnace_order = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_order_delivered value = SHL_order_reserve_packets }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_stock value = SHL_order_reserve_packets }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_order_buy_packets value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_order_price compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_order_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_order_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_order_delivered value = SHL_order_buy_packets }
	}
}

SHL_produce_site = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
		}
		# A mobilisation quota overrides any recipient chosen in the meantime,
		# but never the namestnik's lease.
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = SHL_quota_site
				NOT = { SHL_seal_site_leased = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 1 }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_period_cost value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_period_income value = 60 }
		if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 1 compare = equals } } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_period_cost value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_period_income value = 90 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 2 compare = equals } } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_period_income value = 25 }
		}
		SHL = { SHL_quote_site_wear = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_period_wear value = SHL.SHL_site_wear_quote }
		SHL = { clear_variable = SHL_site_wear_quote }
		# PREV is SHL: every producing site pays the same active deductions.
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_period_income value = PREV.SHL_income_deduction }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_period_income min = 0 max = 90 }
		# Exported metal follows the exchange quote instead of the cartel's 60.
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 compare = equals }
				SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_export_mode value = 1 compare = equals } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_period_income value = PREV.SHL_exchange_price }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_period_income value = 60 }
			clamp_variable = { var = SHL_period_income min = 0 max = 90 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_regular_supply value = 1 compare = equals } }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL_period_cost compare = less_than }
			}
			SHL = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_missing value = PREV.SHL_period_cost }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_missing value = PREV.SHL_furnace_stock }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve value = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_census_of_nine }
					set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve value = SHL_reserve_stock }
				}
				SHL_settle_furnace_order = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL.SHL_order_delivered }
			SHL = { clear_variable = SHL_order_delivered }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL_period_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL_period_cost }
			# Commission the installed loop on its first supplied pour.
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_qanat_works value = 1 compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_qanat_works value = 3 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = SHL_period_wear }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 0 }
			# Explicit country input avoids reading the state after the scope switch.
			set_variable = { var = SHL_period_cash value = SHL_period_income }
			if = {
				limit = { SHL_obligation_here = yes }
				SHL_apply_site_obligation = yes
			}
			else = { SHL_deliver_site_heat = yes }
			SHL = { SHL_collect_site_income = yes }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 1 }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 12 }
			SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -3 } }
			SHL_house_furnace_penalty = yes
			SHL = {
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_idle_sites value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_shortage_notice_seen } }
					set_country_flag = SHL_shortage_notice_seen
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.3 }
				}
			}
		}
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear min = 0 max = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -3 } }
			SHL_house_furnace_penalty = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 90 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 100 compare = less_than }
				SHL = { SHL_accident_ready = yes }
			}
			set_state_flag = SHL_accident_site
			SHL = {
				set_country_flag = SHL_accident_pending
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.40 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
			SHL_extinguish_site = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		# An occupied district of Shahrabad costs its house every cycle.
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = SHL
			NOT = { is_controlled_by = SHL }
		}
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -5 } }
		SHL_apply_house_loyalty = yes
	}
	# A one-cycle war order releases its arsenal quota and restores the chosen recipient.
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_war_orders_prev_recipient }
		clr_state_flag = SHL_quota_site
		set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = SHL_war_orders_prev_recipient }
		clear_variable = SHL_war_orders_prev_recipient
	}
}

# PREV is the paying production site; this effect has no other callers.
SHL_collect_site_income = {
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = PREV.SHL_period_cash }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = PREV.SHL_period_cash }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_last_cycle_income value = PREV.SHL_period_cash }
}

SHL_extinguish_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 0 }
		clear_variable = SHL_restart_settled
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 1 }
			SHL = {
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 5 }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -10 }
			}
			SHL_apply_house_loyalty = yes
		}
		SHL = {
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 5 }
			SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		}
		SHL = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_first_extinction_seen } }
				set_country_flag = SHL_first_extinction_seen
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.4 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# The packet quote is re-read before the debit, so a Svetlogorye contract or
# embargo signed since the last refresh prices this purchase.
SHL_begin_supply = {
	SHL_refresh_packet_price = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				SHL_furnace_site_idle = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 2 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_packet_price compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_packet_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_packet_price }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_deposit value = PREV.SHL_packet_price }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_pp value = 0 }
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_supply = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 1 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 2 compare = less_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock min = 0 max = 3 }
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_deposit
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_pp
				clear_variable = SHL_operation
			}
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_operation = yes }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_begin_repair = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				SHL_furnace_site_idle = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 80 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 80 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 80 }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_deposit value = 80 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_pp value = 0 }
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_repair = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 2 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes } }
			FROM = {
				subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 30 }
				clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear min = 0 max = 100 }
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_deposit
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_pp
				clear_variable = SHL_operation
			}
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_operation = yes }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Focus overhaul: every controlled lit furnace loses 15 wear once.
SHL_overhaul_furnaces = {
	287 = { SHL_overhaul_site = yes }
	288 = { SHL_overhaul_site = yes }
	289 = { SHL_overhaul_site = yes }
	290 = { SHL_overhaul_site = yes }
	291 = { SHL_overhaul_site = yes }
	292 = { SHL_overhaul_site = yes }
	293 = { SHL_overhaul_site = yes }
	294 = { SHL_overhaul_site = yes }
	295 = { SHL_overhaul_site = yes }
	SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
}

SHL_overhaul_site = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 15 }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear min = 0 max = 100 }
	}
}

SHL_begin_training = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				SHL_furnace_site_idle = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 0 compare = equals }
			}
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_political_power < 25 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 100 }
		add_political_power = -25
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 3 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_deposit value = 100 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_pp value = 25 }
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_training = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 3 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes } }
			FROM = {
				set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 1 }
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_deposit
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_pp
				clear_variable = SHL_operation
			}
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_operation = yes }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_begin_restart = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				SHL_furnace_site_idle = yes
				has_variable = SHL_restart_settled
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 0 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 120 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 120 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 120 }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 4 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_deposit value = 120 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_pp value = 0 }
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_restart = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 4 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes } }
			FROM = {
				set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 20 }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 0 }
				clear_variable = SHL_restart_settled
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_deposit
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_pp
				clear_variable = SHL_operation
			}
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_operation = yes }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_cancel_operation = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { has_variable = SHL_operation_deposit } }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = FROM.SHL_operation_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = FROM.SHL_operation_deposit }
		add_political_power = FROM.SHL_operation_pp
		FROM = {
			clear_variable = SHL_operation_deposit
			clear_variable = SHL_operation_pp
			clear_variable = SHL_operation
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_refresh_furnaces = {
	SHL_refresh_starting_institutions = yes
	SHL_reconcile_obligation = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_running_count value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_charters_active value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_charters_running value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_status_1 value = 2 }
	287 = {
		SHL_reconcile_charter_site = yes
		PREV = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_wear_1 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_wear }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_owner_1 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_owner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes }
			SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_status_1 value = 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals } }
				SHL = {
					add_to_variable = { var = SHL_running_count value = 1 }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_status_1 value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_house_opposed }
					}
					SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_status_2 value = 2 }
	288 = {
		SHL_reconcile_charter_site = yes
		PREV = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_wear_2 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_wear }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_owner_2 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_owner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes }
			SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_status_2 value = 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals } }
				SHL = {
					add_to_variable = { var = SHL_running_count value = 1 }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_status_2 value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_house_opposed }
					}
					SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_status_3 value = 2 }
	289 = {
		SHL_reconcile_charter_site = yes
		PREV = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_wear_3 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_wear }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_owner_3 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_owner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes }
			SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_status_3 value = 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals } }
				SHL = {
					add_to_variable = { var = SHL_running_count value = 1 }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_status_3 value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_house_opposed }
					}
					SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_status_4 value = 2 }
	290 = {
		SHL_reconcile_charter_site = yes
		PREV = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_wear_4 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_wear }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_owner_4 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_owner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes }
			SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_status_4 value = 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals } }
				SHL = {
					add_to_variable = { var = SHL_running_count value = 1 }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_status_4 value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_house_opposed }
					}
					SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_status_5 value = 2 }
	291 = {
		SHL_reconcile_charter_site = yes
		PREV = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_wear_5 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_wear }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_owner_5 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_owner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes }
			SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_status_5 value = 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals } }
				SHL = {
					add_to_variable = { var = SHL_running_count value = 1 }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_status_5 value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_house_opposed }
					}
					SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_status_6 value = 2 }
	292 = {
		SHL_reconcile_charter_site = yes
		PREV = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_wear_6 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_wear }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_owner_6 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_owner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes }
			SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_status_6 value = 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals } }
				SHL = {
					add_to_variable = { var = SHL_running_count value = 1 }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_status_6 value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_house_opposed }
					}
					SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_status_7 value = 2 }
	293 = {
		SHL_reconcile_charter_site = yes
		PREV = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_wear_7 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_wear }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_owner_7 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_owner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes }
			SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_status_7 value = 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals } }
				SHL = {
					add_to_variable = { var = SHL_running_count value = 1 }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_status_7 value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_house_opposed }
					}
					SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_status_8 value = 2 }
	294 = {
		SHL_reconcile_charter_site = yes
		PREV = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_wear_8 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_wear }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_owner_8 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_owner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes }
			SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_status_8 value = 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals } }
				SHL = {
					add_to_variable = { var = SHL_running_count value = 1 }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_status_8 value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_house_opposed }
					}
					SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_status_9 value = 2 }
	295 = {
		SHL_reconcile_charter_site = yes
		PREV = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_wear_9 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_wear }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_owner_9 value = PREV.SHL_furnace_owner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes }
			SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_status_9 value = 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals } }
				SHL = {
					add_to_variable = { var = SHL_running_count value = 1 }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_status_9 value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_house_opposed }
					}
					SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_running value = 1 compare = equals }
			294 = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_running_count value = 1 }
		# The tenth house votes once the council of ten has seated it.
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 1 compare = equals }
				has_completed_focus = SHL_h_council_of_ten
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 1 }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_efficiency_factor value = SHL_running_count }
	multiply_variable = { var = SHL_efficiency_factor value = 0.0066666667 }
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_efficiency_factor min = 0 max = 0.06 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_resources_factor value = SHL_efficiency_factor }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pp_gain value = SHL_house_votes }
	multiply_variable = { var = SHL_pp_gain value = 0.0055555556 }
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_pp_gain min = 0 max = 0.05 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pp_gain value = 0.05 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 3 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pp_gain value = 0.10 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pp_gain value = 0.06 }
	}
	SHL_refresh_income_deduction = yes
	SHL_refresh_delivery_terms = yes
	SHL_refresh_house_pressure = yes
	SHL_refresh_delivery_support = yes
	SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = SHL_conclave_industry } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_conclave_industry }
	}
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

SHL_begin_engineers = {
	SHL_refresh_programme_prices = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_project_idle = yes
			has_completed_focus = SHL_engineer_society
			check_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 0 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_engineers_price compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_political_power < 25 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_engineers_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_engineers_price }
		add_political_power = -25
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_deposit value = SHL_engineers_price }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_pp value = 25 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_operation value = 1 }
		SHL_redraw_furnace_transaction = yes
	}
}

SHL_begin_foundation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_project_idle = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_master_dispute }
			has_completed_focus = SHL_right_to_land
			check_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 200 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 200 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 200 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_deposit value = 200 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_pp value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_operation value = 2 }
		SHL_redraw_furnace_transaction = yes
	}
}

SHL_begin_construction = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_project_idle = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_transport_dispute }
			has_completed_focus = SHL_build_tenth_complex
			check_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 2 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 300 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			294 = {
				free_building_slots = {
					building = industrial_complex
					size = 1
					include_locked = no
				}
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 300 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 300 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_deposit value = 300 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_pp value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_operation value = 3 }
		SHL_redraw_furnace_transaction = yes
	}
}

SHL_begin_first_pour = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_project_idle = yes
			has_completed_focus = SHL_first_pour_focus
			check_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 3 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_stock value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 100 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_stock value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_stock_receipt value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_deposit value = 100 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_pp value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_operation value = 4 }
		SHL_redraw_furnace_transaction = yes
	}
}

SHL_cancel_project = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_project_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_project_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_project_deposit }
		add_political_power = SHL_project_pp
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_stock value = SHL_project_stock_receipt }
		SHL_clear_project_receipt = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_begin_tenth_service = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_project_idle = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 4 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_wear value = 20 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 80 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 80 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 80 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_deposit value = 80 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_operation value = 5 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_pp value = 0 }
		SHL_redraw_furnace_transaction = yes
	}
}

SHL_begin_tenth_restart = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_project_idle = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 4 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_running value = 0 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_stock value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 120 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 120 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 120 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_deposit value = 120 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_operation value = 6 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_project_pp value = 0 }
		SHL_redraw_furnace_transaction = yes
	}
}

SHL_clear_project_receipt = {
	clear_variable = SHL_project_deposit
	clear_variable = SHL_project_pp
	clear_variable = SHL_project_operation
	clear_variable = SHL_project_stock_receipt
}

SHL_finish_project = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_project_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = {
				SHL_country_operable = yes
				294 = {
					is_owned_by = SHL
					is_controlled_by = SHL
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_project_operation value = 1 compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 1 }
				SHL_clear_project_receipt = yes
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.7 days = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_project_operation value = 2 compare = equals } }
				if = {
					limit = { 294 = { infrastructure < 5 } }
					294 = { add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
				}
				else = {
					add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_project_deposit }
					add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_project_deposit }
				}
				set_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 2 }
				SHL_clear_project_receipt = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_project_operation value = 3 compare = equals } }
				if = {
					limit = {
						294 = {
							free_building_slots = {
								building = industrial_complex
								size = 1
								include_locked = no
							}
						}
					}
					294 = { add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 3 }
					SHL_clear_project_receipt = yes
				}
				else = { SHL_cancel_project = yes }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_project_operation value = 4 compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 4 }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_running value = 1 }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_wear value = 10 }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_stock value = 2 }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_shortages value = 0 }
				SHL_clear_project_receipt = yes
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.5 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_project_operation value = 5 compare = equals } }
				subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_wear value = 30 }
				clamp_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_wear min = 0 max = 100 }
				SHL_clear_project_receipt = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_project_operation value = 6 compare = equals } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_stock value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_running value = 1 }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_shortages value = 0 }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_wear value = 20 }
					SHL_clear_project_receipt = yes
				}
				else = { SHL_cancel_project = yes }
			}
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_project = yes }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_find_alert_after_cursor = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_alert_cursor value = 1 compare = less_than }
			OR = {
				SHL_furnace_1_worn = yes
				SHL_furnace_1_interrupted = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_alert_target value = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_alert_cursor value = 2 compare = less_than }
			OR = {
				SHL_furnace_2_worn = yes
				SHL_furnace_2_interrupted = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_alert_target value = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_alert_cursor value = 3 compare = less_than }
			OR = {
				SHL_furnace_3_worn = yes
				SHL_furnace_3_interrupted = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_alert_target value = 3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_alert_cursor value = 4 compare = less_than }
			OR = {
				SHL_furnace_4_worn = yes
				SHL_furnace_4_interrupted = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_alert_target value = 4 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_alert_cursor value = 5 compare = less_than }
			OR = {
				SHL_furnace_5_worn = yes
				SHL_furnace_5_interrupted = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_alert_target value = 5 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_alert_cursor value = 6 compare = less_than }
			OR = {
				SHL_furnace_6_worn = yes
				SHL_furnace_6_interrupted = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_alert_target value = 6 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_alert_cursor value = 7 compare = less_than }
			OR = {
				SHL_furnace_7_worn = yes
				SHL_furnace_7_interrupted = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_alert_target value = 7 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_alert_cursor value = 8 compare = less_than }
			OR = {
				SHL_furnace_8_worn = yes
				SHL_furnace_8_interrupted = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_alert_target value = 8 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_alert_cursor value = 9 compare = less_than }
			OR = {
				SHL_furnace_9_worn = yes
				SHL_furnace_9_interrupted = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_alert_target value = 9 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_alert_cursor value = 10 compare = less_than }
			OR = {
				SHL_furnace_10_worn = yes
				SHL_furnace_10_interrupted = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_alert_target value = 10 }
	}
}

SHL_select_next_furnace_alert = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_alert_cursor value = SHL_selected_furnace }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_alert_target value = 0 }
	SHL_find_alert_after_cursor = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_alert_target value = 0 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_alert_cursor value = 0 }
		SHL_find_alert_after_cursor = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_alert_target value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = SHL_alert_target compare = not_equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_supply_settings_open value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = SHL_alert_target }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_supply_settings_open value = 0 }
		SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

SHL_select_next_unfunded_order = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_regular_supply value = 1 compare = equals }
		}
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_next_unfunded_furnace value = 0 compare = greater_than }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = SHL_next_unfunded_furnace value = SHL_selected_furnace compare = not_equals }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_supply_settings_open value = 1 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = SHL_next_unfunded_furnace }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_supply_settings_open value = 0 }
			SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
		}
	}
}

SHL_toggle_priority_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_toggle_auto_reserve = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_priority_1 value = 1 compare = equals } }
			clear_variable = SHL_priority_1
		}
		else = { set_variable = { var = SHL_priority_1 value = 1 } }
		SHL_refresh_furnace_card = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_toggle_auto_reserve = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_priority_3 value = 1 compare = equals } }
			clear_variable = SHL_priority_3
		}
		else = { set_variable = { var = SHL_priority_3 value = 1 } }
		SHL_refresh_furnace_card = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

SHL_raise_reserve_floor = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_raise_reserve_floor = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_floor value = 1 }
		SHL_refresh_furnace_card = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

SHL_lower_reserve_floor = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_lower_reserve_floor = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_floor value = 1 }
		SHL_refresh_furnace_card = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

SHL_toggle_supply_master = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_toggle_auto_reserve = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_regular_supply value = 1 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_regular_supply value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve value = 0 }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_regular_supply value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve value = 1 }
		}
		SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

SHL_toggle_supply_settings = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SHL
			has_country_flag = SHL_furnaces_initialized
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_supply_settings_open value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_supply_settings_open value = 0 }
		}
		else = { set_variable = { var = SHL_supply_settings_open value = 1 } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

SHL_toggle_auto_reserve = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_toggle_auto_reserve = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve value = 0 }
		}
		else = { set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve value = 1 } }
		SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

SHL_enable_planned_supply = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			has_completed_focus = SHL_law_of_continuous_fire
			check_variable = { var = SHL_regular_supply value = 0 compare = equals }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_regular_supply value = 1 }
		SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

			SHL_country_operable = yes

		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }

# A paid project changes its own status as well as the forecast inputs.
SHL_redraw_furnace_transaction = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_live_forecast_enabled = yes }
		SHL_refresh_furnace_card = yes
	}
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
}

SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_live_forecast_enabled = yes
			SHL_forecast_inputs_changed = yes
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnace_card = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = {
	SHL_refresh_delivery_terms = yes
	SHL_refresh_furnace_card = yes
}

# Uses already quoted tariffs, including a railway commissioned in its own reward.
SHL_refresh_furnace_card = {
	SHL_refresh_delivery_forecast = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_recipient value = SHL_recipient_1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_block value = SHL_delivery_block_1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_cash value = SHL_delivery_cash_1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_rifles value = SHL_delivery_rifles_1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_quarters value = SHL_delivery_quarters_1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_crests value = SHL_delivery_crests_1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_recipient value = SHL_recipient_2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_block value = SHL_delivery_block_2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_cash value = SHL_delivery_cash_2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_rifles value = SHL_delivery_rifles_2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_quarters value = SHL_delivery_quarters_2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_crests value = SHL_delivery_crests_2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 3 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_recipient value = SHL_recipient_3 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_block value = SHL_delivery_block_3 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_cash value = SHL_delivery_cash_3 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_rifles value = SHL_delivery_rifles_3 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_quarters value = SHL_delivery_quarters_3 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_crests value = SHL_delivery_crests_3 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 4 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_recipient value = SHL_recipient_4 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_block value = SHL_delivery_block_4 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_cash value = SHL_delivery_cash_4 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_rifles value = SHL_delivery_rifles_4 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_quarters value = SHL_delivery_quarters_4 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_crests value = SHL_delivery_crests_4 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 5 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_recipient value = SHL_recipient_5 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_block value = SHL_delivery_block_5 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_cash value = SHL_delivery_cash_5 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_rifles value = SHL_delivery_rifles_5 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_quarters value = SHL_delivery_quarters_5 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_crests value = SHL_delivery_crests_5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 6 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_recipient value = SHL_recipient_6 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_block value = SHL_delivery_block_6 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_cash value = SHL_delivery_cash_6 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_rifles value = SHL_delivery_rifles_6 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_quarters value = SHL_delivery_quarters_6 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_crests value = SHL_delivery_crests_6 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 7 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_recipient value = SHL_recipient_7 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_block value = SHL_delivery_block_7 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_cash value = SHL_delivery_cash_7 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_rifles value = SHL_delivery_rifles_7 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_quarters value = SHL_delivery_quarters_7 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_crests value = SHL_delivery_crests_7 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 8 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_recipient value = SHL_recipient_8 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_block value = SHL_delivery_block_8 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_cash value = SHL_delivery_cash_8 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_rifles value = SHL_delivery_rifles_8 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_quarters value = SHL_delivery_quarters_8 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_crests value = SHL_delivery_crests_8 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 9 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_recipient value = SHL_recipient_9 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_block value = SHL_delivery_block_9 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_cash value = SHL_delivery_cash_9 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_rifles value = SHL_delivery_rifles_9 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_quarters value = SHL_delivery_quarters_9 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_crests value = SHL_delivery_crests_9 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 10 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_recipient value = SHL_recipient_10 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_block value = SHL_delivery_block_10 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_cash value = SHL_delivery_cash_10 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_rifles value = SHL_delivery_rifles_10 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_quarters value = SHL_delivery_quarters_10 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_delivery_crests value = SHL_delivery_crests_10 }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_wear value = SHL_wear_1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_stock value = SHL_stock_1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_owner value = SHL_owner_1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_status value = SHL_status_1 }
		287 = {
			PREV = {
				set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_training value = PREV.SHL_furnace_training }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_wear value = SHL_wear_2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_stock value = SHL_stock_2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_owner value = SHL_owner_2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_status value = SHL_status_2 }
		288 = {
			PREV = {
				set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_training value = PREV.SHL_furnace_training }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 3 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_wear value = SHL_wear_3 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_stock value = SHL_stock_3 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_owner value = SHL_owner_3 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_status value = SHL_status_3 }
		289 = {
			PREV = {
				set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_training value = PREV.SHL_furnace_training }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 4 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_wear value = SHL_wear_4 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_stock value = SHL_stock_4 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_owner value = SHL_owner_4 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_status value = SHL_status_4 }
		290 = {
			PREV = {
				set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_training value = PREV.SHL_furnace_training }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 5 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_wear value = SHL_wear_5 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_stock value = SHL_stock_5 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_owner value = SHL_owner_5 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_status value = SHL_status_5 }
		291 = {
			PREV = {
				set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_training value = PREV.SHL_furnace_training }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 6 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_wear value = SHL_wear_6 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_stock value = SHL_stock_6 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_owner value = SHL_owner_6 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_status value = SHL_status_6 }
		292 = {
			PREV = {
				set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_training value = PREV.SHL_furnace_training }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 7 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_wear value = SHL_wear_7 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_stock value = SHL_stock_7 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_owner value = SHL_owner_7 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_status value = SHL_status_7 }
		293 = {
			PREV = {
				set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_training value = PREV.SHL_furnace_training }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 8 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_wear value = SHL_wear_8 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_stock value = SHL_stock_8 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_owner value = SHL_owner_8 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_status value = SHL_status_8 }
		294 = {
			PREV = {
				set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_training value = PREV.SHL_furnace_training }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 9 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_wear value = SHL_wear_9 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_stock value = SHL_stock_9 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_owner value = SHL_owner_9 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_status value = SHL_status_9 }
		295 = {
			PREV = {
				set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_training value = PREV.SHL_furnace_training }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 10 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_wear value = SHL_tenth_wear }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_stock value = SHL_tenth_stock }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_training value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_status value = SHL_preview_status }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_owner value = 2 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_owner value = 0 }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_period_cost value = 1 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_period_wear value = 6 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_period_cost value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_period_wear value = 10 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_period_wear value = 3 }
	}
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_selected_period_wear value = SHL_selected_training }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_state value = SHL_selected_furnace }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_selected_state value = 286 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 3 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			var:SHL_selected_state = { SHL_has_cooling_loop = yes }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_selected_period_wear value = 2 }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_selected_period_wear min = 0 max = 100 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 3 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_selected_period_wear value = 4 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_period_income value = SHL_selected_delivery_cash }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_order value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = SHL_obligation_kind
			check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_state value = SHL_obligation_site compare = equals }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_order value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 10 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_state value = 294 }
	}
	SHL_refresh_selected_loyalty = yes
}

# Settle in country scope after filling the destination, so all panel values use the same stocks.
SHL_settle_reserve_packet = {
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_stock value = 1 }
	SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
}

# State scope for the nine district bunkers. The map decision supplies FROM even if the card changes.
SHL_send_reserve_to_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_send_reserve_to_site = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 }
		SHL = { SHL_settle_reserve_packet = yes }
	}
}

# The tenth furnace shares district 294 with VIII and keeps a separate country stock.
SHL_send_reserve_to_tenth = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_send_reserve_to_tenth = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_stock value = 1 }
		SHL_settle_reserve_packet = yes
	}
}

SHL_send_reserve_to_selected = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_send_reserve_to_selected = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 10 compare = equals } }
			SHL_send_reserve_to_tenth = yes
		}
		else = { var:SHL_selected_state = { SHL_send_reserve_to_site = yes } }
	}
}

SHL_distribute_reserve = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_regular_supply value = 1 compare = equals }
		}
		# Recheck the working fires, control and prices together before debiting any packets.
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_can_distribute_reserve = yes }
			287 = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL.SHL_bulk_reserve_1 } }
			288 = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL.SHL_bulk_reserve_2 } }
			289 = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL.SHL_bulk_reserve_3 } }
			290 = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL.SHL_bulk_reserve_4 } }
			291 = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL.SHL_bulk_reserve_5 } }
			292 = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL.SHL_bulk_reserve_6 } }
			293 = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL.SHL_bulk_reserve_7 } }
			294 = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL.SHL_bulk_reserve_8 } }
			295 = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL.SHL_bulk_reserve_9 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_bulk_reserve_10 value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_stock value = SHL_bulk_reserve_10 }
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_stock value = SHL_bulk_reserve_total }
			SHL_refresh_furnace_card = yes
		}
	}
}

SHL_dispatch_ore = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_dispatch_ore = yes }
		291 = { subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 10 compare = equals } }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_stock value = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			var:SHL_selected_state = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 } }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_convoy_dispatch value = 1 }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

		limit = { SHL_can_dispatch_masons = yes }

			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 10 compare = equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_wear value = 15 }
		}
		else = {
			var:SHL_selected_state = { subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 15 } }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_convoy_dispatch value = 2 }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
