	SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
}

SHL_dispatch_sale = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_dispatch_sale = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 10 compare = equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_stock value = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			var:SHL_selected_state = { subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 } }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 40 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 40 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -3 }
		SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_convoy_dispatch value = 3 }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_commit_house_course = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 0 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 1 }
		SHL_form_course_cabinet = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_treasury_capacity_bonus value = 0.10 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 10 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 15 }
		SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.15 }
		SHL_balance_shift = yes
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = SHL_rename_house_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_cosmetic_tag = SHL_conclave_of_ten
			SHL_update_ruler_portrait = yes
			SHL_announce_course_news = yes
			# Course sections and war-tree course branches read SHL_political_course in allow_branch.
			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		}
	}
	SHL_party_representatives_house = yes
}

SHL_purchase_reserve = {
	SHL_refresh_reserve_price = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			SHL_reserve_affordable = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_stock value = 10 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_supplier_premium }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_reserve_price_premium }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_reserve_price_premium }
			clr_country_flag = SHL_supplier_premium
		}
		else = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_reserve_price }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_reserve_price }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_stock value = 2 }
		SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes

			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		}
	}
	SHL_party_representatives_state = yes
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Quarters, crests and borders. Country scope SHL unless a comment says otherwise.
# The 28-day cycle owns every recurring check; missions and one-shot timeout
# events own the dated consequences.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

SHL_initialize_tensions = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 25 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_strike_ready_cycle value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_conspiracy_ready_cycle value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_accident_ready_cycle value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_suppressions value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_water_quota value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_veyr_crisis value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_war_glp value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_income_deduction value = 0 }
	# The starting loyalties average 80, a pressure of 20.
	287 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = 72 } }
	288 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = 90 } }
	289 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = 88 } }
	290 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = 95 } }
	291 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = 82 } }
	292 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = 75 } }
	293 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = 68 } }
	294 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = 85 } }
	295 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = 65 } }
	SHL_refresh_house_pressure = yes
}

SHL_clamp_tensions = {
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_city_support min = 0 max = 100 }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
}

# Input: temp SHL_balance_shift (negative toward the crests), one of the
# literals below. Native add_power_balance_value takes no variable, so the
# chain maps every caller's step to a fixed value.
SHL_balance_shift = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_power_balance = { id = SHL_conclave_balance } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.30 compare = equals } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value = -0.30 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.25 compare = equals } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value = -0.25 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.15 compare = equals } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value = -0.15 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.10 compare = equals } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value = -0.10 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.05 compare = equals } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value = -0.05 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.03 compare = equals } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value = -0.03 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.02 compare = equals } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value = 0.02 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.03 compare = equals } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value = 0.03 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.05 compare = equals } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value = 0.05 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.10 compare = equals } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value = 0.10 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.15 compare = equals } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value = 0.15 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.25 compare = equals } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value = 0.25 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.30 compare = equals } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value = 0.30 }
		}
	}
}

# Every producing furnace pays the same active deductions until their source ends.
SHL_refresh_income_deduction = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_income_deduction value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_veyr_crisis value = 1 compare = equals }
			NOT = { 292 = { check_variable = { var = SHL_duty_paid_cycles value = 0 compare = greater_than } } }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_income_deduction value = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_water_quota value = 1 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_income_deduction value = 3 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_strike_active }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_income_deduction value = 10 }
	}
}

# ----- House loyalty -----
# SHL_house_loyalty (0-100) on the nine furnace states is the loyalty of the
# house of that district. Only SHL_initialize_tensions and
# SHL_change_house_loyalty write it. SHL_house_pressure is 100 minus the mean
# loyalty of the houses on owned land and has one writer,
# SHL_refresh_house_pressure: a change applied to every owned house moves the
# pressure by exactly the opposite amount, up to clamping at 0 and 100. A lost
# district keeps its stored loyalty outside the mean until it is regained.

# STATE. Input: SHL.SHL_loyalty_delta. Only initialised houses change.
SHL_change_house_loyalty = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_house_loyalty }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = SHL.SHL_loyalty_delta }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty min = 0 max = 100 }
	}
}

SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_owned_by = SHL }
		SHL_change_house_loyalty = yes
	}
}

# STATE. Consumes SHL.SHL_loyalty_delta for this house; the caller's furnace
# refresh updates the pressure once after its loop.
SHL_apply_house_loyalty = {
	SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes
	SHL = { clear_variable = SHL_loyalty_delta }
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Input: SHL_loyalty_delta for every house on owned land.
SHL_change_all_loyalty = {
	287 = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	288 = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	289 = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	290 = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	291 = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	292 = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	293 = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	294 = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	295 = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	clear_variable = SHL_loyalty_delta
	SHL_refresh_house_pressure = yes
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Inputs: SHL_loyalty_site (287-295) and SHL_loyalty_delta.
SHL_change_site_loyalty = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_site value = 287 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_site value = 295 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		var:SHL_loyalty_site = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_loyalty_site
	clear_variable = SHL_loyalty_delta
	SHL_refresh_house_pressure = yes
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Inputs: SHL_loyalty_site (287-295), the house the change
# spares, and SHL_loyalty_delta for every other house on owned land. The
# spared house is never touched, so clamping cannot change its loyalty.
SHL_change_other_houses_loyalty = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_site value = 287 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_site value = 295 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		var:SHL_loyalty_site = { set_state_flag = SHL_loyalty_spared }
	}
	287 = { SHL_change_unspared_house_loyalty = yes }
	288 = { SHL_change_unspared_house_loyalty = yes }
	289 = { SHL_change_unspared_house_loyalty = yes }
	290 = { SHL_change_unspared_house_loyalty = yes }
	291 = { SHL_change_unspared_house_loyalty = yes }
	292 = { SHL_change_unspared_house_loyalty = yes }
	293 = { SHL_change_unspared_house_loyalty = yes }
	294 = { SHL_change_unspared_house_loyalty = yes }
	295 = { SHL_change_unspared_house_loyalty = yes }
	clear_variable = SHL_loyalty_site
	clear_variable = SHL_loyalty_delta
	SHL_refresh_house_pressure = yes
}

# STATE. The spared house only loses its transient marker.
SHL_change_unspared_house_loyalty = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = SHL_loyalty_spared }
		clr_state_flag = SHL_loyalty_spared
	}
	else = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Input: SHL_loyalty_delta for the three houses of the qanat border.
SHL_change_qanat_houses_loyalty = {
	287 = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	288 = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	289 = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	clear_variable = SHL_loyalty_delta
	SHL_refresh_house_pressure = yes
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Input: SHL_loyalty_delta for Kerdan's sea road and Veyran's guards.
SHL_change_port_houses_loyalty = {

	295 = { SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes }
	clear_variable = SHL_loyalty_delta
	SHL_refresh_house_pressure = yes
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Input: SHL_loyalty_delta for the house of the flagged accident
# site; ADISCORD_SHL.40 calls it before SHL_resolve_accident clears the flag.
SHL_change_accident_loyalty = {
	287 = { SHL_change_accident_site_loyalty = yes }
	288 = { SHL_change_accident_site_loyalty = yes }
	289 = { SHL_change_accident_site_loyalty = yes }
	290 = { SHL_change_accident_site_loyalty = yes }
	291 = { SHL_change_accident_site_loyalty = yes }
	292 = { SHL_change_accident_site_loyalty = yes }
	293 = { SHL_change_accident_site_loyalty = yes }
	294 = { SHL_change_accident_site_loyalty = yes }
	295 = { SHL_change_accident_site_loyalty = yes }
	clear_variable = SHL_loyalty_delta
	SHL_refresh_house_pressure = yes
}

SHL_change_accident_site_loyalty = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = SHL_accident_site }
		SHL_change_owned_house_loyalty = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. The only writer of SHL_house_pressure. The commune has no crests
# and an empty hall has no pressure.
SHL_refresh_house_pressure = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_sum value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_houses value = 0 }
	287 = { SHL_count_house_loyalty = yes }
	288 = { SHL_count_house_loyalty = yes }
	289 = { SHL_count_house_loyalty = yes }
	290 = { SHL_count_house_loyalty = yes }
	291 = { SHL_count_house_loyalty = yes }
	292 = { SHL_count_house_loyalty = yes }
	293 = { SHL_count_house_loyalty = yes }
	294 = { SHL_count_house_loyalty = yes }
	295 = { SHL_count_house_loyalty = yes }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_house_pressure value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_houses value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_house_pressure value = SHL_loyalty_sum }
		divide_variable = { var = SHL_house_pressure value = SHL_loyalty_houses }
		multiply_variable = { var = SHL_house_pressure value = -1 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_house_pressure value = 100 }
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_loyalty_sum
	SHL_refresh_selected_loyalty = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
}

# STATE scope; PREV is SHL.
SHL_count_house_loyalty = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = SHL
			has_variable = SHL_house_loyalty
		}
		SHL = {
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_sum value = PREV.SHL_house_loyalty }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_houses value = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. GUI mirror of the selected card; the tenth furnace has no house.
SHL_refresh_selected_loyalty = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_loyalty value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 9 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_state value = 287 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_state value = 295 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		var:SHL_selected_state = {
			PREV = { set_variable = { var = SHL_selected_loyalty value = PREV.SHL_house_loyalty } }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Inputs SHL_rate_recipient and SHL_rate_owner of a producing
# site; output SHL_rate_loyalty for its house. Production and the forecast
# read the same rule, so the preview equals the applied change. A crest
# delivery compensates the house even after its furnace changed owner.
SHL_site_loyalty_rate = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_rate_loyalty value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_rate_recipient value = 3 compare = equals }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_rate_loyalty value = SHL_delivery_loyalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_rate_owner value = 0 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_rate_recipient value = 0 compare = equals } }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_rate_loyalty value = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_rate_recipient value = 1 compare = equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_rate_loyalty value = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_rate_recipient value = 2 compare = equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_rate_loyalty value = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 1 compare = equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_rate_loyalty value = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 2 compare = equals } }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_rate_loyalty value = 1 }
		}
	}
}

SHL_clear_loyalty_rate = {
	clear_variable = SHL_rate_recipient
	clear_variable = SHL_rate_owner
	clear_variable = SHL_rate_loyalty
}

# STATE scope; PREV is SHL. The site's own delivery, after its stock was used.
SHL_apply_site_loyalty_rate = {
	SHL = {
		set_variable = { var = SHL_rate_recipient value = PREV.SHL_furnace_recipient }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_rate_owner value = PREV.SHL_furnace_owner }
		SHL_site_loyalty_rate = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = SHL_rate_loyalty }
		SHL_clear_loyalty_rate = yes
	}
	SHL_apply_house_loyalty = yes
}

# STATE scope; PREV is SHL. Fixed production penalties of a house-held furnace.
SHL_house_furnace_penalty = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals } }
		SHL_apply_house_loyalty = yes
	}
	SHL = { clear_variable = SHL_loyalty_delta }
}

# ----- The walkout of a house -----
# SHL_walkout_site (0 or 287-295) is the only pending marker. The cycle tick
# sets it and fires ADISCORD_SHL.141, which starts the 56-day mission
# SHL_house_walkout or buys the house back. Every terminal path resets it.

# COUNTRY SHL. The lowest loyalty below 40 among lit, seated houses outside opposition.
SHL_pick_restless_house = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_loyalty value = 40 }
	287 = { SHL_consider_restless_house = yes }
	288 = { SHL_consider_restless_house = yes }
	289 = { SHL_consider_restless_house = yes }
	290 = { SHL_consider_restless_house = yes }
	291 = { SHL_consider_restless_house = yes }
	292 = { SHL_consider_restless_house = yes }
	293 = { SHL_consider_restless_house = yes }
	294 = { SHL_consider_restless_house = yes }
	295 = { SHL_consider_restless_house = yes }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_walkout_site value = SHL_pick_site }
	clear_variable = SHL_pick_site
	clear_variable = SHL_pick_loyalty
}

# STATE scope; PREV is SHL.
SHL_consider_restless_house = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_house_restless = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = SHL.SHL_pick_loyalty compare = less_than }
		}
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_loyalty value = PREV.SHL_house_loyalty } }
		SHL_record_pick_site = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Output SHL_loyalty_site: the least loyal house below 80 on owned
# land other than Nur's own.
SHL_pick_mediation_house = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_loyalty value = 80 }
	287 = { SHL_consider_mediation_house = yes }
	288 = { SHL_consider_mediation_house = yes }
	289 = { SHL_consider_mediation_house = yes }
	291 = { SHL_consider_mediation_house = yes }
	292 = { SHL_consider_mediation_house = yes }
	293 = { SHL_consider_mediation_house = yes }
	294 = { SHL_consider_mediation_house = yes }
	295 = { SHL_consider_mediation_house = yes }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_site value = SHL_pick_site }
	clear_variable = SHL_pick_site
	clear_variable = SHL_pick_loyalty
}

# STATE scope; PREV is SHL.
SHL_consider_mediation_house = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = SHL
			has_variable = SHL_house_loyalty
			check_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = SHL.SHL_pick_loyalty compare = less_than }
		}
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_loyalty value = PREV.SHL_house_loyalty } }
		SHL_record_pick_site = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL, from SHL_update_tensions. One walkout at a time.
SHL_house_walkout_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_house_walkout }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_walkout_site value = 0 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		SHL_pick_restless_house = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_walkout_site value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.141 }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. A house that walked out or lost a demand returns early once its
# loyalty reaches 55; a quarter's opposition is left to its own 180 days.
SHL_house_return_tick = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_houses_returned value = 0 }
	287 = { SHL_house_return_site = yes }
	288 = { SHL_house_return_site = yes }
	289 = { SHL_house_return_site = yes }
	290 = { SHL_house_return_site = yes }
	291 = { SHL_house_return_site = yes }
	292 = { SHL_house_return_site = yes }
	293 = { SHL_house_return_site = yes }
	294 = { SHL_house_return_site = yes }
	295 = { SHL_house_return_site = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_houses_returned value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_houses_returned
}

# STATE scope; PREV is SHL.
SHL_house_return_site = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_house_seated = yes
			has_state_flag = SHL_house_opposed
			check_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = 55 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		clr_state_flag = SHL_house_opposed
		SHL = {
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_houses_returned value = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.05 }
			SHL_balance_shift = yes
		}
	}
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.141.a, also the unattended answer.
SHL_start_house_walkout = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_walkout_site value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_house_walkout }
		}
		activate_mission = SHL_house_walkout
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_house_walkout } }
		SHL_close_house_walkout = yes
	}
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.141.b: 100 treasury and 25 PP, rechecked at the answer; a
# shortfall starts the mission instead of charging part of the price.
SHL_house_walkout_buyback = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_walkout_site value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_house_walkout }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_political_power < 25 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 100 }
		add_political_power = -25
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_site value = SHL_walkout_site }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 20 }
		SHL_change_site_loyalty = yes
		SHL_close_house_walkout = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
	else = { SHL_start_house_walkout = yes }
}

SHL_close_house_walkout = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_walkout_site value = 0 }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
}

# Mission success: a house that still holds its furnace gains 5 more.
SHL_house_walkout_settled = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_walkout_site value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 5 }
		var:SHL_walkout_site = {
			if = {
				limit = { SHL_house_seated = yes }
				SHL_change_house_loyalty = yes
			}
		}
		clear_variable = SHL_loyalty_delta
	}
	SHL_close_house_walkout = yes
	SHL_refresh_house_pressure = yes
}

# Mission timeout: the house leaves the hall for 180 days.
SHL_house_walkout_timeout = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_walkout_site value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		var:SHL_walkout_site = {
			if = {
				limit = { SHL_house_seated = yes }
				SHL_mark_site_opposed = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.05 }
		SHL_balance_shift = yes
	}
	SHL_close_house_walkout = yes
	SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
}

# Commune and annexation: no house is left to return.
SHL_cancel_house_walkout = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_house_walkout }
		remove_mission = SHL_house_walkout
	}
	SHL_close_house_walkout = yes
}

# ----- House favours -----
# Each favour is an instant decision on its house's own district. The furnace
# card button activates it through activate_targeted_decision, which skips the
# decision's conditions and cost, so every grant rechecks the house, the price
# and the situation and debits each currency once.

# STATE, the granting house. Input SHL.SHL_loyalty_delta, the negative price.
SHL_spend_house_favour = {
	SHL_apply_house_loyalty = yes
	clr_state_flag = SHL_favour_given
	set_state_flag = SHL_favour_given
}

# STATE. Applies SHL_site_wear_delta and SHL_site_stock_delta to a lit furnace.
SHL_change_lit_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_obligation_site_ready = yes }
		SHL_apply_site_change = yes
	}
}

# Arbin: the qanat masters reline every lit furnace of the qanat border.
SHL_grant_favour_arbin = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_house_favours_open = yes
			287 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
			SHL_favour_arbin_price = yes
			SHL_favour_arbin_open = yes
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 40 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 40 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -15 }
		287 = { SHL_spend_house_favour = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_site_wear_delta value = -10 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_site_wear_delta value = -15 }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_site_stock_delta value = 0 }
		287 = { SHL_change_lit_site = yes }
		288 = { SHL_change_lit_site = yes }
		289 = { SHL_change_lit_site = yes }
		clear_variable = SHL_site_wear_delta
		clear_variable = SHL_site_stock_delta
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# Safir: the purse of the Conclave lends against the house's name.
SHL_grant_favour_safir = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_house_favours_open = yes
			288 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
			SHL_favour_safir_price = yes
			SHL_favour_safir_open = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -25
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -20 }
		288 = { SHL_spend_house_favour = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 120 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 120 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# Loran: the Golden Gate holds a fair for every lit furnace selling abroad.
SHL_grant_favour_loran = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_house_favours_open = yes
			289 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
			SHL_favour_loran_price = yes
			SHL_favour_loran_open = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -20
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -15 }
		289 = { SHL_spend_house_favour = yes }
		287 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
		288 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
		289 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
		290 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
		291 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
		292 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
		293 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
		294 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
		295 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# Nur: the mediators bring back the walking-out house or, when no house has
# left the hall, the least loyal other house of owned land.
SHL_grant_favour_nur = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_house_favours_open = yes
			290 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
			SHL_favour_nur_price = yes
			SHL_favour_nur_open = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -30
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -10 }
		290 = { SHL_spend_house_favour = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_walkout_site value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_site value = SHL_walkout_site }
		}
		else = { SHL_pick_mediation_house = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 20 }
		SHL_change_site_loyalty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# Bayrat: three ore packets, each to the lit furnace with the smallest stock.
SHL_grant_favour_bayrat = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_house_favours_open = yes
			291 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
			SHL_favour_bayrat_price = yes
			SHL_favour_bayrat_open = yes
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 30 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 30 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -15 }
		291 = { SHL_spend_house_favour = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_owner value = 9 }
		SHL_stock_lowest_site = yes
		SHL_stock_lowest_site = yes
		SHL_stock_lowest_site = yes
		SHL_clear_pick = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# Kerdan: the sea-road house pays the Veyr duty for the next two productions;
# SHL_produce_period counts them down after the tenth furnace has produced.

		limit = {
			SHL_house_favours_open = yes
			292 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }

			SHL_favour_kerdan_open = yes
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 40 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 40 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -15 }
		292 = { SHL_spend_house_favour = yes }
		292 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_duty_paid_cycles value = 2 } }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

		set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_training_below value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_skip_opposed value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_by_stock value = 0 }
		SHL_pick_worst_site = yes
		SHL_train_pick_site = yes

		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 5 }

# Veyran: the border guards muster one levy at their own district; a levy
# never lands on occupied ground.
SHL_grant_favour_veyran = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_house_favours_open = yes
			295 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
			SHL_favour_veyran_price = yes
			SHL_favour_veyran_open = yes
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 60 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 60 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -20 }
		295 = { SHL_spend_house_favour = yes }
		SHL_ensure_levy_template = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				295 = {
					is_owned_by = SHL
					is_controlled_by = SHL
				}
			}
			295 = { SHL_create_levy = yes }
		}
		else = { SHL_raise_reward_levy = yes }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Furnace card button; each branch repeats its decision's checks.
SHL_activate_selected_favour = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_house_favours_open = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 1 compare = equals }
				287 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
				SHL_favour_arbin_price = yes
				SHL_favour_arbin_open = yes
			}
			activate_targeted_decision = { target = 287 decision = SHL_favour_arbin }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 2 compare = equals }
				288 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
				SHL_favour_safir_price = yes
				SHL_favour_safir_open = yes
			}
			activate_targeted_decision = { target = 288 decision = SHL_favour_safir }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 3 compare = equals }
				289 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
				SHL_favour_loran_price = yes
				SHL_favour_loran_open = yes
			}
			activate_targeted_decision = { target = 289 decision = SHL_favour_loran }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 4 compare = equals }
				290 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
				SHL_favour_nur_price = yes
				SHL_favour_nur_open = yes
			}
			activate_targeted_decision = { target = 290 decision = SHL_favour_nur }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 5 compare = equals }
				291 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
				SHL_favour_bayrat_price = yes
				SHL_favour_bayrat_open = yes
			}
			activate_targeted_decision = { target = 291 decision = SHL_favour_bayrat }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 6 compare = equals }
				292 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
				SHL_favour_kerdan_price = yes
				SHL_favour_kerdan_open = yes
			}
			activate_targeted_decision = { target = 292 decision = SHL_favour_kerdan }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 7 compare = equals }
				293 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
				SHL_favour_sharim_price = yes
				SHL_favour_sharim_open = yes
			}
			activate_targeted_decision = { target = 293 decision = SHL_favour_sharim }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 8 compare = equals }
				294 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
				SHL_favour_darban_price = yes
				SHL_favour_darban_open = yes
			}
			activate_targeted_decision = { target = 294 decision = SHL_favour_darban }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 9 compare = equals }
				295 = { SHL_favour_ready = yes }
				SHL_favour_veyran_price = yes
				SHL_favour_veyran_open = yes
			}
			activate_targeted_decision = { target = 295 decision = SHL_favour_veyran }
		}
	}
}

# Runs once after each settled production period. The country rules move
# every house on owned land by one summed SHL_loyalty_delta.
SHL_update_tensions = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = SHL_unregistered_quarters }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 4 compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = less_than }
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = SHL_k_no_crests }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 1 compare = equals }
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = SHL_common_smelting }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 4 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 2 compare = equals } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 6 }
	}
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = SHL_cycle_idle_sites }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 40 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_shift_arbitration }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 1 }
	}
	# Each chartered lit furnace steadies its quarter; SHL_refresh_furnaces counts them first.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_charters_running value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = SHL_charters_running }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = SHL_charters_running }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_house_mandate_current = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SHL_city_mandate_current = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 2 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SHL_directorate_mandate_current = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 4 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 3 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 7 compare = less_than } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 2 }
	}
	# Before ratification a conclave short of seven house votes lets the quarters
	# gain every cycle; a ratified course no longer counts hereditary votes.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 0 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_house_votes value = 7 compare = less_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.02 }
		SHL_balance_shift = yes
	}
	SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
	SHL_ai_steer_ratification = yes
	SHL_house_return_tick = yes
	SHL_house_walkout_tick = yes
	SHL_house_demand_tick = yes
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
}

SHL_check_crises = {
	SHL_close_lost_keizan_threat = yes
	SHL_expire_border_offers = yes
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_return_offer_ready = yes }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.163 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_strike_ready = yes }
		set_country_flag = SHL_strike_active
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.20 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_conspiracy_ready = yes }
		set_country_flag = SHL_conspiracy_active
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.30 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_number value = 8 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		SHL_start_water_crisis = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_number value = 12 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 4 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		SHL_start_veyr_crisis = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_keizan_demand_ready = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 1 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.70 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SHL_keizan_repeat_ready = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 1 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.75 }
	}
}

# ----- Cold shift (strike) -----

SHL_end_strike = {
	clr_country_flag = SHL_strike_active
	set_variable = { var = SHL_strike_ready_cycle value = SHL_cycle_number }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_strike_ready_cycle value = 4 }
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
	SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
}

SHL_strike_concede = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = SHL_strike_active
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 150 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 35 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -10 }
		SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 5 }
		SHL_end_strike = yes
	}
}

SHL_strike_suppress = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_strike_active }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 25 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 10 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_suppressions value = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.10 }
		SHL_balance_shift = yes
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_suppressions value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.21 days = 3 }
		}
		else = { SHL_end_strike = yes }
	}
}

SHL_declare_martial_law = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_strike_mission }
		remove_mission = SHL_strike_mission
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 30 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 10 }
	SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_suppressions value = 0 }
	SHL_end_strike = yes
}

# ----- Ultimatum of the crests -----

SHL_end_conspiracy = {
	clr_country_flag = SHL_conspiracy_active
	set_variable = { var = SHL_conspiracy_ready_cycle value = SHL_cycle_number }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_conspiracy_ready_cycle value = 4 }
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
}

SHL_conspiracy_guarantees = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_conspiracy_active }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 25 }
		SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 10 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 5 }
		SHL_end_conspiracy = yes
	}
}

# The directorate seizes the two furnaces whose owners signed the ultimatum.
SHL_conspiracy_arrests = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = SHL_conspiracy_active
			SHL_can_arrest_crests = yes
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 30 }
		SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 5 }
		# The two signatory houses lose their furnaces and pay for the ultimatum.
		290 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_owned_by = SHL
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
				}
				set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 2 }
				SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -40 } }
				SHL_apply_house_loyalty = yes
			}
		}
		295 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_owned_by = SHL
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
				}
				set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 2 }
				SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -40 } }
				SHL_apply_house_loyalty = yes
			}
		}
		SHL_end_conspiracy = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_resist_crests = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_conspiracy_mission }
		remove_mission = SHL_conspiracy_mission
	}
	# Every house moves by the same amount, so the pressure settles at 40.
	set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = SHL_house_pressure }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 40 }
	SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
	SHL_end_conspiracy = yes
}

# ----- Hidden courses -----

SHL_retire_nur = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character = SHL_Jalil_Nur }
		retire_character = SHL_Jalil_Nur
	}
}

SHL_commit_regency_course = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 4 compare = equals } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 4 }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_ballot_due
		SHL_form_course_cabinet = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_course_stability value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_treasury_capacity_bonus value = 0.10 }
		promote_character = {
			character = SHL_Darius_Safir
			ideology = industrial_aristocratism
		}
		SHL_retire_nur = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 30 }
		SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
		# Safir's regent leads the hereditary republic.
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_site value = 288 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 10 }
		SHL_change_site_loyalty = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 15 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.30 }
		SHL_balance_shift = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_conspiracy_mission }
			remove_mission = SHL_conspiracy_mission
		}
		SHL_end_conspiracy = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = SHL_rename_regency_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_cosmetic_tag = SHL_hereditary_republic
			SHL_update_ruler_portrait = yes
			SHL_announce_course_news = yes
		}
		# Course sections and war-tree course branches read SHL_political_course in allow_branch.
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		# One native tree: the entry focus opens the course section even when the
		# course was committed by the crisis event or the backing decision. The
		# course value is already set, so the entry reward cannot commit again.
		# The entry focus does not count as completed inside its own reward, so
		# its temporary marker keeps that reward from completing it a second time.
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = SHL_regency_of_crests }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_commit_from_entry value = 1 compare = equals } }
			}
			complete_national_focus = SHL_regency_of_crests
		}
	}
	SHL_party_representatives_regency = yes
}

SHL_commit_commune_course = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 4 compare = equals } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_ballot_due
		SHL_form_course_cabinet = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_course_stability value = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_treasury_capacity_bonus value = 0 }
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = anarchism
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = anarchism
			popularity = 0.35
		}
		promote_character = {
			character = SHL_Salima_Darzi
			ideology = anarchism_ideology
		}
		SHL_retire_nur = yes
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 30 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_suppressions value = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.30 }
		SHL_balance_shift = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_strike_mission }
			remove_mission = SHL_strike_mission
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_conspiracy_mission }
			remove_mission = SHL_conspiracy_mission
		}
		clr_country_flag = SHL_conspiracy_active
		SHL_cancel_house_walkout = yes
		SHL_reassign_commune_crests = yes
		SHL_end_strike = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = SHL_rename_commune_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_cosmetic_tag = SHL_commune_of_fire
			SHL_update_ruler_portrait = yes
			SHL_announce_course_news = yes
		}
		# Course sections and war-tree course branches read SHL_political_course in allow_branch.
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		# One native tree: the entry focus opens the course section even when the
		# course was committed by the crisis event or the backing decision. The
		# course value is already set, so the entry reward cannot commit again.
		# The entry focus does not count as completed inside its own reward, so
		# its temporary marker keeps that reward from completing it a second time.
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = SHL_commune_of_shift_councils }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_commit_from_entry value = 1 compare = equals } }
			}
			complete_national_focus = SHL_commune_of_shift_councils
		}
	}
	SHL_party_representatives_commune = yes
}

# STATE scope. A cold furnace whose ownership changes by course is ready for
# a paid restart without a second settlement decision.
SHL_assign_site_owner_house = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_owned_by = SHL }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 0 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_restart_settled value = 1 }
		}
	}
}

SHL_assign_site_owner_city = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_owned_by = SHL }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 0 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_restart_settled value = 1 }
		}
	}
}

SHL_restore_house_privileges = {
	287 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_house = yes }
	288 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_house = yes }
	289 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_house = yes }
	290 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_house = yes }
	291 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_house = yes }
	292 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_house = yes }
	293 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_house = yes }
	294 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_house = yes }
	295 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_house = yes }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 20 }
	SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 10 }
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
	SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
}

SHL_give_furnaces_to_workers = {
	287 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_city = yes }
	288 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_city = yes }
	289 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_city = yes }
	290 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_city = yes }
	291 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_city = yes }
	292 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_city = yes }
	293 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_city = yes }
	294 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_city = yes }
	295 = { SHL_assign_site_owner_city = yes }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 20 }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 15 }
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
	SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
}

# ----- Furnace accident -----

# STATE scope; PREV is SHL. Only the flagged site is closed.
SHL_close_accident_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = SHL_accident_site }
		clr_state_flag = SHL_accident_site
		if = {
			limit = {
				SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
			SHL_extinguish_site = yes
			if = {
				limit = { industrial_complex > 0 }
				damage_building = {
					type = industrial_complex
					damage = 1
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { SHL = { has_country_flag = SHL_accident_commission } }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

SHL_resolve_accident = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_accident_pending }
		287 = { SHL_close_accident_site = yes }
		288 = { SHL_close_accident_site = yes }
		289 = { SHL_close_accident_site = yes }
		290 = { SHL_close_accident_site = yes }
		291 = { SHL_close_accident_site = yes }
		292 = { SHL_close_accident_site = yes }
		293 = { SHL_close_accident_site = yes }
		294 = { SHL_close_accident_site = yes }
		295 = { SHL_close_accident_site = yes }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_accident_pending
		clr_country_flag = SHL_accident_commission
		set_variable = { var = SHL_accident_ready_cycle value = SHL_cycle_number }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_accident_ready_cycle value = 6 }
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# ----- Furnace levy -----

SHL_ensure_levy_template = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_template = "Furnace Levy" } }
		division_template = {
			name = "Furnace Levy"
			override_model = SHL_ADISCORD_militia_entity
			template_counter = 7
			division_names_group = SHL_INF_01
			is_locked = yes
			force_allow_recruiting = no
			regiments = {
				infantry = {
					x = 0
					y = 0
				}
				infantry = {
					x = 0
					y = 1
				}
				ADISCORD_militia = {
					x = 1
					y = 0
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# STATE scope, owner PREV. create_unit fills the division itself and draws
# nothing from the pools. Paid callers (guard order, regent guard, hired guard,
# campaign aid) require Khazar and debit their price before this helper runs;
# delayed orders refund the receipt when Khazar is lost before delivery. Focus
# rewards and course settlements grant the levy as the reward and debit nothing.
SHL_create_levy = {
	create_unit = {
		division = "division_template = \"Furnace Levy\" start_experience_factor = 0.1 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
		owner = PREV
		allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
	}
}

# 3000 manpower, 2700 rifles and 16 squad weapons per levy. The treasury
# price buys the furnace-made weapons; manpower is the country's own pool.
SHL_begin_guard_order = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_guard_deposit }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_political_power < 25 }
			NOT = { has_manpower < 3000 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 150 }
		add_political_power = -25
		add_manpower = -3000
		set_variable = { var = SHL_guard_deposit value = 150 }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_clear_guard_receipt = {
	clear_variable = SHL_guard_deposit
}

SHL_cancel_guard_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_guard_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_guard_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_guard_deposit }
		add_political_power = 25
		add_manpower = 3000
		SHL_clear_guard_receipt = yes
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_guard_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_guard_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = {
				SHL_country_operable = yes
				699 = {
					is_owned_by = SHL
					is_controlled_by = SHL
				}
			}
			SHL_ensure_levy_template = yes
			699 = { SHL_create_levy = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_first_guard_reported } }
				set_country_flag = SHL_first_guard_reported
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.122 }
			}
			SHL_clear_guard_receipt = yes
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_guard_order = yes }
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Levies supplied by a course: the houses, the city brigades or
# the private guards bring the recruits and their rifles, so a focus reward
# debits nothing. Campaign aid debits its manpower first and requires 699.
# Khazar (699) is the depot; while it is lost, another owned and controlled
# core state receives the levy, so the reward is not silently dropped.
SHL_raise_reward_levy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			699 = {
				is_owned_by = SHL
				is_controlled_by = SHL
			}
		}
		699 = { SHL_create_levy = yes }
	}
	else = {
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = SHL }
			SHL_create_levy = yes
		}
	}
}

SHL_raise_paid_levies_2 = {
	SHL_ensure_levy_template = yes
	SHL_raise_reward_levy = yes
	SHL_raise_reward_levy = yes
}

SHL_raise_paid_levies_3 = {
	SHL_raise_paid_levies_2 = yes
	SHL_raise_reward_levy = yes
}

# ----- Qanat crisis and water war (KYZ) -----

SHL_start_water_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 0 compare = equals }
			SHL_qanat_counterpart = yes
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 1 }
		KYZ = { ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.50 }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# KYZ scope after its own answer. Payment and delivery are settled together.
SHL_sign_water_treaty = {
	SHL = {
		clr_country_flag = SHL_water_offer_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_water_offer_declined }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 1 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 0 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				NOT = { has_war_with = KYZ }
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 100 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_water_quota value = 1 }
			SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.53 }
		}
		else = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.54 } }
	}
}

# KYZ scope. The brotherhood ends the dispute without a quota.
SHL_sign_water_brotherhood = {
	SHL = {
		clr_country_flag = SHL_water_offer_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 0 compare = equals }
				NOT = { has_war_with = KYZ }
			}
			set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_brotherhood
			set_variable = { var = SHL_water_quota value = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 0 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 1 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 2 compare = equals }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 5 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_completed_focus = SHL_southern_compact
						has_completed_focus = SHL_commune_of_fire
					}
				}
				SHL_request_southern_alliance = yes
			}
			SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.53 }
		}
		else = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.54 } }
	}
}

SHL_begin_wells = {
	SHL_refresh_programme_prices = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_wells_deposit }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_wells_price compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_wells_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_wells_price }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_wells_deposit value = SHL_wells_price }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_wells_price value = 0 compare = equals } }
			set_country_flag = SHL_free_well_used
		}
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_cancel_wells = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_wells_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_wells_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_wells_deposit }
		clear_variable = SHL_wells_deposit
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

# A dispute already closed by treaty, brotherhood or war refunds the wells.
SHL_finish_wells = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_wells_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = {
				SHL_country_operable = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 3 }
			clear_variable = SHL_wells_deposit
			SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_wells = yes }
	}
}

# STATE scope, owner KYZ.
SHL_raise_qanat_guard = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = KYZ
			is_controlled_by = KYZ
		}
		create_unit = {
			division = "division_template = \"Qanat Guard\" start_experience_factor = 0.15 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
			owner = KYZ
			allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
		}
	}
}

SHL_start_qanat_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_seize_qanats = yes }
		KYZ = {
			ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes
			add_war_support = 0.15
			add_manpower = 6000
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment
				amount = 4000
				producer = KYZ
			}
		}
		280 = { SHL_raise_qanat_guard = yes }
		284 = { SHL_raise_qanat_guard = yes }
		286 = { SHL_raise_qanat_guard = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 1 }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_redemption_refused_KYZ
		SHL_announce_first_war = yes
		set_country_flag = SHL_war_kyz_clock
		SHL_use_campaign_reserve = yes
		declare_war_on = {
			target = KYZ
			type = take_state_focus
			generator = { 280 284 285 286 }
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.56 days = 270 }
	}
}

SHL_close_qanat_war = {
	# Close the dispatcher guard before white peace can invoke on_peace.
	set_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_war_with = KYZ }
		white_peace = KYZ
	}
	clr_country_flag = SHL_war_kyz_clock
	# Permanent trace of a finished war; defeat and armistice leave no other marker.
	set_country_flag = SHL_qanat_war_closed
	SHL_front_clear_kyz = yes
	SHL_clean_campaign = yes
}

# STATE scope; transfers only land KYZ still owns.
SHL_take_state_from_kyz = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_owned_by = KYZ }
		SHL = { transfer_state = PREV }
		set_state_controller_to = SHL
	}
}

# The three victory reports (ADISCORD_SHL.57, .66 and .73) pull the War for
# the South deadline in every answer, so the date their tooltip shows is still
# the one before the pull.
SHL_qanat_war_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 1 compare = equals } }
		SHL_close_qanat_war = yes
		280 = { SHL_take_state_from_kyz = yes }
		284 = { SHL_take_state_from_kyz = yes }
		285 = { SHL_take_state_from_kyz = yes }
		286 = { SHL_take_state_from_kyz = yes }
		SHL_recover_ceded_from_kyz_all = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 4 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_water_quota value = 0 }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = SHL_captured_qanats days = 365 }
		set_country_flag = SHL_revanche_due_KYZ
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.76 days = 365 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 10 }
		SHL_change_qanat_houses_loyalty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.57 }
	}
}

SHL_qanat_war_defeat = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 1 compare = equals } }
		SHL_close_qanat_war = yes
		if = {
			limit = { 287 = { is_owned_by = SHL } }
			KYZ = { transfer_state = 287 }
			287 = {
				set_state_controller_to = KYZ
				set_state_flag = SHL_ceded_state
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_water_quota value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -10 }
		SHL_change_qanat_houses_loyalty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.58 }
	}
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.56 records the grade in its immediate block. Grade 1 keeps the
# Keizan districts Shahrabad holds, grade 3 cedes the recorded furnace, grade 2
# (or no record) is the status quo.
SHL_qanat_war_armistice = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_country_flag = SHL_armistice_settling
		SHL_close_qanat_war = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_armistice_grade value = 1 compare = equals } }
			280 = { SHL_armistice_take_from_kyz = yes }
			284 = { SHL_armistice_take_from_kyz = yes }
			285 = { SHL_armistice_take_from_kyz = yes }
			286 = { SHL_armistice_take_from_kyz = yes }
			set_country_flag = SHL_revanche_due_KYZ
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.76 days = 365 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_armistice_grade value = 3 compare = equals } }
			287 = { SHL_armistice_cede_to_kyz = yes }
			288 = { SHL_armistice_cede_to_kyz = yes }
			289 = { SHL_armistice_cede_to_kyz = yes }
		}
		clr_country_flag = SHL_armistice_settling
		SHL_clear_armistice_kyz = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# ----- Veyr duties and port war (GLP) -----

SHL_start_veyr_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_veyr_crisis value = 0 compare = equals }
			SHL_glp_counterpart = yes
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_veyr_crisis value = 1 }
		GLP = { ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.60 }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# GLP scope after its own answer.
SHL_sign_veyr_union = {
	SHL = {
		clr_country_flag = SHL_veyr_offer_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_veyr_offer_declined }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_war_glp value = 0 compare = equals }
				NOT = { has_war_with = GLP }
			}
			set_country_flag = SHL_veyr_partner
			GLP = { SHL_reconcile_kyz_grievance = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_veyr_crisis value = 0 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = SHL_veyr_crisis value = 1 compare = equals }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { var = SHL_veyr_crisis value = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_conclave_of_ten }
				SHL_request_golden_alliance = yes
			}
			SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.62 }
		}
		else = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.63 } }
	}
}

SHL_begin_harbour = {
	SHL_refresh_programme_prices = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_harbour_deposit }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_veyr_crisis value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_harbour_price compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_harbour_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_harbour_price }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_harbour_deposit value = SHL_harbour_price }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_cancel_harbour = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_harbour_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_harbour_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_harbour_deposit }
		clear_variable = SHL_harbour_deposit
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

# The harbour ends the duty; the dockyard is added only while Kerdan has room.
SHL_finish_harbour = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_harbour_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = {
				SHL_country_operable = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_veyr_crisis value = 1 compare = equals }
				292 = {
					is_owned_by = SHL
					is_controlled_by = SHL
				}
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_veyr_crisis value = 3 }
			clear_variable = SHL_harbour_deposit
			292 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = dockyard
							size = 1
							include_locked = no
						}
					}
					add_building_construction = {
						type = dockyard
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}
			SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_harbour = yes }
	}
}

# STATE scope, owner GLP.
SHL_glp_raise_prismatic_guard = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = GLP
			is_controlled_by = GLP
		}
		create_unit = {
			division = "division_template = \"Prismatic Guard\" start_experience_factor = 0.15 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
			owner = GLP
			allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
		}
	}
}

SHL_start_glp_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_strike_veyr = yes }
		GLP = {
			set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_border_grievance
			ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes
			add_war_support = 0.15
			add_manpower = 6000
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment
				amount = 4000
				producer = GLP
			}
		}
		297 = { SHL_glp_raise_prismatic_guard = yes }
		298 = { SHL_glp_raise_prismatic_guard = yes }
		708 = { SHL_glp_raise_prismatic_guard = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_war_glp value = 1 }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_redemption_refused_GLP
		SHL_announce_first_war = yes
		set_country_flag = SHL_war_glp_clock
		SHL_use_campaign_reserve = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_key_to_sea }
			set_country_flag = SHL_war_glp_sea_goal
			declare_war_on = {
				target = GLP
				type = take_state_focus
				generator = { 297 298 299 708 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = SHL_war_glp_sea_goal
			declare_war_on = {
				target = GLP
				type = take_state_focus
				generator = { 297 299 }
			}
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.65 days = 270 }
	}
}

SHL_close_glp_war = {
	# Close the dispatcher guard before white peace can invoke on_peace.
	set_variable = { var = SHL_war_glp value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_war_with = GLP }
		white_peace = GLP
	}
	clr_country_flag = SHL_war_glp_clock
	# Permanent trace of a finished war; defeat and armistice leave no other marker.
	set_country_flag = SHL_glp_war_closed
	SHL_front_clear_glp = yes
	SHL_clean_campaign = yes
}

# STATE scope; transfers only land GLP still owns.
SHL_take_state_from_glp = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_owned_by = GLP }
		SHL = { transfer_state = PREV }
		set_state_controller_to = SHL
	}
}

SHL_glp_war_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_glp value = 1 compare = equals } }
		SHL_close_glp_war = yes
		297 = { SHL_take_state_from_glp = yes }
		299 = { SHL_take_state_from_glp = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_war_glp_sea_goal }
			298 = { SHL_take_state_from_glp = yes }
			708 = { SHL_take_state_from_glp = yes }
		}
		clr_country_flag = SHL_war_glp_sea_goal
		SHL_recover_ceded_from_glp_all = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_veyr_crisis value = 4 }
		set_country_flag = SHL_revanche_due_GLP
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.76 days = 365 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 10 }
		SHL_change_port_houses_loyalty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.66 }
	}
}

SHL_glp_war_defeat = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_glp value = 1 compare = equals } }
		SHL_close_glp_war = yes
		clr_country_flag = SHL_war_glp_sea_goal
		if = {
			limit = { 296 = { is_owned_by = SHL } }
			GLP = { transfer_state = 296 }
			296 = {
				set_state_controller_to = GLP
				set_state_flag = SHL_ceded_state
			}
		}
		add_timed_idea = { idea = SHL_veyr_bondage days = 365 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -10 }
		SHL_change_port_houses_loyalty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.67 }
	}
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.65 records the grade in its immediate block; see
# SHL_qanat_war_armistice for the three grades.
SHL_glp_war_armistice = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_glp value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_country_flag = SHL_armistice_settling
		SHL_close_glp_war = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_glp_armistice_grade value = 1 compare = equals } }
			297 = { SHL_armistice_take_from_glp = yes }
			299 = { SHL_armistice_take_from_glp = yes }
			298 = { SHL_armistice_take_from_glp = yes }
			708 = { SHL_armistice_take_from_glp = yes }
			set_country_flag = SHL_revanche_due_GLP
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.76 days = 365 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_glp_armistice_grade value = 3 compare = equals } }
			296 = { SHL_armistice_cede_to_glp = yes }
			733 = { SHL_armistice_cede_to_glp = yes }
			293 = { SHL_armistice_cede_to_glp = yes }
			295 = { SHL_armistice_cede_to_glp = yes }
		}
		clr_country_flag = SHL_war_glp_sea_goal
		clr_country_flag = SHL_armistice_settling
		SHL_clear_armistice_glp = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# ----- Keizan ration and invasion (KYZ) -----
# SHL_war_kyz: 0 idle, 1 limited qanat war, 2 ration war. Both wars share the
# tag, so every settlement branch checks the value, not only the enemy.

# COUNTRY MZR/GLP. Reconciliation closes invitations, including unanswered events.
SHL_reconcile_kyz_grievance = {
	clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_border_grievance
	SHL_clear_kyz_partner_offer = yes
}

SHL_clear_kyz_partner_offer = {
	clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_pact_pending
	clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_pact_signed
	clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_neutrality_pending
}

# COUNTRY SHL. A single invitation per preparation period; refusal never replaces
# the announced candidate with an unannounced neighbour.
SHL_prepare_kyz_partner = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_kyz_diplomacy_open = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_diplomacy_started }
		}
		set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_diplomacy_started
		if = {
			limit = { MZR = { SHL_kyz_partner_eligible = yes } }
			MZR = { SHL_invite_kyz_partner = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { GLP = { SHL_kyz_partner_eligible = yes } }
			GLP = { SHL_invite_kyz_partner = yes }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY partner. Wake the existing army before its preparation window begins.
SHL_invite_kyz_partner = {
	ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes
	set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_pact_pending
	SHL = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.124 } }
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.125 }
}

SHL_accept_kyz_pact = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_kyz_partner_offer_current = yes
			has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_pact_pending
		}
		clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_pact_pending
		set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_pact_signed
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Only the eventual agreement charges the quoted resources.
SHL_request_kyz_neutrality = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_kyz_diplomacy_open = yes
			SHL_kyz_neutrality_affordable = yes
			MZR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_neutrality_pending } }
			GLP = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_neutrality_pending } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { MZR = { SHL_kyz_partner_offer_current = yes } }
			MZR = {
				set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_neutrality_pending
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.126 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { GLP = { SHL_kyz_partner_offer_current = yes } }
			GLP = {
				set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_neutrality_pending
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.126 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY partner. The receipt is consumed before payment, so a stale offer
# cannot debit SHL twice or deliver compensation after hostilities begin.
SHL_accept_kyz_neutrality = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_kyz_neutrality_current = yes }
		SHL_reconcile_kyz_grievance = yes
		SHL = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 100 }
			add_political_power = -30
			SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.127 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 100 }
	}
	else = { SHL_decline_kyz_neutrality = yes }
}

SHL_decline_kyz_neutrality = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_neutrality_pending }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_neutrality_pending
		SHL = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.128 } }
	}
}

# Mazar's grievance lasts only for the ultimatum it backs; Veyr's comes from
# its own limited war and survives the ration war's terminal paths.
SHL_clear_kyz_diplomacy = {
	clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_diplomacy_started
	MZR = {
		SHL_clear_kyz_partner_offer = yes
		clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_border_grievance
	}
	GLP = { SHL_clear_kyz_partner_offer = yes }
}

# COUNTRY partner, after the SHL/KYZ declaration. The pact covers this war only.
SHL_join_kyz_side = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_pact_signed
			SHL_kyz_partner_eligible = yes
			SHL = { has_war_with = KYZ }
		}
		KYZ = {
			create_faction_from_template = {
				template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
				name = SHL_kyz_border_pact_faction
			}
			set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_temporary_faction
			add_to_faction = PREV
		}
		set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_war_partner
		add_to_war = {
			targeted_alliance = KYZ
			enemy = SHL
			hostility_reason = asked_to_join
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL or its recorded ally. End only the scripted coalition's wars.
SHL_peace_kyz_side = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_war_with = KYZ }
		white_peace = KYZ
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			MZR = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_war_partner }
			has_war_with = MZR
		}
		white_peace = MZR
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			GLP = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_war_partner }
			has_war_with = GLP
		}
		white_peace = GLP
	}
}

# The "water for metal" treaty (SHL_water_crisis = 2) halves the first payment.
SHL_keizan_pay_first = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 1 compare = equals }
			SHL_keizan_first_payment_affordable = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_first_price value = 250 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 2 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_first_price value = 125 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_keizan_first_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_keizan_first_price }
		add_political_power = -30
		set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_repeat_cycle value = SHL_cycle_number }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_repeat_cycle value = 10 }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_keizan_pay_final = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 400 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 400 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 400 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 3 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_outcome value = 6 }
		SHL_clear_kyz_diplomacy = yes
		SHL_cancel_kyz_mobilization = yes
		SHL_clean_campaign = yes
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_keizan_refuse = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 5 }
		activate_mission = SHL_keizan_deadline
		# Mazar has no limited war with Shahrabad; the chamber of the common
		# ration backs Keizan's water claim, which is its grievance.
		if = {
			limit = {
				MZR = {
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = SHL }
					NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
				}
			}
			MZR = { set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_border_grievance }
		}
		SHL_prepare_kyz_partner = yes
	}
}

# The last payment closes the pending deadline before Keizan can march.
SHL_keizan_buyout = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 5 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 350 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_political_power < 50 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 350 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 350 }
		add_political_power = -50
		set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 3 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_outcome value = 6 }
		SHL_clear_kyz_diplomacy = yes
		SHL_cancel_kyz_mobilization = yes
		SHL_clean_campaign = yes
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_keizan_deadline }
			remove_mission = SHL_keizan_deadline
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY KYZ. Cistern Watch is a Mazrah history template; Keizan receives its
# own locked copy before the war levy, so the AI designer cannot replace it.
SHL_kyz_prepare_cistern_template = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_template = "Cistern Watch" } }
		division_template = {
			name = "Cistern Watch"
			template_counter = 80
			division_names_group = ADISCORD_INF_01
			is_locked = yes
			regiments = {
				ADISCORD_militia = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				ADISCORD_militia = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# STATE scope, owner KYZ.
SHL_kyz_raise_cistern_watch = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = KYZ
			is_controlled_by = KYZ
		}
		create_unit = {
			division = "division_template = \"Cistern Watch\" start_experience_factor = 0.2 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
			owner = KYZ
			allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
		}
		create_unit = {
			division = "division_template = \"Cistern Watch\" start_experience_factor = 0.15 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
			owner = KYZ
			allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
		}
	}
}

# Deadline timeout. A busy or vanished Keizan postpones the demand instead of
# discarding it.
SHL_start_kyz_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 5 compare = equals }
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			SHL_keizan_can_threaten = yes
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 4 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 2 }
		SHL_announce_first_war = yes
		set_country_flag = SHL_ration_war_clock
		SHL_use_campaign_reserve = yes
		SHL = { SHL_reserve_kyz_principal = yes }
		KYZ = { SHL_reserve_kyz_principal = yes }
		KYZ = {
			add_war_support = 0.20
			add_manpower = 12000
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment
				amount = 8000
				producer = KYZ
			}
			SHL_kyz_prepare_cistern_template = yes
		}
		280 = { SHL_kyz_raise_cistern_watch = yes }
		284 = { SHL_kyz_raise_cistern_watch = yes }
		283 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_owned_by = KYZ
					is_controlled_by = KYZ
				}
				create_unit = {
