					division = "division_template = \"Cistern Watch\" start_experience_factor = 0.1 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
					owner = KYZ
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_offensive }
			declare_war_on = {
				target = KYZ
				type = take_state_focus
				generator = { 280 284 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			KYZ = {
				declare_war_on = {
					target = SHL
					type = take_state_focus
					generator = { 707 }
				}
			}
		}
		MZR = { SHL_join_kyz_side = yes }
		GLP = { SHL_join_kyz_side = yes }
		MZR = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = yes
					is_in_faction_with = SHL
					NOT = { has_war_with = KYZ }
				}
				add_to_war = {
					targeted_alliance = SHL
					enemy = KYZ
					hostility_reason = asked_to_join
				}
				set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_shl_partner
			}
		}
		GLP = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = yes
					is_in_faction_with = SHL
					NOT = { has_war_with = KYZ }
				}
				add_to_war = {
					targeted_alliance = SHL
					enemy = KYZ
					hostility_reason = asked_to_join
				}
				set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_shl_partner
			}
		}
		SHL_clear_kyz_diplomacy = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.71 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.72 days = 300 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 5 compare = equals } }
		SHL_clear_kyz_diplomacy = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_repeat_cycle value = SHL_cycle_number }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_repeat_cycle value = 4 }
	}
}

SHL_close_kyz_war = {
	# Close the dispatcher guard before white peace can invoke on_peace.
	set_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 0 }
	SHL_peace_kyz_side = yes
	MZR = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_shl_partner }
			SHL_peace_kyz_side = yes
		}
	}
	GLP = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_shl_partner }
			SHL_peace_kyz_side = yes
		}
	}
	# Annexation can move leadership to the remaining signatory before on_annex.
	MZR = { SHL_dissolve_inherited_kyz_pact = yes }
	GLP = { SHL_dissolve_inherited_kyz_pact = yes }
	KYZ = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_temporary_faction }
			if = {
				limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
				dismantle_faction = yes
			}
			clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_temporary_faction
		}
	}
	MZR = {
		clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_shl_partner
		clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_war_partner
	}
	GLP = {
		clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_shl_partner
		clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_war_partner
	}
	SHL_clear_kyz_diplomacy = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 3 }
	clr_country_flag = SHL_ration_war_clock
	clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_offensive
	SHL_front_clear_kyz = yes
	SHL_clean_campaign = yes
	SHL = { SHL_release_kyz_principal = yes }
	KYZ = { SHL_release_kyz_principal = yes }
}

SHL_dissolve_inherited_kyz_pact = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_war_partner
			is_faction_leader = yes
			KYZ = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_temporary_faction }
		}
		dismantle_faction = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY principal; an allied capitulation must not end this limited war.
SHL_reserve_kyz_principal = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_major = no
				has_country_flag = RUS_crisis_added_major
				RUS_crisis_other_major_required = yes
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_added_major
		set_major = yes
	}
}

SHL_release_kyz_principal = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_added_major }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_added_major
		RUS_crisis_release_other_major = yes
	}
}

SHL_kyz_war_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 2 compare = equals } }
		SHL_announce_keizan_repelled = yes
		SHL_close_kyz_war = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_outcome value = 1 }
		280 = { SHL_take_state_from_kyz = yes }
		284 = { SHL_take_state_from_kyz = yes }
		SHL_recover_ceded_from_kyz_all = yes
		add_timed_idea = { idea = SHL_victory_over_ration days = 365 }
		set_country_flag = SHL_revanche_due_KYZ
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.76 days = 365 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 5 }
		SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.73 }
	}
}

# Keizan requisitions two fifths of a positive treasury; the furnaces stay.
SHL_kyz_war_defeat = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 2 compare = equals } }
		SHL_close_kyz_war = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_outcome value = 4 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_requisition value = ADISCORD_economy_treasury }
			multiply_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_requisition value = 0.4 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_keizan_requisition }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_keizan_requisition }
		}
		add_timed_idea = { idea = SHL_keizan_ration days = 540 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -5 }
		SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.74 }
	}
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.72 records the grade in its immediate block; see
# SHL_qanat_war_armistice for the three grades. The news of a repelled march
# needs Zarkhan to remain Shahrabad's.
SHL_kyz_war_armistice = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_country_flag = SHL_armistice_settling
		SHL_close_kyz_war = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_outcome value = 5 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_armistice_grade value = 1 compare = equals } }
			280 = { SHL_armistice_take_from_kyz = yes }
			284 = { SHL_armistice_take_from_kyz = yes }
			set_country_flag = SHL_revanche_due_KYZ
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.76 days = 365 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_armistice_grade value = 3 compare = equals } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						707 = { SHL_armistice_cession_here = yes }
						287 = { SHL_armistice_cession_here = yes }
						288 = { SHL_armistice_cession_here = yes }
						289 = { SHL_armistice_cession_here = yes }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_outcome value = 4 }
			}
			707 = { SHL_armistice_cede_to_kyz = yes }
			287 = { SHL_armistice_cede_to_kyz = yes }
			288 = { SHL_armistice_cede_to_kyz = yes }
			289 = { SHL_armistice_cede_to_kyz = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 707 = { is_owned_by = SHL } }
			SHL_announce_keizan_repelled = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = SHL_armistice_settling
		SHL_clear_armistice_kyz = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# ----- Limited-war fronts, terms and indemnity -----
# Country scope SHL unless a comment says otherwise. The front predicates are
# scripted triggers; on_state_control_changed queues one check per day, and
# that check alone starts or stops the four hold missions. SHL_terms_war codes:
# 1 qanat war and 2 ration war (Keizan wins), 3 port war (Veyr wins).

# Batches every control change of one day into a single next-day check.
SHL_queue_front_check = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_limited_war_active = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_front_check_pending }
		}
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = SHL_front_check_pending
			value = 1
			days = 2
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.160 days = 1 }
	}
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.160. A hold mission runs only while its side meets its goal.
SHL_front_check = {
	clr_country_flag = SHL_front_check_pending
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_kyz_front_ours = yes
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_kyz_front_hold }
		}
		activate_mission = SHL_kyz_front_hold
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_kyz_front_ours = no
			has_active_mission = SHL_kyz_front_hold
		}
		remove_mission = SHL_kyz_front_hold
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_kyz_front_theirs = yes
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_kyz_front_lost }
		}
		activate_mission = SHL_kyz_front_lost
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_kyz_front_theirs = no
			has_active_mission = SHL_kyz_front_lost
		}
		remove_mission = SHL_kyz_front_lost
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_glp_front_ours = yes
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_glp_front_hold }
		}
		activate_mission = SHL_glp_front_hold
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_glp_front_ours = no
			has_active_mission = SHL_glp_front_hold
		}
		remove_mission = SHL_glp_front_hold
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_glp_front_theirs = yes
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_glp_front_lost }
		}
		activate_mission = SHL_glp_front_lost
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_glp_front_theirs = no
			has_active_mission = SHL_glp_front_lost
		}
		remove_mission = SHL_glp_front_lost
	}
}

# Mission timeouts. The goal is checked again, so a front lost on the last
# day settles nothing.
SHL_resolve_kyz_hold = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_kyz_front_ours = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 1 compare = equals } }
			SHL_qanat_war_victory = yes
		}
		else = { SHL_kyz_war_victory = yes }
	}
}

SHL_resolve_kyz_loss = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_kyz_front_theirs = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 1 compare = equals } }
			SHL_qanat_war_lost = yes
		}
		else = { SHL_kyz_war_lost = yes }
	}
}

SHL_resolve_glp_hold = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_glp_front_ours = yes }
		SHL_glp_war_victory = yes
	}
}

SHL_resolve_glp_loss = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_glp_front_theirs = yes }
		SHL_glp_war_lost = yes
	}
}

# Every close of a Keizan war. The armistice reads its own snapshot after the
# close, so it keeps the flags until it has transferred the districts.
SHL_front_clear_kyz = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_kyz_front_hold }
		remove_mission = SHL_kyz_front_hold
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_kyz_front_lost }
		remove_mission = SHL_kyz_front_lost
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_armistice_settling } }
		SHL_clear_armistice_kyz = yes
	}
}

SHL_front_clear_glp = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_glp_front_hold }
		remove_mission = SHL_glp_front_hold
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_glp_front_lost }
		remove_mission = SHL_glp_front_lost
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_armistice_settling } }
		SHL_clear_armistice_glp = yes
	}
}

SHL_clear_armistice_kyz = {
	280 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	284 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	285 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	286 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	287 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	288 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	289 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	707 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	clear_variable = SHL_kyz_armistice_grade
}

SHL_clear_armistice_glp = {
	297 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	298 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	299 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	708 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	293 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	295 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	296 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	733 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	clear_variable = SHL_glp_armistice_grade
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.56 and .72 immediate. Counts the objectives each side holds and
# records what an armistice would transfer: every Keizan district Shahrabad's
# side holds, Zarkhan when Keizan's side holds it, and at most one furnace
# (287 > 289 > 288), none behind the furnace garrisons. Grades: 1 Shahrabad
# ahead, 2 even, 3 Keizan ahead.
SHL_snapshot_kyz_front = {
	SHL_clear_armistice_kyz = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_front_ours value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_front_theirs value = 0 }
	280 = { SHL_snapshot_kyz_district = yes }
	284 = { SHL_snapshot_kyz_district = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 1 compare = equals } }
		285 = { SHL_snapshot_kyz_district = yes }
		286 = { SHL_snapshot_kyz_district = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 2 compare = equals }
			707 = { SHL_held_by_kyz_side = yes }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_front_theirs value = 1 }
		707 = { set_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 287 = { SHL_held_by_kyz_side = yes } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_front_theirs value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 288 = { SHL_held_by_kyz_side = yes } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_front_theirs value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 289 = { SHL_held_by_kyz_side = yes } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_front_theirs value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_completed_focus = SHL_furnace_garrisons } }
		if = {
			limit = { 287 = { SHL_held_by_kyz_side = yes } }
			287 = { set_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 289 = { SHL_held_by_kyz_side = yes } }
			289 = { set_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 288 = { SHL_held_by_kyz_side = yes } }
			288 = { set_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_armistice_grade value = 2 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_front_ours value = SHL_front_theirs compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_armistice_grade value = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_front_theirs value = SHL_front_ours compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_armistice_grade value = 3 }
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_front_ours
	clear_variable = SHL_front_theirs
}

# STATE Keizan district held by Shahrabad's side.
SHL_snapshot_kyz_district = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = KYZ
			SHL_cistern_coalition_control = yes
		}
		set_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held
		SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_front_ours value = 1 } }
	}
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.65 immediate: the ports (298 and 708 only under the sea goal)
# against Salik, the Veyran border and at most one furnace (295 > 293).
SHL_snapshot_glp_front = {
	SHL_clear_armistice_glp = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_front_ours value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_front_theirs value = 0 }
	297 = { SHL_snapshot_glp_district = yes }
	299 = { SHL_snapshot_glp_district = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_war_glp_sea_goal }
		298 = { SHL_snapshot_glp_district = yes }
		708 = { SHL_snapshot_glp_district = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 296 = { SHL_held_by_glp = yes } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_front_theirs value = 1 }
		296 = { set_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 733 = { SHL_held_by_glp = yes } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_front_theirs value = 1 }
		733 = { set_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 293 = { SHL_held_by_glp = yes } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_front_theirs value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 295 = { SHL_held_by_glp = yes } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_front_theirs value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_completed_focus = SHL_furnace_garrisons } }
		if = {
			limit = { 295 = { SHL_held_by_glp = yes } }
			295 = { set_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 293 = { SHL_held_by_glp = yes } }
			293 = { set_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_glp_armistice_grade value = 2 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_front_ours value = SHL_front_theirs compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_glp_armistice_grade value = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_front_theirs value = SHL_front_ours compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_glp_armistice_grade value = 3 }
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_front_ours
	clear_variable = SHL_front_theirs
}

# STATE Veyr district held by Shahrabad.
SHL_snapshot_glp_district = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = GLP
			is_controlled_by = SHL
		}
		set_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held
		SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_front_ours value = 1 } }
	}
}

# STATE helpers of the armistice; the transfer effects recheck ownership.
SHL_armistice_take_from_kyz = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
		SHL_take_state_from_kyz = yes
	}
}

SHL_armistice_take_from_glp = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
		SHL_take_state_from_glp = yes
	}
}

SHL_armistice_cede_to_kyz = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
		SHL_cede_state_to_kyz = yes
	}
}

SHL_armistice_cede_to_glp = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = SHL_armistice_held }
		SHL_cede_state_to_glp = yes
	}
}

# STATE. Plain transfer of a Shahrabad district to the neighbour.
SHL_hand_state_to_kyz = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = SHL
			KYZ = { exists = yes }
		}
		KYZ = { transfer_state = PREV }
		set_state_controller_to = KYZ
	}
}

SHL_hand_state_to_glp = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = SHL
			GLP = { exists = yes }
		}
		GLP = { transfer_state = PREV }
		set_state_controller_to = GLP
	}
}

# STATE. A cession after a lost war carries the dated flag that the return
# offer of SHL_check_crises and a later victory read.
SHL_cede_state_to_kyz = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = SHL
			KYZ = { exists = yes }
		}
		SHL_hand_state_to_kyz = yes
		set_state_flag = SHL_ceded_state
	}
}

SHL_cede_state_to_glp = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = SHL
			GLP = { exists = yes }
		}
		SHL_hand_state_to_glp = yes
		set_state_flag = SHL_ceded_state
	}
}

# STATE. A ceded district returns from the neighbour that holds it.
SHL_recover_ceded_from_kyz = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = SHL_ceded_state
			is_owned_by = KYZ
		}
		SHL = { transfer_state = PREV }
		set_state_controller_to = SHL
		clr_state_flag = SHL_ceded_state
	}
}

SHL_recover_ceded_from_glp = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = SHL_ceded_state
			is_owned_by = GLP
		}
		SHL = { transfer_state = PREV }
		set_state_controller_to = SHL
		clr_state_flag = SHL_ceded_state
	}
}

SHL_recover_ceded_from_kyz_all = {
	287 = { SHL_recover_ceded_from_kyz = yes }
	288 = { SHL_recover_ceded_from_kyz = yes }
	289 = { SHL_recover_ceded_from_kyz = yes }
	707 = { SHL_recover_ceded_from_kyz = yes }
}

SHL_recover_ceded_from_glp_all = {
	293 = { SHL_recover_ceded_from_glp = yes }
	295 = { SHL_recover_ceded_from_glp = yes }
	296 = { SHL_recover_ceded_from_glp = yes }
	733 = { SHL_recover_ceded_from_glp = yes }
}

# The price of a lost limited war, paid on every route to terms.
SHL_defeat_fallout = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -15 }
	SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 5 }
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.10 }
	SHL_balance_shift = yes
	add_war_support = -0.05
}

# Timeout of SHL_kyz_front_lost or SHL_glp_front_lost. The candidates are
# recorded while the winner still holds them; one set of terms is pending at a
# time, and a second loss inside its window ends as the status quo. The year of
# border truce keeps the dispute decisions closed whatever terms the winner
# picks, so a new declaration cannot suspend the tithe.
SHL_qanat_war_lost = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 1 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SHL_terms_war } }
			SHL_record_kyz_terms_candidates = yes
			set_variable = { var = SHL_terms_war value = 1 }
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = SHL_terms_pending
				value = 1
				days = 40
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -10 }
		SHL_change_qanat_houses_loyalty = yes
		SHL_close_qanat_war = yes
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = SHL_border_truce_KYZ
			value = 1
			days = 365
		}
		SHL_defeat_fallout = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_terms_war value = 1 compare = equals } }
			KYZ = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.161 } }
		}
	}
}

SHL_kyz_war_lost = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 2 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SHL_terms_war } }
			SHL_record_kyz_terms_candidates = yes
			set_variable = { var = SHL_terms_war value = 2 }
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = SHL_terms_pending
				value = 1
				days = 40
			}
		}
		SHL_close_kyz_war = yes
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = SHL_border_truce_KYZ
			value = 1
			days = 365
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_outcome value = 4 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -5 }
		SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
		SHL_defeat_fallout = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_terms_war value = 2 compare = equals } }
			KYZ = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.161 } }
		}
	}
}

SHL_glp_war_lost = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_glp value = 1 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SHL_terms_war } }
			SHL_record_glp_terms_candidates = yes
			set_variable = { var = SHL_terms_war value = 3 }
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = SHL_terms_pending
				value = 1
				days = 40
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -10 }
		SHL_change_port_houses_loyalty = yes
		SHL_close_glp_war = yes
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = SHL_border_truce_GLP
			value = 1
			days = 365
		}
		clr_country_flag = SHL_war_glp_sea_goal
		SHL_defeat_fallout = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_terms_war value = 3 compare = equals } }
			GLP = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.161 } }
		}
	}
}

# Zarkhan in the ration war and at most one qanat furnace (287 > 289 > 288);
# the furnace garrisons keep every furnace out of the terms.
SHL_record_kyz_terms_candidates = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 2 compare = equals }
			707 = { SHL_held_by_kyz_side = yes }
		}
		707 = { set_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_completed_focus = SHL_furnace_garrisons } }
		if = {
			limit = { 287 = { SHL_held_by_kyz_side = yes } }
			287 = { set_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 289 = { SHL_held_by_kyz_side = yes } }
			289 = { set_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 288 = { SHL_held_by_kyz_side = yes } }
			288 = { set_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
		}
	}
}

# Salik and the Veyran border when held, and at most one furnace (295 > 293).
SHL_record_glp_terms_candidates = {
	if = {
		limit = { 296 = { SHL_held_by_glp = yes } }
		296 = { set_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 733 = { SHL_held_by_glp = yes } }
		733 = { set_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_completed_focus = SHL_furnace_garrisons } }
		if = {
			limit = { 295 = { SHL_held_by_glp = yes } }
			295 = { set_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 293 = { SHL_held_by_glp = yes } }
			293 = { set_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
		}
	}
}

SHL_clear_terms_candidates = {
	287 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
	288 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
	289 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
	293 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
	295 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
	296 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
	707 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
	733 = { clr_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
}

# STATE helpers of the district terms.
SHL_cede_candidate_to_kyz = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
		SHL_cede_state_to_kyz = yes
	}
}

SHL_cede_candidate_to_glp = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = SHL_terms_candidate }
		SHL_cede_state_to_glp = yes
	}
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.161 answers, run in SHL scope with the winner as ROOT. The
# pending SHL_terms_war is the receipt: each answer settles it once.
SHL_terms_tithe = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = SHL_terms_war
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_indemnity_due }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_due value = 240 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_recipient value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_terms_war value = 2 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_due value = 320 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_terms_war value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_recipient value = 2 }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_last_terms value = 1 }
		SHL_terms_finish = yes
	}
}

SHL_terms_land = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_terms_war }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_terms_war value = 3 compare = equals } }
			296 = { SHL_cede_candidate_to_glp = yes }
			733 = { SHL_cede_candidate_to_glp = yes }
			293 = { SHL_cede_candidate_to_glp = yes }
			295 = { SHL_cede_candidate_to_glp = yes }
		}
		else = {
			707 = { SHL_cede_candidate_to_kyz = yes }
			287 = { SHL_cede_candidate_to_kyz = yes }
			288 = { SHL_cede_candidate_to_kyz = yes }
			289 = { SHL_cede_candidate_to_kyz = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_last_terms value = 2 }
		SHL_terms_finish = yes
	}
}

# The treaty binds both sides for two years and adds the war's economic rider.
SHL_terms_treaty = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_terms_war }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_terms_war value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = SHL_neutral_to_GLP
				value = 1
				days = 730
			}
			add_timed_idea = { idea = SHL_veyr_bondage days = 365 }
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = SHL_neutral_to_KYZ
				value = 1
				days = 730
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_terms_war value = 1 compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_water_quota value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 1 compare = equals } }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 2 }
				}
			}
			else = { add_timed_idea = { idea = SHL_keizan_ration days = 540 } }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_last_terms value = 3 }
		SHL_terms_finish = yes
	}
}

# Fires the report to Shahrabad and the regional news with the winner in FROM.
SHL_terms_finish = {
	SHL_clear_terms_candidates = yes
	clr_country_flag = SHL_terms_pending
	clear_variable = SHL_terms_war
	SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.162 }
	news_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL_news.10 hours = 6 }
}

# Per cycle. Unanswered terms die with their 40-day flag; a redemption offer
# that its neighbour never answered is refunded in full. A ceded district that
# came back by another route (annexation, a later peace) drops its old claim, so
# a future loss of it never inherits an aged return offer.
SHL_expire_border_offers = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = SHL_terms_war
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_terms_pending }
		}
		SHL_clear_terms_candidates = yes
		clear_variable = SHL_terms_war
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = SHL_redemption_deposit
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_redemption_pending }
		}
		SHL_refund_redemption = yes
	}
	287 = { SHL_drop_returned_cession = yes }
	288 = { SHL_drop_returned_cession = yes }
	289 = { SHL_drop_returned_cession = yes }
	293 = { SHL_drop_returned_cession = yes }
	295 = { SHL_drop_returned_cession = yes }
	296 = { SHL_drop_returned_cession = yes }
	707 = { SHL_drop_returned_cession = yes }
	733 = { SHL_drop_returned_cession = yes }
}

# STATE. See SHL_expire_border_offers.
SHL_drop_returned_cession = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = SHL_ceded_state
			is_owned_by = SHL
		}
		clr_state_flag = SHL_ceded_state
	}
}

# Inside SHL_produce_period. Every held lit furnace of the cycle pays 8 of the
# debt, never more than remains; the debt waits while the recipient fights
# Shahrabad and ends with a vanished or subject recipient. Codes: 1 KYZ, 2 GLP.
SHL_pay_indemnity = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_indemnity_recipient_valid = no }
		SHL_clear_indemnity = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SHL_indemnity_recipient_at_peace = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_payment value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				287 = {
					SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_payment value = 8 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				288 = {
					SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_payment value = 8 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				289 = {
					SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_payment value = 8 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				290 = {
					SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_payment value = 8 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				291 = {
					SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_payment value = 8 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				292 = {
					SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_payment value = 8 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				293 = {
					SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_payment value = 8 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				294 = {
					SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_payment value = 8 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				295 = {
					SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_payment value = 8 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_payment value = SHL_indemnity_due compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_payment value = SHL_indemnity_due }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_indemnity_payment }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_indemnity_payment }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_last_cycle_income value = SHL_indemnity_payment }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_recipient value = 1 compare = equals } }
			KYZ = {
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = PREV.SHL_indemnity_payment }
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = PREV.SHL_indemnity_payment }
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			GLP = {
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = PREV.SHL_indemnity_payment }
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = PREV.SHL_indemnity_payment }
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_due value = SHL_indemnity_payment }
		clear_variable = SHL_indemnity_payment
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_completed_focus = SHL_border_settlement_office } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -2 }
			SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
			SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_due value = 0 compare = less_than_or_equals } }
			SHL_clear_indemnity = yes
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

SHL_clear_indemnity = {
	clear_variable = SHL_indemnity_due
	clear_variable = SHL_indemnity_recipient
}

# SHL_settle_indemnity: the remaining debt and 25 political power, once.
SHL_buy_out_indemnity = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = SHL_indemnity_due
			SHL_indemnity_recipient_valid = yes
			SHL_indemnity_recipient_at_peace = yes
			SHL_indemnity_buyout_affordable = yes
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_indemnity_due }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_indemnity_due }
		add_political_power = -25
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_indemnity_recipient value = 1 compare = equals } }
			KYZ = {
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = PREV.SHL_indemnity_due }
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = PREV.SHL_indemnity_due }
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			GLP = {
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = PREV.SHL_indemnity_due }
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = PREV.SHL_indemnity_due }
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		SHL_clear_indemnity = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

# ----- Return of ceded districts -----
# SHL_check_crises offers ADISCORD_SHL.163 once a ceded district has waited its
# year (half a year with the border settlement office). The redemption is an
# escrow: SHL_redemption_deposit and SHL_redemption_pp until the neighbour
# answers ADISCORD_SHL.164 or its 40-day pending flag lapses. Codes: 1 KYZ, 2 GLP.

# ADISCORD_SHL.163 immediate. Keizan is answered first when both are due.
SHL_pick_return_neighbour = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_return_neighbour value = 2 }
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_return_offer_kyz_ready = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_return_neighbour value = 1 }
	}
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.163 answer: the Conclave accepts the border of that neighbour.
SHL_accept_ceded_border = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_return_neighbour value = 1 compare = equals } }
		287 = { SHL_drop_ceded_claim_kyz = yes }
		288 = { SHL_drop_ceded_claim_kyz = yes }
		289 = { SHL_drop_ceded_claim_kyz = yes }
		707 = { SHL_drop_ceded_claim_kyz = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_return_neighbour value = 2 compare = equals } }
		293 = { SHL_drop_ceded_claim_glp = yes }
		295 = { SHL_drop_ceded_claim_glp = yes }
		296 = { SHL_drop_ceded_claim_glp = yes }
		733 = { SHL_drop_ceded_claim_glp = yes }
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_return_neighbour
	set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 5 }
	SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
}

# STATE helpers: only the answering neighbour's districts are released.
SHL_drop_ceded_claim_kyz = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_owned_by = KYZ }
		clr_state_flag = SHL_ceded_state
	}
}

SHL_drop_ceded_claim_glp = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_owned_by = GLP }
		clr_state_flag = SHL_ceded_state
	}
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.163 answer. Payment is held until the neighbour's own choice.
SHL_offer_redemption = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_redemption_deposit }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_return_neighbour value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			SHL_redemption_affordable = yes
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_redemption_deposit value = 250 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_border_settlement_office }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_redemption_deposit value = 200 }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_redemption_pp value = 25 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_redemption_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_redemption_deposit }
		add_political_power = -25
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = SHL_redemption_pending
			value = 1
			days = 40
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_return_neighbour value = 1 compare = equals } }
			KYZ = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.164 } }
		}
		else = {
			GLP = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.164 } }
		}
	}
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.164 acceptance, called in SHL scope. The deposit and the
# districts change hands together and only once. A neighbour that no longer
# owns any ceded district by the time it answers receives nothing; the escrow
# returns to Shahrabad in full.
SHL_complete_redemption = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = SHL_redemption_deposit
			has_country_flag = SHL_redemption_pending
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			SHL_redemption_deliverable = no
		}
		SHL_refund_redemption = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = SHL_redemption_deposit
			has_country_flag = SHL_redemption_pending
			SHL_country_operable = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_return_neighbour value = 1 compare = equals } }
			KYZ = {
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = PREV.SHL_redemption_deposit }
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = PREV.SHL_redemption_deposit }
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
			SHL_recover_ceded_from_kyz_all = yes
		}
		else = {
			GLP = {
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = PREV.SHL_redemption_deposit }
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = PREV.SHL_redemption_deposit }
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
			SHL_recover_ceded_from_glp_all = yes
		}
		clear_variable = SHL_redemption_deposit
		clear_variable = SHL_redemption_pp
		clr_country_flag = SHL_redemption_pending
		clear_variable = SHL_return_neighbour
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.165 }
	}
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.164 refusal, called in SHL scope: a full refund, and the
# refusal reopens the limited war against that neighbour.
SHL_refuse_redemption = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_redemption_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_return_neighbour value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_country_flag = SHL_redemption_refused_KYZ
		}
		else = { set_country_flag = SHL_redemption_refused_GLP }
		SHL_refund_redemption = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.166 }
	}
}

SHL_refund_redemption = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_redemption_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_redemption_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_redemption_deposit }
		add_political_power = SHL_redemption_pp
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		clear_variable = SHL_redemption_deposit
		clear_variable = SHL_redemption_pp
		clr_country_flag = SHL_redemption_pending
		clear_variable = SHL_return_neighbour
	}
}

# ----- Answers of the victor (ADISCORD_SHL.57.b and .66.b) -----

# The head wells and the districts behind them go back to the communities in
# exchange for a lasting peace.
SHL_return_wells_to_communities = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_return_wells = yes }
		280 = { SHL_hand_state_to_kyz = yes }
		284 = { SHL_hand_state_to_kyz = yes }
		285 = { SHL_hand_state_to_kyz = yes }
		286 = { SHL_hand_state_to_kyz = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 5 }
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = SHL_neutral_to_KYZ
			value = 1
			days = 730
		}
		clr_country_flag = SHL_revanche_due_KYZ
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = SHL_captured_qanats }
			remove_ideas = SHL_captured_qanats
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 10 }
		SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# The ports return to Veyr under a harbour union instead of an occupation.
SHL_open_harbour_union = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_open_harbour_union = yes }
		297 = { SHL_hand_state_to_glp = yes }
		299 = { SHL_hand_state_to_glp = yes }
		298 = { SHL_hand_state_to_glp = yes }
		708 = { SHL_hand_state_to_glp = yes }
		set_country_flag = SHL_veyr_partner
		GLP = { SHL_reconcile_kyz_grievance = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_veyr_crisis value = 2 }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_revanche_due_GLP
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# ----- Field batteries of the border furnaces (operation 13) -----
# The battery holds the site's operation slot for 14 days: the paid escrow is
# the site's SHL_operation_deposit, refunded by SHL_cancel_operation when the
# district is lost.

SHL_begin_front_battery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				SHL_furnace_site_idle = yes
				SHL_front_battery_site = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_political_power < 20 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 100 }
		add_political_power = -20
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 13 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_deposit value = 100 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_pp value = 20 }
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_front_battery = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 13 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes } }
			FROM = {
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 10 }
				clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear min = 0 max = 100 }
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_deposit
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_pp
				clear_variable = SHL_operation
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_siege_founders }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = artillery_equipment
					amount = 48
					producer = SHL
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = artillery_equipment
					amount = 36
					producer = SHL
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { SHL_front_battery_site = yes } }
				SHL_front_battery_strike = yes
			}
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_operation = yes }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# FROM is the battery site. Explicit neighbour lists keep the strike on the
# districts that border it.
SHL_front_battery_strike = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { state = 287 } }
		280 = { SHL_front_battery_hit_kyz = yes }
		286 = { SHL_front_battery_hit_kyz = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { FROM = { state = 288 } }
		280 = { SHL_front_battery_hit_kyz = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { FROM = { state = 289 } }
		284 = { SHL_front_battery_hit_kyz = yes }
		285 = { SHL_front_battery_hit_kyz = yes }
		286 = { SHL_front_battery_hit_kyz = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { FROM = { state = 293 } }
		297 = { SHL_front_battery_hit_glp = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { FROM = { state = 295 } }
		298 = { SHL_front_battery_hit_glp = yes }
		299 = { SHL_front_battery_hit_glp = yes }
		708 = { SHL_front_battery_hit_glp = yes }
	}
}

# STATE. Only land the enemy owns and holds is shelled; siege founders also
# hit its arms works.
SHL_front_battery_hit_kyz = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = KYZ
			is_controlled_by = KYZ
			SHL = { has_war_with = KYZ }
		}
		SHL_front_battery_hit = yes
	}
}

SHL_front_battery_hit_glp = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = GLP
			is_controlled_by = GLP
			SHL = { has_war_with = GLP }
		}
		SHL_front_battery_hit = yes
	}
}

SHL_front_battery_hit = {
	if = {
		limit = { infrastructure > 0 }
		damage_building = {
			type = infrastructure
			damage = 1
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL = { has_completed_focus = SHL_siege_founders }
			arms_factory > 0
		}
		damage_building = {
			type = arms_factory
			damage = 1
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Border-war focuses add to the SHL_bw_* variables; the modifier
# acts only while a limited war is fought.
SHL_refresh_border_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_border_war_dynamic } } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_border_war_dynamic }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# ----- Alliances -----

# Partner scope after its own answer; an existing SHL alliance is reused.
SHL_join_southern_compact = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL = {
				SHL_can_lead_alliance = yes
				has_country_flag = SHL_southern_alliance_pending
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 2 compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_south_coalition_target_war_clear = yes
			is_subject = no
			is_in_faction = no
			NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
		}
		SHL = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_faction = no }
				create_faction_from_template = {
					template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
					name = SHL_southern_compact_faction
				}
			}
			add_to_faction = PREV
			clr_country_flag = SHL_southern_alliance_pending
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.92 }
		}
		SHL_reconcile_kyz_grievance = yes
	}
	else = {
		SHL = {
			clr_country_flag = SHL_southern_alliance_pending
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.93 }
		}
	}
}

SHL_join_golden_gate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL = {
				SHL_can_lead_alliance = yes
				has_country_flag = SHL_golden_alliance_pending
				check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
			ADISCORD_south_coalition_target_war_clear = yes
			is_subject = no
			is_in_faction = no
			NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
		}
		SHL = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_faction = no }
				create_faction_from_template = {
					template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
					name = SHL_golden_gate_faction
				}
			}
			add_to_faction = PREV
			clr_country_flag = SHL_golden_alliance_pending
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.92 }
		}
		SHL_reconcile_kyz_grievance = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				SHL = { has_war_with = KYZ }
				NOT = { has_war_with = KYZ }
			}
			add_to_war = {
				targeted_alliance = SHL
				enemy = KYZ
				hostility_reason = asked_to_join
			}
			set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_shl_partner
		}
	}
	else = {
		SHL = {
			clr_country_flag = SHL_golden_alliance_pending
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.93 }
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Obligations of the furnaces. Country ledger; production enters from its site.
# Only the 28-day production callback advances work or withholds revenue.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

SHL_record_obligation_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { state = 287 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 287 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 288 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 288 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 289 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 289 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 290 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 290 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 291 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 291 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 292 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 292 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 293 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 293 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 294 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 294 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 295 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 295 } }
	}
}

SHL_record_relief_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { state = 287 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 287 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 288 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 288 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 289 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 289 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 290 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 290 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 291 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 291 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 292 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 292 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 293 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 293 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 294 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 294 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 295 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 295 } }
	}
}

SHL_open_obligation = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_deposit value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_liability value = 0 }
	FROM = { SHL_record_obligation_site = yes }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_progress value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_owner value = FROM.SHL_furnace_owner }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_course value = SHL_political_course }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_deadline value = SHL_cycle_number }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_deadline value = 3 }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
}

SHL_clear_obligation = {
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_batch_price
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_kind
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_site
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_recipient
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_progress
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_owner
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_course
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_deadline
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_deposit
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_liability
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_overdue
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_extended
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
}

# Cancellation settles the remaining receipt once, including money already spent.
# An unpaid advance becomes ordinary public debt instead of negative cash.
SHL_cancel_obligation = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_obligation_kind }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_obligation_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_obligation_deposit }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_payment value = ADISCORD_economy_treasury }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_payment min = 0 max = SHL_obligation_liability }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_obligation_payment }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_obligation_payment }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_liability value = SHL_obligation_payment }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_debt value = SHL_obligation_liability }
		ADISCORD_economy_reprice_debt = yes
		SHL_clear_obligation = yes
		clear_variable = SHL_obligation_payment
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

SHL_begin_export = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_obligation_idle = yes
			SHL_export_access = yes
			FROM = {
				SHL_obligation_site_ready = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		SHL_open_obligation = yes
		FROM = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 11 compare = equals } }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_deadline value = 1 }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 1 }
		# The liability returns the advance plus a 30 penalty on cancellation.
		SHL_refresh_programme_prices = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_liability value = SHL_export_advance }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_liability value = 30 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_export_advance }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_export_advance }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_begin_civic_order = {
	SHL_refresh_delivery_terms = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_obligation_idle = yes
			SHL_civic_access = yes
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_civic_price compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			FROM = {
				SHL_obligation_site_ready = yes
				infrastructure < 5
			}
		}
		SHL_open_obligation = yes
		FROM = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 2 } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_deposit value = SHL_civic_price }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_civic_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_civic_price }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_begin_arsenal_order = {
	SHL_refresh_delivery_terms = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_obligation_idle = yes
			SHL_arsenal_access = yes
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_arsenal_price compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			FROM = { SHL_obligation_site_ready = yes }
		}
		SHL_open_obligation = yes
		FROM = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 1 } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 3 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_deposit value = SHL_arsenal_price }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_batch_price value = SHL_arsenal_price }
		divide_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_batch_price value = 2 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_arsenal_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_arsenal_price }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_begin_pledge = {
	SHL_refresh_delivery_terms = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_obligation_idle = yes
			has_completed_focus = SHL_regency_council
			check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 4 compare = equals }
			FROM = {
				SHL_obligation_site_ready = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		SHL_open_obligation = yes
		FROM = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 4 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_liability value = SHL_pledge_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 150 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 8 }
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_begin_relief = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_obligation_idle = yes
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = SHL_furnaces_to_workers
				has_completed_focus = SHL_k_rotating_smelting
			}
			check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals }
			FROM = {
				SHL_obligation_site_ready = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		SHL_open_obligation = yes
		FROM = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 2 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 52 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_progress value = 1 }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 5 }
	}
}

SHL_choose_relief_recipient = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 5 compare = equals }
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_obligation_recipient }
			FROM = { SHL_relief_site_ready = yes }
		}
		FROM = { SHL_record_relief_site = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

# Ownership loss or a new constitution settles the old institution's receipt.
# Occupation alone pauses work; liberation resumes its stocks and progress.
SHL_reconcile_obligation = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_obligation_kind }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 6 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_course value = SHL_political_course }
		}
		# Ratification preserves the provisional programme and its paid work.
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_course value = 0 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = SHL_obligation_kind compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 3 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_course value = SHL_political_course }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_owner value = SHL_political_course }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_owner value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = SHL_obligation_course compare = equals } } }
			SHL_cancel_obligation = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 287 compare = equals } }
			287 = { SHL_check_obligation_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 288 compare = equals } }
			288 = { SHL_check_obligation_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 289 compare = equals } }
			289 = { SHL_check_obligation_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 290 compare = equals } }
			290 = { SHL_check_obligation_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 291 compare = equals } }
			291 = { SHL_check_obligation_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 292 compare = equals } }
			292 = { SHL_check_obligation_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 293 compare = equals } }
			293 = { SHL_check_obligation_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 294 compare = equals } }
			294 = { SHL_check_obligation_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 295 compare = equals } }
			295 = { SHL_check_obligation_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 287 compare = equals } }
			287 = { SHL_check_relief_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 288 compare = equals } }
			288 = { SHL_check_relief_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 289 compare = equals } }
			289 = { SHL_check_relief_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 290 compare = equals } }
			290 = { SHL_check_relief_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 291 compare = equals } }
			291 = { SHL_check_relief_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 292 compare = equals } }
			292 = { SHL_check_relief_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 293 compare = equals } }
			293 = { SHL_check_relief_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 294 compare = equals } }
			294 = { SHL_check_relief_owner = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 295 compare = equals } }
			295 = { SHL_check_relief_owner = yes }
		}
	}
}

SHL_check_obligation_owner = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { is_owned_by = SHL }
				AND = {
					SHL = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 6 compare = equals } } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = SHL.SHL_obligation_owner compare = equals } }
				}
			}
		}
		SHL = { SHL_cancel_obligation = yes }
	}
}

SHL_check_relief_owner = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_owned_by = SHL } }
		SHL = { SHL_cancel_obligation = yes }
	}
}

SHL_check_relief_ready = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_relief_site_ready = yes }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient_ready value = 1 } }
	}
}

# STATE scope, called only after raw materials have actually been consumed.
SHL_apply_site_obligation = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_obligation_here = yes }
		SHL = { SHL_progress_obligation = yes }
	}
}

# PREV is the producing site. A pledge diverts cash, other mandates divert metal.
SHL_progress_obligation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 4 compare = equals } }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 6 compare = less_than }
		}
		PREV = { set_variable = { var = SHL_period_cash value = 0 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 4 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_payment value = PREV.SHL_period_cash }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_payment min = 0 max = SHL_obligation_liability }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_liability value = SHL_obligation_payment }
		PREV = { subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_period_cash value = SHL.SHL_obligation_payment } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_liability value = 0 compare = less_than_or_equals } }
			SHL_clear_obligation = yes
		}
		clear_variable = SHL_obligation_payment
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_obligation_overdue }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_progress value = 2 compare = less_than }
		}
		SHL_refresh_relief_readiness = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 5 compare = equals } }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient_ready value = 1 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			PREV = { set_variable = { var = SHL_period_cash value = 0 } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_work value = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 1 compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_work value = 2 }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_remaining value = 2 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_remaining value = SHL_obligation_progress }
			clamp_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_work min = 0 max = SHL_obligation_remaining }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_progress value = SHL_obligation_work }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 3 compare = equals } }
				SHL_deliver_arsenal_batch = yes
			}
		}
		clear_variable = SHL_obligation_recipient_ready
		clear_variable = SHL_obligation_work
		clear_variable = SHL_obligation_remaining
	}
}

SHL_deliver_arsenal_batch = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_payment value = SHL_obligation_work }
	multiply_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_payment value = SHL_obligation_batch_price }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_deposit value = SHL_obligation_payment }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_rifles value = SHL_obligation_work }
	multiply_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_rifles value = 6750 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_support value = SHL_obligation_work }
	multiply_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_support value = 5 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
		type = infantry_equipment
		amount = SHL_obligation_rifles
		producer = SHL
	}
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
		type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0
		amount = SHL_obligation_support
		producer = SHL
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_payment
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_rifles
	clear_variable = SHL_obligation_support
}

SHL_deliver_civic_order = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
			infrastructure < 5
		}
		add_building_construction = {
			type = infrastructure
			level = 1
			instant_build = yes
		}
		SHL = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_deposit value = 0 }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 5 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 5 }
		}
	}
}

SHL_deliver_relief = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_relief_site_ready = yes }
		SHL = { SHL_refresh_programme_prices = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = SHL.SHL_cauldron_repair }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear min = 0 max = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 0 compare = equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 20 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 0 }
			clear_variable = SHL_restart_settled
		}
		SHL = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 8 }
			SHL_clear_obligation = yes
		}
	}
}

SHL_complete_obligation = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 1 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = SHL_obligation_extended }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 90 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 90 }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 120 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 120 }
		}
		clear_variable = SHL_obligation_liability
		# The export house earned the contract.
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_site value = SHL_obligation_site }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 15 }
		SHL_change_site_loyalty = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 2 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 287 compare = equals } }
			287 = { SHL_deliver_civic_order = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 288 compare = equals } }
			288 = { SHL_deliver_civic_order = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 289 compare = equals } }
			289 = { SHL_deliver_civic_order = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 290 compare = equals } }
			290 = { SHL_deliver_civic_order = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 291 compare = equals } }
			291 = { SHL_deliver_civic_order = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 292 compare = equals } }
			292 = { SHL_deliver_civic_order = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 293 compare = equals } }
			293 = { SHL_deliver_civic_order = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 294 compare = equals } }
			294 = { SHL_deliver_civic_order = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = 295 compare = equals } }
			295 = { SHL_deliver_civic_order = yes }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_obligation_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_obligation_deposit }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 5 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 287 compare = equals } }
			287 = { SHL_deliver_relief = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 288 compare = equals } }
			288 = { SHL_deliver_relief = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 289 compare = equals } }
			289 = { SHL_deliver_relief = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 290 compare = equals } }
			290 = { SHL_deliver_relief = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 291 compare = equals } }
			291 = { SHL_deliver_relief = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 292 compare = equals } }
			292 = { SHL_deliver_relief = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 293 compare = equals } }
			293 = { SHL_deliver_relief = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 294 compare = equals } }
			294 = { SHL_deliver_relief = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 295 compare = equals } }
			295 = { SHL_deliver_relief = yes }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 6 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_campaign_reserve value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_war_with = KYZ
					has_war_with = GLP
					has_war_with = MZR
				}
			}
			SHL_use_campaign_reserve = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 5 compare = equals } } }
		SHL_clear_obligation = yes
	}
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

SHL_check_obligation_deadline = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 1 compare = equals }
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_obligation_overdue }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_number value = SHL_obligation_deadline compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_overdue value = 1 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.101 }
	}
}

SHL_extend_export = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 1 compare = equals }
			has_variable = SHL_obligation_overdue
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_obligation_extended }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_extended value = 1 }
		clear_variable = SHL_obligation_overdue
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_deadline value = SHL_cycle_number }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_deadline value = 2 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_liability value = 30 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 8 }
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
	}
}
