SHL_refresh_relief_readiness = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient_ready value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 287 compare = equals } }
		287 = { SHL_check_relief_ready = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 288 compare = equals } }
		288 = { SHL_check_relief_ready = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 289 compare = equals } }
		289 = { SHL_check_relief_ready = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 290 compare = equals } }
		290 = { SHL_check_relief_ready = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 291 compare = equals } }
		291 = { SHL_check_relief_ready = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 292 compare = equals } }
		292 = { SHL_check_relief_ready = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 293 compare = equals } }
		293 = { SHL_check_relief_ready = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 294 compare = equals } }
		294 = { SHL_check_relief_ready = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_recipient value = 295 compare = equals } }
		295 = { SHL_check_relief_ready = yes }
	}
}

SHL_request_golden_alliance = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_can_lead_alliance = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 1 compare = equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_golden_alliance_pending }
			GLP = {
				exists = yes
				ADISCORD_south_coalition_target_war_clear = yes
				is_subject = no
				is_in_faction = no
				NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = SHL_golden_alliance_pending
		GLP = {
			ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.91 }
		}
	}
}

SHL_request_southern_alliance = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_can_lead_alliance = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_southern_alliance_pending }
			KYZ = {
				exists = yes
				ADISCORD_south_coalition_target_war_clear = yes
				is_subject = no
				is_in_faction = no
				NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = SHL_southern_alliance_pending
		KYZ = {
			ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.90 }
		}
	}
}

# Close only the interrupted contest; external peace awards no authored treaty.
SHL_close_external_kyz_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 2 compare = equals }
			OR = {
				exists = no
				is_subject = yes
				KYZ = {
					OR = {
						exists = no
						is_subject = yes
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_war_with = KYZ }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 0 }
		SHL_close_kyz_war = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_outcome value = 5 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 1 compare = equals }
			OR = {
				exists = no
				is_subject = yes
				KYZ = {
					OR = {
						exists = no
						is_subject = yes
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_war_with = KYZ }
			}
		}
		SHL_close_qanat_war = yes
	}
}

# The port war's counterpart of SHL_close_external_kyz_war: the status quo.
SHL_close_external_glp_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_war_glp value = 1 compare = equals }
			OR = {
				exists = no
				is_subject = yes
				GLP = {
					OR = {
						exists = no
						is_subject = yes
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_war_with = GLP }
			}
		}
		SHL_close_glp_war = yes
		clr_country_flag = SHL_war_glp_sea_goal
	}
}

# Starting institutions are removed through their actual reforms, never a timer.
SHL_refresh_starting_institutions = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SHL_hereditary_seats
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = SHL_admit_tenth_house
				has_completed_focus = SHL_rights_without_fire
				has_completed_focus = SHL_state_furnaces
				has_completed_focus = SHL_restore_privileges
				has_completed_focus = SHL_furnaces_to_workers
			}
		}
		remove_ideas = SHL_hereditary_seats
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = SHL_external_freight
			has_completed_focus = SHL_own_transport
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				SHL_qanat_counterpart = no
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_veyr_crisis value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				SHL_glp_counterpart = no
			}
		}
		remove_ideas = SHL_external_freight
	}
}

SHL_begin_campaign_convoy = {
	SHL_refresh_programme_prices = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_can_prepare_columns = yes
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_convoy_deposit compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			FROM = { SHL_obligation_site_ready = yes }
		}
		SHL_open_obligation = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 6 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_deposit value = SHL_convoy_deposit }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_convoy_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_convoy_deposit }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

# The convoy is consumed by one war only. Unused material can be demobilised.
SHL_use_campaign_reserve = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_campaign_reserve }
		clear_variable = SHL_campaign_reserve
		add_timed_idea = { idea = SHL_prepared_columns days = 90 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_forced_march_ready }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_forced_march_ready
		add_timed_idea = { idea = SHL_forced_columns days = 45 }
	}
}

SHL_demobilise_convoy = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_campaign_reserve }
		clear_variable = SHL_campaign_reserve
		clr_country_flag = SHL_forced_march_ready
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 13500
			producer = SHL
		}
	}
}

SHL_begin_kyz_mobilization = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_march_on_keizan = yes }
		set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_mobilizing
		SHL_prepare_kyz_partner = yes
		KYZ = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.111 } }
	}
}

SHL_finish_kyz_mobilization = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_mobilizing }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_mobilizing
		if = {
			limit = {
				SHL_can_march_on_keizan = yes
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 5 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_keizan_deadline }
				remove_mission = SHL_keizan_deadline
			}
			set_country_flag = SHL_kyz_offensive
			SHL_start_kyz_war = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = 50
			if = {
				limit = { SHL_kyz_diplomacy_open = no }
				SHL_clear_kyz_diplomacy = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

SHL_clean_campaign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = KYZ }
			NOT = { has_war_with = GLP }
			NOT = { has_war_with = MZR }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 6 compare = equals } }
			SHL_cancel_obligation = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = SHL_forced_march_ready
		remove_ideas = SHL_prepared_columns
		remove_ideas = SHL_forced_columns
		clr_country_flag = SHL_campaign_assistance_used
		SHL_cancel_kyz_mobilization = yes
	}
}

SHL_cancel_kyz_mobilization = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_mobilizing }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_kyz_mobilizing
		add_political_power = 50
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_kyz_diplomacy_open = no }
			SHL_clear_kyz_diplomacy = yes
		}
	}
}

SHL_take_house_campaign_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_house_campaign_aid = yes }
		add_political_power = -25
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 400 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_debt_added value = 400 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_debt value = 500 }
		ADISCORD_economy_reprice_debt = yes
		set_country_flag = SHL_campaign_assistance_used
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

SHL_take_city_campaign_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_city_campaign_aid = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 15 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 150 }
		add_manpower = -6000
		SHL_raise_paid_levies_2 = yes
		set_country_flag = SHL_campaign_assistance_used
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

SHL_take_directorate_campaign_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_directorate_campaign_aid = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_stock value = 2 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 8 }
		set_country_flag = SHL_forced_march_ready
		set_country_flag = SHL_campaign_assistance_used
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

SHL_take_regent_campaign_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_regent_campaign_aid = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_liability value = 180 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 15 }
		add_manpower = -6000
		SHL_raise_paid_levies_2 = yes
		set_country_flag = SHL_campaign_assistance_used
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

SHL_take_commune_campaign_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_commune_campaign_aid = yes }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -2700 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0 amount = -16 }
		add_manpower = -3000
		SHL_ensure_levy_template = yes
		699 = { SHL_create_levy = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 12 }
		set_country_flag = SHL_campaign_assistance_used
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

SHL_restore_cistern_regions = {
	KYZ = { transfer_state = 280 }
	280 = { set_state_controller_to = KYZ }
	KYZ = { transfer_state = 284 }
	284 = { set_state_controller_to = KYZ }
}

SHL_take_cistern_concession = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_can_settle_cisterns = yes
			OR = {
				SHL_house_mandate_current = yes
				SHL_regent_mandate_current = yes
			}
		}
		SHL_restore_cistern_regions = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_outcome value = 2 }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cistern_charter_pending
		set_variable = { var = SHL_concession_balance value = 450 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 4 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_concession_payment value = SHL_obligation_liability }
			clamp_variable = { var = SHL_concession_payment min = 0 max = 450 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_liability value = SHL_concession_payment }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_concession_balance value = SHL_concession_payment }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_liability value = 0 compare = less_than_or_equals } }
				SHL_clear_obligation = yes
			}
			clear_variable = SHL_concession_payment
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_concession_balance }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_concession_balance }
		clear_variable = SHL_concession_balance
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_request_cistern_charter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_can_offer_cistern_charter = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_cistern_charter_pending }
		}
		set_country_flag = SHL_cistern_charter_pending
		KYZ = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.112 } }
	}
}

# KYZ scope after its own answer. A changed course closes the offer without land transfer.
SHL_accept_cistern_charter = {
	SHL = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = SHL_cistern_charter_pending
				SHL_can_offer_cistern_charter = yes
			}
			SHL_restore_cistern_regions = yes
			# The pre-war charter also closes the ration demand and its deadline.
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_outcome value = 1 compare = equals } } }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 3 }
				if = {
					limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_keizan_deadline }
					remove_mission = SHL_keizan_deadline
				}
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_outcome value = 3 }
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_faction = no }
				create_faction_from_template = {
					template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
					name = SHL_southern_compact_faction
				}
			}
			add_to_faction = KYZ
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.113 }
		}
		else = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.114 } }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cistern_charter_pending
	}
}

SHL_record_south_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { state = 280 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 280 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 284 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 284 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 707 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 707 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 287 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 287 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 288 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 288 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 289 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 289 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 290 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 290 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 291 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 291 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 292 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 292 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 293 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 293 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 294 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 294 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 295 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 295 } }
	}
}

SHL_begin_southern_programme = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_south_country_ready = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_south_deposit }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 200 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			FROM = { SHL_south_site_has_result = yes }
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_manpower < 3000 }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 2700 } }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0 < 16 } }
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = { SHL_record_south_site = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_south_course value = SHL_political_course }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_south_deposit value = 200 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 200 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 200 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
			add_manpower = -3000
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -2700 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0 amount = -16 }
		}
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_clear_south_receipt = {
	clear_variable = SHL_south_deposit
	clear_variable = SHL_south_site
	clear_variable = SHL_south_course
}

SHL_cancel_southern_programme = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_south_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_south_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_south_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
			add_manpower = 3000
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 2700 producer = SHL }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0 amount = 16 producer = SHL }
		}
		SHL_clear_south_receipt = yes
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

# STATE scope, reached only for the saved receipt's target.
SHL_deliver_southern_programme = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_south_site_has_result = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_course value = 1 compare = equals } } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = industrial_complex
						size = 1
						include_locked = no
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes }
			}
			else = { owner = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 2700 producer = SHL } } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_course value = 2 compare = equals } } }
			if = {
				limit = { infrastructure < 5 }
				add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
			}
			else = { owner = { add_manpower = 2000 } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_owned_by = SHL }
				add_core_of = SHL
			}
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 10 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_course value = 3 compare = equals } } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = arms_factory
						size = 1
						include_locked = no
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
			}
			else = { owner = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0 amount = 32 producer = SHL } } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_course value = 4 compare = equals } } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 2 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 30 }
			clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear min = 0 max = 100 }
			SHL = { add_ideas = SHL_restored_house_guard }
			SHL = {
				set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 30 }
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 10 }
			}
			SHL_apply_house_loyalty = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_course value = 5 compare = equals } } }
			if = {
				limit = { infrastructure < 5 }
				add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
			}
			owner = {
				SHL_ensure_levy_template = yes
				PREV = { SHL_create_levy = yes }
			}
		}
		SHL = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_southern_programme value = SHL_south_course }
			SHL_clear_south_receipt = yes
			SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
			SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		}
	}
}

SHL_finish_southern_programme = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = SHL_south_deposit
			SHL_south_country_ready = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_south_course value = SHL_political_course compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 280 compare = equals } }
			280 = { SHL_deliver_southern_programme = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 284 compare = equals } }
			284 = { SHL_deliver_southern_programme = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 707 compare = equals } }
			707 = { SHL_deliver_southern_programme = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 287 compare = equals } }
			287 = { SHL_deliver_southern_programme = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 288 compare = equals } }
			288 = { SHL_deliver_southern_programme = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 289 compare = equals } }
			289 = { SHL_deliver_southern_programme = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 290 compare = equals } }
			290 = { SHL_deliver_southern_programme = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 291 compare = equals } }
			291 = { SHL_deliver_southern_programme = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 292 compare = equals } }
			292 = { SHL_deliver_southern_programme = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 293 compare = equals } }
			293 = { SHL_deliver_southern_programme = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 294 compare = equals } }
			294 = { SHL_deliver_southern_programme = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_south_site value = 295 compare = equals } }
			295 = { SHL_deliver_southern_programme = yes }
		}
	}
	SHL_cancel_southern_programme = yes
}

# A foreign war postpones the demand; loss of its political or territorial basis
# ends it. Both head wells gone (a won qanat war) or the brotherhood count as loss.
SHL_close_lost_keizan_threat = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 3 compare = equals } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_war_kyz value = 2 compare = equals } }
			NOT = { has_war_with = KYZ }
			OR = {
				KYZ = { exists = no }
				KYZ = { is_subject = yes }
				KYZ = { is_in_faction_with = SHL }
				AND = {
					NOT = { 280 = { is_owned_by = KYZ } }
					NOT = { 284 = { is_owned_by = KYZ } }
				}
				NOT = { 707 = { is_owned_by = SHL } }
				has_country_flag = SHL_kyz_brotherhood
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_keizan_state value = 3 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_kyz_outcome value = 7 }
		SHL_clear_kyz_diplomacy = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_keizan_deadline }
			remove_mission = SHL_keizan_deadline
		}
		SHL_cancel_kyz_mobilization = yes
		SHL_clean_campaign = yes
	}
}

# Cabinet replacement follows the ratified course; other departments keep their staff.
SHL_form_course_cabinet = {
	remove_ideas = minister_SHL_Amin_Rashad
	remove_ideas = minister_SHL_Mariam_Arbin
	remove_ideas = minister_SHL_Rauf_Darban
	remove_ideas = minister_SHL_Darius_Safir
	remove_ideas = minister_SHL_Salima_Darzi
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 2 compare = equals } }
		add_ideas = minister_SHL_Mariam_Arbin
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 3 compare = equals } }
		add_ideas = minister_SHL_Rauf_Darban
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 4 compare = equals } }
		add_ideas = minister_SHL_Darius_Safir
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
		add_ideas = minister_SHL_Salima_Darzi
	}
	else = { add_ideas = minister_SHL_Amin_Rashad }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.123 }
}

SHL_recognise_quarter_registers = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			has_idea = SHL_unregistered_quarters
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 80 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 80 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 80 }
		remove_ideas = SHL_unregistered_quarters
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 8 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -5 }
		SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.05 }
		SHL_balance_shift = yes
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Attach the focus aggregate on first use, then recalculate it.
SHL_refresh_conclave_power = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_conclave_power_dynamic } } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_conclave_power_dynamic }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# ----- War for the South -----

# COUNTRY SHL. Focuses of SHL_south_war_focus add to the SHL_sw_* variables.
SHL_refresh_south_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_south_war_dynamic } } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_south_war_dynamic }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# COUNTRY SHL, on every terminal path of the war for the South. The houses
# collect the war bonds once. A repelled campaign keeps the war results for
# the next campaign over the same completed tree; a settled South ends them.
SHL_sw_close_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_sw_war_bonds_debt }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_sw_war_bonds_debt
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 200 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 200 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_final_resolved }
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_south_war_dynamic } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_south_war_dynamic }
		}
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_war_support
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_stability
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_political_power
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_army_attack
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_army_defence
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_army_org
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_army_morale
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_breakthrough
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_conscription
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_reinforce
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_supply_consumption
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_out_of_supply
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_factory_output
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_arms_speed
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_max_efficiency
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_consumer_goods
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_resistance
		clear_variable = SHL_sw_compliance
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Two levy divisions in a controlled core state, Khazar first.
SHL_sw_raise_quarter_levy = {
	SHL_ensure_levy_template = yes
	random_owned_controlled_state = {
		limit = { is_core_of = SHL }
		prioritize = { 699 }
		SHL_create_levy = yes
		SHL_create_levy = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. A random incident at most once per draw; the cooldown and the
# open-crisis flag keep incidents from piling up.
SHL_crisis_draw = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_crisis_cooldown }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_crisis_open }
		}
		random_list = {
			92 = { }
			8 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					has_completed_focus = SHL_incident_office
				}
				set_country_flag = SHL_crisis_open
				set_country_flag = { flag = SHL_crisis_cooldown value = 1 days = 120 }
				# An open ledger removes the two corruption incidents from the draw.
				random_list = {
					20 = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_crisis.1 hours = 1 } }
					20 = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_crisis.2 hours = 1 } }
					20 = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_crisis.3 hours = 1 } }
					20 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							has_completed_focus = SHL_c_open_ledger
						}
						country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_crisis.4 hours = 1 }
					}
					20 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							has_completed_focus = SHL_c_open_ledger
						}
						country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_crisis.5 hours = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# ----- Life of the furnaces -----
# Ambient vignettes for a human player. Drawn once per 28-day cycle from
# SHL_run_cycle; a fired event sets SHL_life_cooldown in its immediate block
# and closes the channel for 120 days. Each branch is weighted out by the local
# condition it describes; the sandstorm has none, so the list never empties.
SHL_life_draw = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = no
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_life_cooldown }
		}
		random_list = {
			85 = { }
			15 = {
				random_list = {
					10 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = {
								293 = {
									SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
									check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
								}
							}
						}
						country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_life.20 hours = 2 }
					}
					10 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = { 291 = { is_owned_by = SHL } }
						}
						country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_life.21 hours = 2 }
					}
					10 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							check_variable = { var = SHL_house_pressure value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						}
						country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_life.22 hours = 2 }
					}
					10 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 compare = less_than }
						}
						country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_life.23 hours = 2 }
					}
					10 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							OR = {
								NOT = { KYZ = { exists = yes } }
								check_variable = { var = SHL_water_crisis value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							}
						}
						country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_life.24 hours = 2 }
					}
					10 = {
						country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_life.25 hours = 2 }
					}
					10 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							check_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						}
						country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_life.26 hours = 2 }
					}
					10 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							check_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 1 compare = less_than }
						}
						country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_life.27 hours = 2 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# ----- Furnace recipients -----
# 0 export, 1 arsenal, 2 quarters, 3 crest. Ownership and voting rights are separate.
SHL_refresh_delivery_terms = {
	SHL_refresh_programme_prices = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_standpipe_relief value = 3 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_strike_active }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_standpipe_relief value = 8 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = SHL_political_course }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 0 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_new_house }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_open_conclave }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_nur_directorate }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 3 }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_civic_price value = 120 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_arsenal_price value = 120 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pledge_price value = 180 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_rifles value = 2700 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_quarters value = 4 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_crest value = 4 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 1 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 4 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_crest value = 6 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 5 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_quarters value = 6 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_civic_price value = 90 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 3 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_arsenal_price value = 90 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 21 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 2 compare = equals }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_civic_price value = 60 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 22 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 2 compare = equals }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_arsenal_price value = 90 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 31 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 3 compare = equals }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_civic_price value = 90 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 32 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 3 compare = equals }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_arsenal_price value = 60 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 41 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 4 compare = equals }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_arsenal_price value = 90 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 42 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 4 compare = equals }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pledge_price value = 165 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 51 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_course value = 5 compare = equals }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_quarters value = 8 }
	}
	# Course programmes lower the order prices after the ending discounts.
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_c_civic_orders_plus }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_civic_price value = 30 }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_civic_price min = 30 max = 120 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_d_arsenal_orders_plus }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_arsenal_price value = 30 }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_arsenal_price min = 30 max = 120 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_r_pledge_plus }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pledge_price value = 150 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 1 compare = equals } }
		multiply_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_rifles value = 2 }
		multiply_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_quarters value = 1.5 }
		multiply_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_crest value = 1.5 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 2 compare = equals } }
		divide_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_rifles value = 2 }
		divide_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_quarters value = 2 }
		divide_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_crest value = 2 }
	}
	# A crest delivery from one district goes to its own house at three times
	# the national figure.
	set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_loyalty value = SHL_delivery_crest }
	multiply_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_loyalty value = 3 }
}

# STATE scope; called after consuming stock, only if no paid order owns the heat.
SHL_deliver_site_heat = {
	SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_recipient value = PREV.SHL_furnace_recipient } }
	SHL = { SHL_deliver_heat = yes }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 2 compare = equals }
			SHL_has_standpipe = yes
		}
		SHL = { subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = SHL_standpipe_relief } }
	}
	SHL_apply_site_loyalty_rate = yes
	# A leased furnace keeps only the remainder of its cash; SHL_deliver_heat
	# has no goods for recipient 4.
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_seal_site_leased = yes }
		SHL_seal_settle_lease_site = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_period_cash value = 0 }
	}
}

# The tenth furnace shares district 294 and its lease; it has no marker of its own.
SHL_deliver_tenth_heat = {
	if = {
		limit = { 294 = { SHL_seal_site_leased = yes } }
		SHL_seal_settle_lease_tenth = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_recipient value = SHL_tenth_recipient }
		SHL_deliver_heat = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_recipient value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_period_income value = 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_recipient value = 3 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_crest_delivered value = 1 }
		}
		# The tenth furnace has no house of its own: its crest goes to all of them.
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_recipient value = 3 compare = equals }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = SHL_delivery_crest }
			SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
		}
	}
}

# Country scope; shared output rates also drive the forecast, never the stocks.
SHL_deliver_heat = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_recipient value = 1 compare = equals } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = SHL_delivery_rifles
			producer = SHL
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_recipient value = 2 compare = equals } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = SHL_delivery_quarters }
	}
}

# Loyal votes: seated lit houses outside opposition at loyalty 55 or more,
# and the tenth house after its crest delivery in the house course.
SHL_refresh_delivery_support = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_crest_support value = 0 }
	287 = {
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_house_loyal_vote = yes }
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_crest_support value = 1 } }
		}
	}
	288 = {
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_house_loyal_vote = yes }
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_crest_support value = 1 } }
		}
	}
	289 = {
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_house_loyal_vote = yes }
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_crest_support value = 1 } }
		}
	}
	290 = {
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_house_loyal_vote = yes }
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_crest_support value = 1 } }
		}
	}
	291 = {
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_house_loyal_vote = yes }
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_crest_support value = 1 } }
		}
	}
	292 = {
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_house_loyal_vote = yes }
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_crest_support value = 1 } }
		}
	}
	293 = {
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_house_loyal_vote = yes }
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_crest_support value = 1 } }
		}
	}
	294 = {
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_house_loyal_vote = yes }
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_crest_support value = 1 } }
		}
	}
	295 = {
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_house_loyal_vote = yes }
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_crest_support value = 1 } }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 4 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_running value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_crest_delivered value = 1 compare = equals }
			294 = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_crest_support value = 1 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_crest_support_twice value = SHL_crest_support }
	multiply_variable = { var = SHL_crest_support_twice value = 2 }
}


# Temporary recipient input from a GUI button; the effect rechecks the real site.
SHL_assign_selected_delivery = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_can_assign_delivery = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_assignment value = 0 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_assignment value = 3 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = SHL_assignment value = 3 compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 1 compare = equals } }
			287 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = PREV.SHL_assignment } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 2 compare = equals } }
			288 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = PREV.SHL_assignment } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 3 compare = equals } }
			289 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = PREV.SHL_assignment } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 4 compare = equals } }
			290 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = PREV.SHL_assignment } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 5 compare = equals } }
			291 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = PREV.SHL_assignment } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 6 compare = equals } }
			292 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = PREV.SHL_assignment } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 7 compare = equals } }
			293 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = PREV.SHL_assignment } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 8 compare = equals } }
			294 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = PREV.SHL_assignment } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 9 compare = equals } }
			295 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = PREV.SHL_assignment } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_selected_furnace value = 10 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_recipient value = SHL_assignment }
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# AI reserves a full supply cycle and three repairs before diverting up to three furnaces.
# Reuse the production pulse; no daily country or state scan.
SHL_ai_allocate_deliveries = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_ai_delivery_reserve value = SHL_auto_packet_price }
		multiply_variable = { var = SHL_ai_delivery_reserve value = 10 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 1 compare = equals } }
			multiply_variable = { var = SHL_ai_delivery_reserve value = 2 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_ai_delivery_reserve value = 240 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_ai_quarters_left value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_ai_crests_left value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_ai_arsenal_left value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_ai_delivery_reserve compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 35 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_ai_quarters_left value = 1 }
			}
			# One crest for a restless hall or a walkout, two when both press.
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_house_pressure value = 35 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						check_variable = { var = SHL_walkout_site value = 0 compare = greater_than }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { var = SHL_ai_crests_left value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_house_pressure value = 35 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						check_variable = { var = SHL_walkout_site value = 0 compare = greater_than }
					}
					set_variable = { var = SHL_ai_crests_left value = 2 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_war = yes }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_ai_arsenal_left value = 1 }
			}
		}
		287 = { SHL_ai_allocate_site = yes }
		288 = { SHL_ai_allocate_site = yes }
		289 = { SHL_ai_allocate_site = yes }
		290 = { SHL_ai_allocate_site = yes }
		291 = { SHL_ai_allocate_site = yes }
		292 = { SHL_ai_allocate_site = yes }
		293 = { SHL_ai_allocate_site = yes }
		294 = { SHL_ai_allocate_site = yes }
		295 = { SHL_ai_allocate_site = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_recipient value = 0 }
	}
}

SHL_ai_allocate_site = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_obligation_site_ready = yes
			NOT = { SHL_obligation_here = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_ai_quarters_left value = 0 compare = greater_than } } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 2 }
			SHL = { subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_ai_quarters_left value = 1 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_ai_crests_left value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_house_loyalty value = 55 compare = less_than }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 3 }
			SHL = { subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_ai_crests_left value = 1 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_ai_arsenal_left value = 0 compare = greater_than } } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 1 }
			SHL = { subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_ai_arsenal_left value = 1 } }
		}
	}
}

# AI only, once per cycle after the pre-ratification drift. The quarters gain
# every cycle while fewer than seven house furnaces vote, so a preliminary
# course whose ratification needs another range never opens without player
# levers. Once the course's focus prerequisites are complete, the balance moves
# toward the inner part of the required range; the margin keeps the four-week
# ratification focus valid against the next cycle's drift. The city course also
# needs its civic mandate.
SHL_ai_steer_ratification = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 0 compare = equals }
			has_completed_focus = SHL_workers_charter
			has_power_balance = { id = SHL_conclave_balance }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_common_dispatch }
			if = {
				limit = { power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value > 0.20 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.10 }
				SHL_balance_shift = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value > 0.05 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.05 }
				SHL_balance_shift = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value < -0.20 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.10 }
				SHL_balance_shift = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value < -0.05 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.05 }
				SHL_balance_shift = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_house_compromise }
			if = {
				limit = { power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value > 0 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.10 }
				SHL_balance_shift = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value > -0.20 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.05 }
				SHL_balance_shift = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_city_charters }
			if = {
				limit = { power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value < 0 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.10 }
				SHL_balance_shift = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { power_balance_value = { id = SHL_conclave_balance value < 0.20 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.05 }
				SHL_balance_shift = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 35 compare = less_than } }
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 5 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Preview uses a separate cash budget in production order, including planned supply.
# It never writes production stocks, paid receipts, equipment or political scales.
SHL_refresh_delivery_forecast = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_for_plan value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_ai = no
				check_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_regular_supply value = 1 compare = equals }
			SHL_reserve_has_packet = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_for_plan value = SHL_reserve_stock }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve value = SHL_reserve_for_plan }
	SHL_apply_reserve_floor = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_allowance value = SHL_order_reserve }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_projection value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_auto_plan_left value = SHL_order_reserve }
	SHL_project_furnace_deliveries = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_bulk_reserve_total value = SHL_projected_transfers }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_total value = SHL_projected_auto_transfers }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_bulk_reserve_1 value = SHL_planned_transfer_1 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_bulk_reserve_2 value = SHL_planned_transfer_2 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_bulk_reserve_3 value = SHL_planned_transfer_3 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_bulk_reserve_4 value = SHL_planned_transfer_4 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_bulk_reserve_5 value = SHL_planned_transfer_5 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_bulk_reserve_6 value = SHL_planned_transfer_6 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_bulk_reserve_7 value = SHL_planned_transfer_7 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_bulk_reserve_8 value = SHL_planned_transfer_8 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_bulk_reserve_9 value = SHL_planned_transfer_9 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_bulk_reserve_10 value = SHL_planned_transfer_10 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_1 value = SHL_planned_auto_transfer_1 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_2 value = SHL_planned_auto_transfer_2 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_3 value = SHL_planned_auto_transfer_3 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_4 value = SHL_planned_auto_transfer_4 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_5 value = SHL_planned_auto_transfer_5 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_6 value = SHL_planned_auto_transfer_6 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_7 value = SHL_planned_auto_transfer_7 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_8 value = SHL_planned_auto_transfer_8 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_9 value = SHL_planned_auto_transfer_9 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_10 value = SHL_planned_auto_transfer_10 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_total value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			SHL_has_reserve_priorities = yes
		}
		SHL_plan_priority_deliveries = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_total value = SHL_projected_transfers }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_1 value = SHL_planned_transfer_1 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_2 value = SHL_planned_transfer_2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_3 value = SHL_planned_transfer_3 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_4 value = SHL_planned_transfer_4 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_5 value = SHL_planned_transfer_5 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_6 value = SHL_planned_transfer_6 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_7 value = SHL_planned_transfer_7 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_8 value = SHL_planned_transfer_8 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_9 value = SHL_planned_transfer_9 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_10 value = SHL_planned_transfer_10 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve value = 1 compare = equals } }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve_total value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_for_plan value = 0 }
			SHL_project_furnace_deliveries = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		# Reprice the pour when the active policy delivers fewer packets than a
		# manual distribution. Earlier fires can change the budget for later ones.
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_for_plan value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_auto_reserve value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_for_plan value = SHL_auto_reserve_total }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_bulk_reserve_total value = SHL_reserve_for_plan compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_auto_plan_left value = 0 }
			SHL_project_furnace_deliveries = yes
		}
	}
	SHL_preview_cycle_settlement = yes
	clear_variable = SHL_preview_lease_transfer
}

# Keep production and cash purchases in I-X order. First offer the reserve only
# to marked furnaces. Their remaining shortages are the limits for later trials.
SHL_plan_priority_deliveries = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve value = SHL_reserve_stock }
	SHL_apply_reserve_floor = yes
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_budget value = SHL_order_reserve }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_projection value = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_furnace value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_last_marked_order value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_open_ordinary_tail value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_auto_plan_left value = 0 }
	SHL_reprice_priority_deliveries = yes
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_bound_1 value = SHL_rejected_1 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_bound_2 value = SHL_rejected_2 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_bound_3 value = SHL_rejected_3 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_bound_4 value = SHL_rejected_4 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_bound_5 value = SHL_rejected_5 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_bound_6 value = SHL_rejected_6 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_bound_7 value = SHL_rejected_7 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_bound_8 value = SHL_rejected_8 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_bound_9 value = SHL_rejected_9 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_bound_10 value = SHL_rejected_10 }
	# A later trial cannot change what an earlier furnace bought or received.
	# Keep accepted earlier grants and admit one ordinary order at a time.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_for_plan value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_marked value = SHL_priority_1 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_missing value = SHL_rejected_1 }
		SHL_try_ordinary_reserve_order = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_marked value = SHL_priority_2 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_missing value = SHL_rejected_2 }
		SHL_try_ordinary_reserve_order = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_marked value = SHL_priority_3 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_missing value = SHL_rejected_3 }
		SHL_try_ordinary_reserve_order = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_marked value = SHL_priority_4 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_missing value = SHL_rejected_4 }
		SHL_try_ordinary_reserve_order = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_marked value = SHL_priority_5 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_missing value = SHL_rejected_5 }
		SHL_try_ordinary_reserve_order = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_marked value = SHL_priority_6 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_missing value = SHL_rejected_6 }
		SHL_try_ordinary_reserve_order = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_marked value = SHL_priority_7 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_missing value = SHL_rejected_7 }
		SHL_try_ordinary_reserve_order = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_marked value = SHL_priority_8 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_missing value = SHL_rejected_8 }
		SHL_try_ordinary_reserve_order = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_marked value = SHL_priority_9 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_missing value = SHL_rejected_9 }
		SHL_try_ordinary_reserve_order = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_marked value = SHL_priority_10 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_missing value = SHL_rejected_10 }
		SHL_try_ordinary_reserve_order = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_projection value = 0 }
}

SHL_reprice_priority_deliveries = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_for_plan value = SHL_priority_budget }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_regressed value = 0 }
	SHL_project_furnace_deliveries = yes
}

SHL_try_ordinary_reserve_order = {
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_furnace value = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_trial_marked value = 0 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_open_ordinary_tail value = 0 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_trial_missing value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_for_plan value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = SHL_census_of_nine
				check_variable = { var = SHL_trial_furnace value = 10 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		# Once the last marked order has cleared, later purchases cannot affect it.
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_trial_furnace value = SHL_last_marked_order compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_open_ordinary_tail value = 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_grant value = SHL_trial_missing }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_grant max = SHL_reserve_for_plan }
		SHL_reprice_priority_deliveries = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_priority_regressed value = 1 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_trial_grant value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_grant value = 1 }
			SHL_reprice_priority_deliveries = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_priority_regressed value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_trial_grant value = 0 }
			SHL_reprice_priority_deliveries = yes
		}
	}
}

# The current order may draw the uncommitted surplus. Later ordinary orders
# have no allowance until their own turn in the planner.
SHL_limit_ordinary_trial = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_furnace_number value = SHL_trial_furnace compare = less_than } }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve max = SHL_previous_transfer }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_open_ordinary_tail value = 0 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_furnace_number value = SHL_trial_furnace compare = equals } }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve max = SHL_trial_grant }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve value = 0 } }
	}
}

SHL_project_furnace_deliveries = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_projected_transfers value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_projected_auto_transfers value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_treasury value = ADISCORD_economy_treasury }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_tenth_stock value = SHL_tenth_stock }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_reserve value = SHL_reserve_stock }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_budget value = ADISCORD_economy_treasury }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_income value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_autobuy_packets value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_autobuy_cost value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_unfunded_packets value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_missed_pours value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_next_unfunded_furnace value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_furnace_number value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_order_work value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_order_lost value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_pledge_paid value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lit_sites value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_relief_restartable value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_relief_ready value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lease_transfer value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_release_leases value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_seal_release_due = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_release_leases value = 1 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_budget value = SHL_seal_petition_deposit }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 5 compare = equals }
			var:SHL_obligation_recipient = { SHL_relief_site_ready = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_relief_ready value = 1 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_rifles value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_quarters value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_crests value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_loyalty_sum value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cost value = 1 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cash value = 60 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cost value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cash value = 90 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_shift_policy value = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cash value = 25 }
	}
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cash value = SHL_income_deduction }
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cash min = 0 max = 90 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_export_cash value = SHL_preview_cash }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_export_mode value = 1 compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_export_cash value = SHL_exchange_price }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_export_cash value = 60 }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_export_cash min = 0 max = 90 }
	}
	287 = {
		SHL = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = PREV.SHL_furnace_recipient }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_stock value = PREV.SHL_furnace_stock }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_owner value = PREV.SHL_furnace_owner }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_stock_1 value = SHL_preview_stock }
			SHL_read_district_pour_status = yes
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 287 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_priority_projection value = 1 compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_site_marked value = SHL_priority_1 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_trial_furnace value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_site_bound value = SHL_priority_bound_1 }
					set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_previous_transfer value = SHL_planned_transfer_1 }
				}
			}
			SHL_preview_delivery = yes
			set_variable = { var = SHL_recipient_1 value = SHL_preview_recipient }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_block_1 value = SHL_preview_block }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_planned_transfer_1 value = SHL_planned_site_transfer }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_rejected_1 value = SHL_rejected_packets }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_planned_auto_transfer_1 value = SHL_auto_site_transfer }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_order_packets_1 value = SHL_preview_packets }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_order_shortfall_1 value = SHL_preview_shortfall }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_cash_1 value = SHL_preview_site_cash }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_rifles_1 value = SHL_preview_site_rifles }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_quarters_1 value = SHL_preview_site_quarters }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_crests_1 value = SHL_preview_site_crests }
		}
	}
	288 = {
		SHL = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = PREV.SHL_furnace_recipient }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_stock value = PREV.SHL_furnace_stock }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_owner value = PREV.SHL_furnace_owner }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_stock_2 value = SHL_preview_stock }
			SHL_read_district_pour_status = yes
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 288 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_priority_projection value = 1 compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_site_marked value = SHL_priority_2 }
				if = {

	295 = {
		SHL = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = PREV.SHL_furnace_recipient }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_stock value = PREV.SHL_furnace_stock }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_owner value = PREV.SHL_furnace_owner }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_stock_9 value = SHL_preview_stock }
			SHL_read_district_pour_status = yes
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 295 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_priority_projection value = 1 compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_site_marked value = SHL_priority_9 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_trial_furnace value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_site_bound value = SHL_priority_bound_9 }
					set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_previous_transfer value = SHL_planned_transfer_9 }
				}
			}
			SHL_preview_delivery = yes
			set_variable = { var = SHL_recipient_9 value = SHL_preview_recipient }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_block_9 value = SHL_preview_block }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_planned_transfer_9 value = SHL_planned_site_transfer }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_rejected_9 value = SHL_rejected_packets }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_planned_auto_transfer_9 value = SHL_auto_site_transfer }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_order_packets_9 value = SHL_preview_packets }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_order_shortfall_9 value = SHL_preview_shortfall }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_cash_9 value = SHL_preview_site_cash }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_rifles_9 value = SHL_preview_site_rifles }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_quarters_9 value = SHL_preview_site_quarters }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_crests_9 value = SHL_preview_site_crests }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_recipient_10 value = SHL_tenth_recipient }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = SHL_tenth_recipient }
	if = {
		limit = {
			294 = { SHL_seal_site_leased = yes }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_release_leases value = 0 compare = equals }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_recipient_10 value = 4 }
		# A supplied final service pour at VIII redeems the lease before X delivers.
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_block_8 value = 0 compare = equals } }
					294 = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_seal_service_cycles value = 1 compare = equals } } }
					294 = { has_state_flag = SHL_seal_cold_shift }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = 4 }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_stock value = SHL_tenth_stock }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_status value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_project_stage value = 4 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_status value = SHL_tenth_running }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { 294 = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes } } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_status value = 2 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_priority_projection value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_site_marked value = SHL_priority_10 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_trial_furnace value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_site_bound value = SHL_priority_bound_10 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_previous_transfer value = SHL_planned_transfer_10 }
		}
	}
	SHL_preview_delivery = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_block_10 value = SHL_preview_block }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_planned_transfer_10 value = SHL_planned_site_transfer }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_rejected_10 value = SHL_rejected_packets }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_planned_auto_transfer_10 value = SHL_auto_site_transfer }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_order_packets_10 value = SHL_preview_packets }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_order_shortfall_10 value = SHL_preview_shortfall }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_cash_10 value = SHL_preview_site_cash }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_rifles_10 value = SHL_preview_site_rifles }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_quarters_10 value = SHL_preview_site_quarters }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_delivery_crests_10 value = SHL_preview_site_crests }
	# The totals line shows the expected change of the pressure: the mean of the
	# site changes over the counted houses plus the tenth furnace's crest.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_houses value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
		}
		divide_variable = { var = SHL_preview_loyalty_sum value = SHL_loyalty_houses }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_crests value = SHL_preview_loyalty_sum }
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_preview_loyalty_sum
	clear_variable = SHL_preview_owner
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_unrest_delta value = 0 }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_preview_unrest_delta value = SHL_preview_quarters }
}

# SHL scope, after all ten production previews. Only the projected ledger changes.
SHL_preview_cycle_settlement = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_order_income value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_contract_income value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_credit_net value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_indemnity value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_progress value = SHL_obligation_progress }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_progress value = SHL_preview_order_work }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_progress value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_order_income value = 120 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = SHL_obligation_extended }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_order_income value = 90 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 2 compare = equals }
				var:SHL_obligation_site = {
					NOT = {
						AND = {
							SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
							infrastructure < 5
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_order_income value = SHL_obligation_deposit }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 5 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_relief_restartable value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lit_sites value = 1 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_credit_cycles value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_credit_net value = 25 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_metal_clients value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		VAL = { SHL_preview_metal_receipt = yes }
		STP = { SHL_preview_metal_receipt = yes }
		STS = { SHL_preview_metal_receipt = yes }
		RUS = { SHL_preview_metal_receipt = yes }
		GLP = { SHL_preview_metal_receipt = yes }
		NAM = { SHL_preview_metal_receipt = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = SHL_indemnity_due
			SHL_indemnity_recipient_valid = yes
			SHL_indemnity_recipient_at_peace = yes
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_indemnity value = SHL_preview_lit_sites }
		multiply_variable = { var = SHL_preview_indemnity value = 8 }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_indemnity min = 0 max = SHL_indemnity_due }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = SHL_seal_chapter_open
			check_variable = { var = SHL_seal_bond_cycles value = 0 compare = greater_than }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_preview_credit_net value = 25 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_release_leases value = 1 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_order_income value = SHL_seal_petition_deposit }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cycle_net value = SHL_preview_income }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cycle_net value = SHL_preview_order_income }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cycle_net value = SHL_preview_contract_income }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cycle_net value = SHL_preview_credit_net }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cycle_net value = SHL_preview_indemnity }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cycle_net value = SHL_cycle_bonus_income }
	# An extinguished hereditary furnace becomes civic. The refresh following
	# the pour cancels an unfinished house order against the remaining treasury.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_order_lost value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_progress value = 2 compare = less_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_refund value = SHL_obligation_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_batch_spent value = SHL_preview_order_work }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_batch_spent value = SHL_obligation_batch_price }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_refund value = SHL_preview_batch_spent }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_liability value = SHL_obligation_liability }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_liability value = SHL_preview_pledge_paid }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_repayment value = ADISCORD_economy_treasury }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_repayment value = SHL_preview_cycle_net }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_repayment value = SHL_preview_refund }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_repayment min = 0 max = SHL_preview_liability }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_refund value = SHL_preview_repayment }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_order_income value = SHL_preview_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_cycle_net value = SHL_preview_refund }
	}
}

# Buyer scope. The namestnik receives any projected lease proceeds before paying
# the year's or the two-year metal tariff. The brokers' premium is paid by the market.
SHL_preview_metal_receipt = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_metal_client }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_min_treasury value = SHL_metal_cycle_price }
		if = {
			limit = { tag = NAM }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_min_treasury value = SHL.SHL_preview_lease_transfer }
		}
		clear_variable = SHL_forecast_contract_receipt
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_forecast_buyer_can_pay = yes }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_contract_receipt value = SHL_metal_cycle_price }
			SHL = {
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_contract_income value = PREV.SHL_forecast_contract_receipt }
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_metal_brokers }
					add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_contract_income value = 15 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY, PREV is a district. The indemnity counts fires still lit after wear and shortages.
SHL_preview_site_condition = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lit value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_end_wear value = PREV.SHL_furnace_wear }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_end_stock value = SHL_preview_stock }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_status value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lit value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_block value = 3 compare = equals } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_end_wear value = 12 }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lit value = 0 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			SHL_quote_site_wear = yes
			add_to_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_end_wear value = SHL_site_wear_quote }
			clear_variable = SHL_site_wear_quote
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_end_stock value = SHL_preview_cost }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = 4 compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_block value = 0 compare = equals }
					PREV = { has_state_flag = SHL_seal_cold_shift }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_end_wear value = 6 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_end_wear value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lit value = 0 }
		}
	}
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lit_sites value = SHL_preview_lit }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_status value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lit value = 0 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
			has_variable = SHL_obligation_kind
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 6 compare = equals } }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = SHL_obligation_site compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_order_lost value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 5 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = SHL_obligation_recipient compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_relief_ready value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				PREV = { SHL_furnace_site_idle = yes }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_owner value = 1 compare = equals }
					AND = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_status value = 1 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lit value = 0 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lit value = 0 compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_end_stock value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_relief_ready value = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_relief_restartable value = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lit value = 1 compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_end_wear value = 30 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_relief_ready value = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# The production-day quote can be requested between panel refreshes.
# Read the district's actual controller and fire before promising it supplies.
SHL_read_district_pour_status = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_status value = 2 }
	if = {
		limit = { PREV = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_status value = PREV.SHL_furnace_running }
	}
}

SHL_preview_delivery = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_rejected_packets value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_planned_site_transfer value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_auto_site_transfer value = 0 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_furnace_number value = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_packets value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_shortfall value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_block value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_cash value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_rifles value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_quarters value = 0 }
