	set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_crests value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 0 compare = greater_than }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = 4 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_release_leases value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = 4 compare = equals } }
				var:SHL_preview_site = { has_state_flag = SHL_quota_site }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = 1 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_status value = 1 compare = equals } } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_block value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_status value = 2 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_block value = 2 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_regular_supply value = 1 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_stock value = SHL_preview_cost compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_missing value = SHL_preview_cost }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_missing value = SHL_preview_stock }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_budget value = SHL_preview_budget }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve value = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_completed_focus = SHL_census_of_nine
						check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 0 compare = equals }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve value = SHL_reserve_for_plan }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_priority_projection value = 1 compare = equals } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_site_marked value = 0 compare = equals } }
					SHL_limit_ordinary_trial = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_trial_furnace value = 0 compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_last_marked_order value = SHL_forecast_furnace_number }
				}
			}
			SHL_quote_furnace_order = yes
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_for_plan value = SHL_order_reserve_packets }
			add_to_temp_variable = { var = SHL_projected_transfers value = SHL_order_reserve_packets }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = SHL_order_reserve_packets value = 0 compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_auto_plan_left value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
				# Both distributions honour the same orders until the first one
				# crosses the floor. Only whole packets fit in that final order.
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_auto_site_transfer value = SHL_order_reserve_packets }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_auto_plan_left value = SHL_order_reserve_packets compare = less_than } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_auto_site_transfer value = 1 }
				}
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_auto_plan_left value = SHL_auto_site_transfer }
				add_to_temp_variable = { var = SHL_projected_auto_transfers value = SHL_auto_site_transfer }
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_stock value = SHL_order_reserve_packets }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_planned_site_transfer value = SHL_order_reserve_packets }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_packets value = SHL_order_buy_packets }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_budget value = SHL_order_price compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_autobuy_packets value = SHL_preview_packets }
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_autobuy_cost value = SHL_order_price }
				subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_preview_budget value = SHL_order_price }
				subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_preview_income value = SHL_order_price }
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_stock value = SHL_preview_packets }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_packets value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_rejected_packets value = SHL_preview_packets }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_priority_projection value = 1 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = SHL_trial_furnace value = 0 compare = greater_than }
						check_variable = { var = SHL_site_marked value = 1 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = SHL_rejected_packets value = SHL_site_bound compare = greater_than }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_priority_regressed value = 1 }
				}
				# A refused order leaves cash available to the next district.
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_shortfall value = SHL_order_price }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_shortfall value = SHL_preview_budget }
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_unfunded_packets value = SHL_preview_packets }
				# Keep the first rejection for wraparound until reaching one beyond the selection.
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { var = SHL_next_unfunded_furnace value = 0 compare = equals }
							AND = {
								check_variable = { var = SHL_forecast_furnace_number value = SHL_selected_furnace compare = greater_than }
								check_variable = { var = SHL_next_unfunded_furnace value = SHL_selected_furnace compare = less_than_or_equals }
							}
						}
					}
					set_variable = { var = SHL_next_unfunded_furnace value = SHL_forecast_furnace_number }
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_pour_cash value = SHL_preview_cash }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 0 compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = 0 compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_pour_cash value = SHL_preview_export_cash }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_stock value = SHL_preview_cost compare = less_than } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_block value = 3 }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_missed_pours value = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = SHL_obligation_kind
				check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_site value = SHL_preview_site compare = equals }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_block value = 4 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 4 compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_cash value = SHL_preview_pour_cash }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_pledge_paid value = SHL_preview_pour_cash }
				clamp_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_pledge_paid min = 0 max = SHL_obligation_liability }
				subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_cash value = SHL_obligation_liability }
				clamp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_cash min = 0 max = 90 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_variable = SHL_obligation_overdue }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_progress value = 2 compare = less_than }
					OR = {
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 5 compare = equals } }
						check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_relief_ready value = 1 compare = equals }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_order_work value = 2 }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_order_work value = SHL_obligation_progress }
				clamp_temp_variable = { var = SHL_preview_order_work min = 0 max = SHL_preview_cost }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 3 compare = equals } }
					set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_rifles value = SHL_preview_order_work }
					multiply_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_rifles value = 6750 }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 6 compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_cash value = SHL_preview_pour_cash }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = 0 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_cash value = SHL_preview_pour_cash }
		}
		# The lease keeps the remainder after the namestnik's share; a furnace
		# serving its redemption keeps nothing (SHL_seal_settle_lease_site).
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = 4 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_cash value = SHL_preview_pour_cash }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lease_due value = SHL_preview_cash }
			multiply_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lease_due value = SHL_seal_lease_share }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 0 compare = equals }
							294 = { has_variable = SHL_seal_service_cycles }
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 0 compare = greater_than }
							var:SHL_preview_site = { has_variable = SHL_seal_service_cycles }
						}
					}
				}
				set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lease_due value = SHL_preview_cash }
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_cash value = SHL_preview_lease_due }
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_subject_of = NAM
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 0 compare = equals }
						var:SHL_preview_site = { NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_seal_cold_shift } }
					}
				}
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_lease_transfer value = SHL_preview_lease_due }
			}
			clear_variable = SHL_preview_lease_due
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_rifles value = SHL_delivery_rifles }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = 2 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_quarters value = SHL_delivery_quarters }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 0 compare = greater_than }
					var:SHL_preview_site = { SHL_has_standpipe = yes }
				}
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_quarters value = SHL_standpipe_relief }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_recipient value = 3 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 0 compare = equals }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_crests value = SHL_delivery_crest }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_income value = SHL_preview_site_cash }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_budget value = SHL_preview_site_cash }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_rifles value = SHL_preview_site_rifles }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_quarters value = SHL_preview_site_quarters }
		# A furnace site shows its house's loyalty change, the tenth its crest.
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_block value = 0 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_rate_recipient value = SHL_preview_recipient }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_rate_owner value = SHL_preview_owner }
			SHL_site_loyalty_rate = yes
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_crests value = SHL_rate_loyalty }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_loyalty_sum value = SHL_rate_loyalty }
			SHL_clear_loyalty_rate = yes
		}
		else = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_preview_crests value = SHL_preview_site_crests } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			OR = {
				has_variable = SHL_indemnity_due
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = SHL_obligation_site compare = equals }
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 5 compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = SHL_obligation_recipient compare = equals }
				}
			}
		}
		SHL_preview_site_condition = yes
	}
	# The certain penalties of SHL_produce_site: an idle house furnace and an
	# occupied district of Shahrabad.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_block value = 3 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_crests value = -3 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_preview_loyalty_sum value = 3 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_preview_block value = 2 compare = equals }
				var:SHL_preview_site = { is_owned_by = SHL }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_preview_site_crests value = -5 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_preview_loyalty_sum value = 5 }
		}
	}
}

SHL_reassign_commune_crests = {
	287 = {
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 2 }
		}
	}
	288 = {
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 2 }
		}
	}
	289 = {
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 2 }
		}
	}
	290 = {
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 2 }
		}
	}
	291 = {
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 2 }
		}
	}
	292 = {
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 2 }
		}
	}
	293 = {
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 2 }
		}
	}
	294 = {
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 2 }
		}
	}
	295 = {
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 2 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_recipient value = 3 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_recipient value = 2 }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Course programmes. Country scope SHL unless a comment says otherwise. Course
# focuses write the price variables; consumers read them at payment and check
# the same quote again at delivery.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# A focus does not count as completed inside its own completion_reward. A
# reward that changes a quote derived from has_completed_focus queues this
# refresh (ADISCORD_SHL.167) for the next hour, after the focus is recorded.
SHL_queue_focus_refresh = {
	country_event = {
		id = ADISCORD_SHL.167
		hours = 1
	}
}

# Programme prices are derived from completed course focuses on every refresh,
# so a focus reward never writes them. Consumers read the variables at payment
# and refund the recorded deposit.
SHL_refresh_programme_prices = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_guard_discount value = 0 }
	# The course discount replaces the contract discount instead of adding to it.
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_desert_guard_contracts }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_guard_discount value = 0.2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_h_conclave_mercenaries }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_guard_discount value = 0.25 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_guard_price value = 1 }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_guard_price value = SHL_guard_discount }
	multiply_variable = { var = SHL_guard_price value = 300 }
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_guard_price min = 0 max = 300 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_wells_price value = 250 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_c_water_works }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_wells_price value = 200 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_loran_cisterns }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_wells_price value = 50 }
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_wells_price min = 0 max = 250 }
	# The workers' wells are drilled once for free. The flag is set at payment,
	# or by the focus when it trained the qanat furnaces instead.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_completed_focus = SHL_k_workers_wells
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_free_well_used }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_wells_price value = 0 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_engineers_price value = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_engineers_of_the_tenth }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_engineers_price value = 75 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_harbour_price value = 300 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_c_harbour_works }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_harbour_price value = 250 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_export_advance value = 60 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_h_export_houses }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_export_advance value = 90 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_convoy_deposit value = 120 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_d_march_logistics }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_convoy_deposit value = 90 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_northern_road }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_convoy_deposit value = 30 }
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_convoy_deposit min = 30 max = 120 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_cauldron_repair value = 30 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_k_cauldron_plus }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_cauldron_repair value = 40 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_raid_oil_pp value = 40 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_k_strikes_on_fields }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_raid_oil_pp value = 0 }
	}
	SHL_refresh_reserve_price = yes
	SHL_refresh_packet_price = yes
}

# The common fund lowers the reserve quote from 90 to 75 and the fuel ledger
# takes 5 off either; the supplier premium of ADISCORD_SHL.8 adds 10 on top.
# SHL_purchase_reserve re-reads the quote before the debit so a focus completed
# mid-cycle applies at once.
SHL_refresh_reserve_price = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_price value = 90 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_common_fund }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_price value = 75 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_fuel_ledger }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_price value = 5 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_price_premium value = SHL_reserve_price }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_price_premium value = 10 }
}

# The embargo overrides the oil contract. National discounts apply to that
# quote before the handling fee for automatic orders.
SHL_refresh_packet_price = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_packet_price value = 40 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_oil_client }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_packet_price value = 35 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = NAM_fuel_embargo_modifier } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_packet_price value = 50 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_smuggler_fuel }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_packet_price value = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_own_distillery }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_packet_price value = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_completed_focus = SHL_baryat_mines
			291 = {
				is_owned_by = SHL
				is_controlled_by = SHL
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_packet_price value = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = SHL_nam_fuel_treaty
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = NAM_fuel_embargo_modifier } }
			NOT = { has_war_with = NAM }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_packet_price value = 10 }
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_packet_price min = 20 max = 50 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_packet_price value = SHL_packet_price }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_completed_focus = SHL_houses_railway } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_auto_packet_price value = 5 }
	}
}

# NAM calls this in the affected buyer's scope when an oil contract or embargo changes.
SHL_refresh_oil_supply_quote = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SHL
			exists = yes
			has_country_flag = SHL_furnaces_initialized
		}
		SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

SHL_waive_packet_fee = {
	SHL_refresh_delivery_terms = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_auto_packet_price value = SHL_packet_price }
	SHL_refresh_furnace_card = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
}

SHL_initialize_programmes = {
	add_to_array = { SHL_caravan_partners = VAL }
	add_to_array = { SHL_caravan_partners = STP }
	add_to_array = { SHL_caravan_partners = STS }
	add_to_array = { SHL_caravan_partners = RUS }
	add_to_array = { SHL_caravan_partners = GLP }
	add_to_array = { SHL_caravan_partners = NAM }
	SHL_refresh_programme_prices = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_metal_clients value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_metal_max_clients value = 3 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_bonus_income value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_copi_prospected value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_export_mode value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_exchange_price value = 60 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_credit_cycles value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_demand_cycle value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_revanche_neighbour value = 0 }
}

# ----- Metal exchange (houses) -----

# One quote per cycle for every furnace that sells its metal abroad.
SHL_roll_exchange_price = {
	random_list = {
		20 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_exchange_price value = 45 } }
		30 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_exchange_price value = 55 } }
		30 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_exchange_price value = 65 } }
		20 = { set_variable = { var = SHL_exchange_price value = 75 } }
	}
}

SHL_commit_metal_exchange = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_export_mode value = 1 }
	SHL_roll_exchange_price = yes
	SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
}

SHL_toggle_export_mode = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_export_mode value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_export_mode value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_exchange_price value = 60 }
		SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
	else = { SHL_commit_metal_exchange = yes }
}

# ----- Cargo insurance and house credit (houses) -----

SHL_begin_cargo_insurance = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_insurance_deposit }
			NOT = { has_idea = SHL_insured_cargo }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 100 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_insurance_deposit value = 100 }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_cancel_cargo_insurance = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_insurance_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_insurance_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_insurance_deposit }
		clear_variable = SHL_insurance_deposit
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_cargo_insurance = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_insurance_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_country_operable = yes }
			clear_variable = SHL_insurance_deposit
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = convoy_1
				amount = 5
				producer = SHL
			}
			add_timed_idea = { idea = SHL_insured_cargo days = 180 }
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_cargo_insurance = yes }
	}
}

# FROM is the borrowing country. The debt flag is read by the consolidation
# offers; the treasury recovers the loan over six cycles.
SHL_grant_house_credit = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				is_subject = no
				NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_south_indebted_to_SHL }
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 150 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_credit_cycles value = 6 }
		FROM = { set_country_flag = ADISCORD_south_indebted_to_SHL }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

# ----- Consolidation missions -----
# Started by the course focuses; the mission goal is the settled target.
# A term is not opened once the final war has started or the South is settled:
# its target and timeout demand are void after either point.

SHL_start_cons_debt_term = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_cons_debt_term }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_final_started }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_final_resolved }
		}
		activate_mission = SHL_cons_debt_term
	}
}

SHL_start_cons_charter_round = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_cons_charter_round }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_final_started }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_final_resolved }
		}
		activate_mission = SHL_cons_charter_round
	}
}

SHL_start_cons_ultimatum = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_cons_ultimatum }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_final_started }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_final_resolved }
		}
		activate_mission = SHL_cons_ultimatum
	}
}

SHL_start_cons_toll_term = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_cons_toll_term }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_final_started }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_final_resolved }
		}
		activate_mission = SHL_cons_toll_term
	}
}

SHL_start_cons_councils = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_cons_councils }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_final_started }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_final_resolved }
		}
		activate_mission = SHL_cons_councils
	}
}

SHL_cons_campaign_won = {
	add_political_power = 25
	ADISCORD_south_pull_clock_campaign = yes
}

# ----- Council elections (cities) -----

SHL_start_council_election = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_council_election }
		}
		activate_mission = SHL_council_election
	}
}

# The election counts at the deadline and schedules the next one.
SHL_hold_council_election = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 40 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_political_power = 50
		add_stability = 0.03
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.03 }
		SHL_balance_shift = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.05 }
		SHL_balance_shift = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 5 }
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
	}
	SHL_start_council_election = yes
}

# ----- Furnace school and quarter hospital (cities) -----
# Both use the per-site operation escrow: kinds 6 and 7.

SHL_begin_school = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				SHL_furnace_site_idle = yes
				NOT = { SHL_obligation_here = yes }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 2 compare = less_than }
			}
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 100 }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 6 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_deposit value = 100 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_pp value = 0 }
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_school = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 6 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes } }
			FROM = {
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training min = 0 max = 2 }
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_deposit
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_pp
				clear_variable = SHL_operation
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 2 }
			SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_operation = yes }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_begin_hospital = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				SHL_furnace_site_idle = yes
				NOT = { SHL_obligation_here = yes }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_quarter_hospital }
			}
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 120 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 120 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 120 }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 7 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_deposit value = 120 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_pp value = 0 }
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_hospital = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 7 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes } }
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = SHL_quarter_hospital
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_deposit
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_pp
				clear_variable = SHL_operation
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 4 }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 3 }
			SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_operation = yes }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# ----- Mobilisation quota (directorate) -----
# The state flag forces the arsenal recipient inside SHL_produce_site.

SHL_begin_quota = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_quota_site }
				NOT = { SHL_obligation_here = yes }
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			set_state_flag = SHL_quota_site
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 1 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 4 }
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_quota = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { has_state_flag = SHL_quota_site } }
		FROM = { clr_state_flag = SHL_quota_site }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment
				amount = 600
				producer = SHL
			}
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_cancel_quota = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { has_state_flag = SHL_quota_site } }
		FROM = { clr_state_flag = SHL_quota_site }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# ----- Directorate railway -----

SHL_begin_directorate_railway = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_railway_deposit }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 200 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 200 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 200 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_railway_deposit value = 200 }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_cancel_directorate_railway = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_railway_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_railway_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_railway_deposit }
		clear_variable = SHL_railway_deposit
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

# STATE scope. A level is added only where the site still has room.
SHL_deliver_railway_level = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = SHL
			is_controlled_by = SHL
			infrastructure < 5
		}
		add_building_construction = {
			type = infrastructure
			level = 1
			instant_build = yes
		}
	}
}

SHL_finish_directorate_railway = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_railway_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_country_operable = yes }
			clear_variable = SHL_railway_deposit
			294 = { SHL_deliver_railway_level = yes }
			699 = { SHL_deliver_railway_level = yes }
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_directorate_railway = yes }
	}
}

# ----- State concessions (directorate) -----

# STATE scope. A state furnace returns to its house for a lease payment.
SHL_lease_state_furnace = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 2 compare = equals }
			NOT = { SHL_obligation_here = yes }
		}
		SHL_assign_site_owner_house = yes
		SHL = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 100 }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_lease_count value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 10 }
		}
		SHL_apply_house_loyalty = yes
	}
}

SHL_settle_leases = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_lease_count value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.05 }
		SHL_balance_shift = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_lease_count
}

SHL_grant_state_concession = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_lease_count value = 0 }
	FROM = { SHL_lease_state_furnace = yes }
	SHL_settle_leases = yes
}

SHL_lease_all_state_furnaces = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_lease_count value = 0 }
	287 = { SHL_lease_state_furnace = yes }
	288 = { SHL_lease_state_furnace = yes }
	289 = { SHL_lease_state_furnace = yes }
	290 = { SHL_lease_state_furnace = yes }
	291 = { SHL_lease_state_furnace = yes }
	292 = { SHL_lease_state_furnace = yes }
	293 = { SHL_lease_state_furnace = yes }
	294 = { SHL_lease_state_furnace = yes }
	295 = { SHL_lease_state_furnace = yes }
	SHL_settle_leases = yes
}

# ----- Crest concessions and the regent's guard (regency) -----

# The sale cooldown is a timed flag so a wartime focus can lift it.
SHL_sell_crest_concessions = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_concessions_sold }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 300 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_debt_added value = 300 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_debt value = 500 }
		ADISCORD_economy_reprice_debt = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 5 }
		SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						ADISCORD_south_final_active = yes
						has_completed_focus = SHL_r_sell_future
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = SHL_concessions_sold
				value = 1
				days = 180
			}
		}
	}
}

SHL_begin_regent_guard = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_regent_guard_deposit }
			699 = {
				is_owned_by = SHL
				is_controlled_by = SHL
			}
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_manpower < 3000 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 150 }
		add_manpower = -3000
		set_variable = { var = SHL_regent_guard_deposit value = 150 }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_cancel_regent_guard = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_regent_guard_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_regent_guard_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_regent_guard_deposit }
		add_manpower = 3000
		clear_variable = SHL_regent_guard_deposit
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_regent_guard = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_regent_guard_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = {
				SHL_country_operable = yes
				699 = {
					is_owned_by = SHL
					is_controlled_by = SHL
				}
			}
			clear_variable = SHL_regent_guard_deposit
			SHL_ensure_levy_template = yes
			699 = { SHL_create_levy = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_idea = SHL_restored_house_guard } }
				add_ideas = SHL_restored_house_guard
			}
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_regent_guard = yes }
	}
}

# FROM is the pledged furnace. The tribunal forecloses at once for 40 PP.
SHL_tribunal_foreclose = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_obligation_kind value = 4 compare = equals }
			NOT = { has_political_power < 40 }
			FROM = {
				SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
				SHL_obligation_here = yes
			}
		}
		add_political_power = -40
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 1 }
			SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -30 } }
			SHL_apply_house_loyalty = yes
		}
		SHL_clear_obligation = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 8 }
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# ----- Desert guard price (treasury) -----

SHL_hire_guard = {
	SHL_refresh_programme_prices = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_guard_hired }
			699 = {
				is_owned_by = SHL
				is_controlled_by = SHL
			}
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_guard_price compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_guard_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_guard_price }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_ensure_levy_template = yes
		699 = { SHL_create_levy = yes }
		# Hiring contracts lift the 120-day interval during the War for the South.
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						ADISCORD_south_final_active = yes
						OR = {
							has_completed_focus = SHL_h_hired_contracts
							has_completed_focus = SHL_r_hire_armies
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = SHL_guard_hired
				value = 1
				days = 120
			}
		}
	}
}

# ----- Worst-furnace picker -----
# Inputs: SHL_pick_owner (0/1/2, 9 = any owner), SHL_pick_training_below
# (3 = any), SHL_pick_skip_opposed (1 skips houses already in opposition).
# Output: SHL_pick_site (0 when no lit furnace qualifies) and SHL_pick_wear.

# Inputs: SHL_pick_owner (9 = any), SHL_pick_training_below, SHL_pick_skip_opposed
# and SHL_pick_by_stock (1 = the lit furnace with the smallest stock instead of
# the most worn one; SHL_pick_wear then carries the stock of the chosen site).
SHL_pick_worst_site = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_wear value = -1 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_by_stock value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_wear value = 99 }
	}
	287 = { SHL_consider_pick_site = yes }
	288 = { SHL_consider_pick_site = yes }
	289 = { SHL_consider_pick_site = yes }
	290 = { SHL_consider_pick_site = yes }
	291 = { SHL_consider_pick_site = yes }
	292 = { SHL_consider_pick_site = yes }
	293 = { SHL_consider_pick_site = yes }
	294 = { SHL_consider_pick_site = yes }
	295 = { SHL_consider_pick_site = yes }
}

# STATE scope.
SHL_consider_pick_site = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_by_stock value = 1 compare = equals } }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL.SHL_pick_wear compare = less_than }
				}
				AND = {
					NOT = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_by_stock value = 1 compare = equals } } }
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = SHL.SHL_pick_wear compare = greater_than }
				}
			}
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = SHL.SHL_pick_training_below compare = less_than }
			OR = {
				SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_owner value = 9 compare = equals } }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = SHL.SHL_pick_owner compare = equals }
			}
			OR = {
				SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_skip_opposed value = 0 compare = equals } }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_house_opposed }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_by_stock value = 1 compare = equals } } }
			SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_wear value = PREV.SHL_furnace_stock } }
		}
		else = { SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_wear value = PREV.SHL_furnace_wear } } }
		SHL_record_pick_site = yes
	}
}

SHL_record_pick_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { state = 287 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 287 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 288 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 288 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 289 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 289 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 290 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 290 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 291 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 291 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 292 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 292 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 293 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 293 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 294 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 294 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 295 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 295 } }
	}
}

SHL_clear_pick = {
	clear_variable = SHL_pick_site
	clear_variable = SHL_pick_wear
	clear_variable = SHL_pick_owner
	clear_variable = SHL_pick_training_below
	clear_variable = SHL_pick_skip_opposed
	clear_variable = SHL_pick_by_stock
}

# STATE scope.
SHL_train_site = {
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training value = 1 }
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_training min = 0 max = 2 }
}

SHL_train_pick_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 287 compare = equals } }
		287 = { SHL_train_site = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 288 compare = equals } }
		288 = { SHL_train_site = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 289 compare = equals } }
		289 = { SHL_train_site = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 290 compare = equals } }
		290 = { SHL_train_site = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 291 compare = equals } }
		291 = { SHL_train_site = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 292 compare = equals } }
		292 = { SHL_train_site = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 293 compare = equals } }
		293 = { SHL_train_site = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 294 compare = equals } }
		294 = { SHL_train_site = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 295 compare = equals } }
		295 = { SHL_train_site = yes }
	}
}

# ----- Shift school, brotherly supply and strike support (commune) -----

SHL_begin_shift_school = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_shift_school_deposit }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 80 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 80 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 80 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_shift_school_deposit value = 80 }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_cancel_shift_school = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_shift_school_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_shift_school_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_shift_school_deposit }
		clear_variable = SHL_shift_school_deposit
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

# The most worn lit furnace that still has room for a trained shift.
SHL_finish_shift_school = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_shift_school_deposit }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_owner value = 9 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_training_below value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_skip_opposed value = 0 }
		SHL_pick_worst_site = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				SHL_country_operable = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			}
			clear_variable = SHL_shift_school_deposit
			SHL_train_pick_site = yes
			SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_shift_school = yes }
		SHL_clear_pick = yes
	}
}

# STATE scope candidate. The first city furnace with a spare packet donates.
SHL_consider_brotherly_donor = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 0 compare = equals } }
			SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		SHL_record_brotherly_donor = yes
	}
}

SHL_record_brotherly_donor = {
	if = {
		limit = { state = 287 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 287 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 288 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 288 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 289 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 289 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 290 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 290 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 291 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 291 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 292 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 292 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 293 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 293 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 294 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 294 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 295 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 295 } }
	}
}

# STATE scope; PREV is SHL. Moves SHL_brotherly_delta packets at the donor.
SHL_adjust_donor_stock = {
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = PREV.SHL_brotherly_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock min = 0 max = 3 }
}

SHL_adjust_brotherly_donor = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 287 compare = equals } }
		287 = { SHL_adjust_donor_stock = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 288 compare = equals } }
		288 = { SHL_adjust_donor_stock = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 289 compare = equals } }
		289 = { SHL_adjust_donor_stock = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 290 compare = equals } }
		290 = { SHL_adjust_donor_stock = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 291 compare = equals } }
		291 = { SHL_adjust_donor_stock = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 292 compare = equals } }
		292 = { SHL_adjust_donor_stock = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 293 compare = equals } }
		293 = { SHL_adjust_donor_stock = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 294 compare = equals } }
		294 = { SHL_adjust_donor_stock = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 295 compare = equals } }
		295 = { SHL_adjust_donor_stock = yes }
	}
}

# FROM is the receiving furnace. The donor's packet leaves at once and
# arrives seven days later; one transfer at a time.
SHL_begin_brotherly_supply = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_brotherly_donor }
			FROM = {
				SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 1 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 0 }
		287 = { SHL_consider_brotherly_donor = yes }
		288 = { SHL_consider_brotherly_donor = yes }
		289 = { SHL_consider_brotherly_donor = yes }
		290 = { SHL_consider_brotherly_donor = yes }
		291 = { SHL_consider_brotherly_donor = yes }
		292 = { SHL_consider_brotherly_donor = yes }
		293 = { SHL_consider_brotherly_donor = yes }
		294 = { SHL_consider_brotherly_donor = yes }
		295 = { SHL_consider_brotherly_donor = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_donor value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_delta value = -1 }
			SHL_adjust_brotherly_donor = yes
			clear_variable = SHL_brotherly_delta
			FROM = { set_state_flag = SHL_brotherly_recipient }
			SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		}
		else = { clear_variable = SHL_brotherly_donor }
	}
}

SHL_cancel_brotherly_supply = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_brotherly_donor }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_brotherly_delta value = 1 }
		SHL_adjust_brotherly_donor = yes
		clear_variable = SHL_brotherly_delta
		clear_variable = SHL_brotherly_donor
		FROM = { clr_state_flag = SHL_brotherly_recipient }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_brotherly_supply = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_brotherly_donor }
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
					has_state_flag = SHL_brotherly_recipient
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock min = 0 max = 3 }
				clr_state_flag = SHL_brotherly_recipient
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 2 }
			SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
			clear_variable = SHL_brotherly_donor
			SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_brotherly_supply = yes }
	}
}

# FROM is the supported country (GLP or MZR). The receipt lives on the target
# so both neighbours can be supported at once.
SHL_begin_strike_support = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_political_power < 20 }
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				is_subject = no
				NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
				NOT = { has_variable = SHL_strike_support_deposit }
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 50 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 50 }
		add_political_power = -20
		FROM = { set_variable = { var = SHL_strike_support_deposit value = 50 } }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_cancel_strike_support = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { has_variable = SHL_strike_support_deposit } }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = FROM.SHL_strike_support_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = FROM.SHL_strike_support_deposit }
		add_political_power = 20
		FROM = { clear_variable = SHL_strike_support_deposit }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

# Backed councils gain influence only while their government is open to negotiations.
SHL_finish_strike_support = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { has_variable = SHL_strike_support_deposit } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				SHL_country_operable = yes
				FROM = {
					exists = yes
					NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				clear_variable = SHL_strike_support_deposit
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_cons_councils_support value = 25 }
				clamp_variable = { var = SHL_cons_councils_support min = 0 max = 100 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = SHL_cons_councils_support value = 75 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						ADISCORD_south_campaign_target_settled = no
					}
					set_country_flag = SHL_cons_councils_backed
					SHL_back_coalition_delegation = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_strike_support = yes }
	}
}

# ----- Road stations (houses) -----

SHL_sell_road_stations = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_can_sell_road_stations = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 150 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		if = {
			limit = { 234 = { is_owned_by = SHL } }
			KDR = { transfer_state = 234 }
			234 = { set_state_controller_to = KDR }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 242 = { is_owned_by = SHL } }
			KDR = { transfer_state = 242 }
			242 = { set_state_controller_to = KDR }
		}
		234 = { remove_claim_by = SHL }
		242 = { remove_claim_by = SHL }
	}
}

# ----- Second act: the house demand -----
# Every fourth cycle after ratification the house (in the city and commune
# courses the quarter) with the most worn lit furnace presents a demand.

SHL_house_demand_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_house_demand }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_demand_pending }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_demand_cycle value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_demand_cycle value = 4 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_demand_cycle value = 0 }
			SHL_pick_demand_site = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				set_country_flag = SHL_demand_pending
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.140 }
			}
		}
	}
}

SHL_pick_demand_site = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_owner value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_quarter_demand_course = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_owner value = 1 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_training_below value = 3 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_skip_opposed value = 1 }
	SHL_pick_worst_site = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = SHL_pick_site }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_demand_wear value = SHL_pick_wear }
	SHL_clear_pick = yes
}

SHL_close_house_demand = {
	clr_country_flag = SHL_demand_pending
	set_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = 0 }
	clear_variable = SHL_demand_wear
}

SHL_house_demand_pay = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 100 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_demand_loyalty value = 10 }
		SHL_change_demand_house_loyalty = yes
		SHL_close_house_demand = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Input SHL_demand_loyalty for the demanding house; a quarter's
# demand in the city and commune courses moves no house.
SHL_change_demand_house_loyalty = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { SHL_quarter_demand_course = yes }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = 0 compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_site value = SHL_demand_site }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = SHL_demand_loyalty }
		SHL_change_site_loyalty = yes
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_demand_loyalty
}

SHL_house_demand_yield = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_quarter_demand_course = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.05 }
		SHL_balance_shift = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.05 }
		SHL_balance_shift = yes
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_demand_loyalty value = 5 }
	SHL_change_demand_house_loyalty = yes
	SHL_close_house_demand = yes
}

# The demand site stays recorded for the mission goal.
SHL_house_demand_refuse = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_quarter_demand_course = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 10 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { var = SHL_demand_loyalty value = -25 }
		SHL_change_demand_house_loyalty = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -5 }
		SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
	}
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
	clr_country_flag = SHL_demand_pending
	SHL_start_house_demand = yes
}

SHL_start_house_demand = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_active_mission = SHL_house_demand }
		}
		activate_mission = SHL_house_demand
	}
}

SHL_house_demand_settled = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_quarter_demand_course = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 5 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_demand_loyalty value = 15 }
	SHL_change_demand_house_loyalty = yes
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
	SHL_close_house_demand = yes
}

# STATE scope. The opposed house loses its cabinet seat for 180 days.
SHL_mark_site_opposed = {
	set_state_flag = {
		flag = SHL_house_opposed
		value = 1
		days = 180
	}
}

SHL_house_demand_timeout = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = 287 compare = equals } }
		287 = { SHL_mark_site_opposed = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = 288 compare = equals } }
		288 = { SHL_mark_site_opposed = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = 289 compare = equals } }
		289 = { SHL_mark_site_opposed = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = 290 compare = equals } }
		290 = { SHL_mark_site_opposed = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = 291 compare = equals } }
		291 = { SHL_mark_site_opposed = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = 292 compare = equals } }
		292 = { SHL_mark_site_opposed = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = 293 compare = equals } }
		293 = { SHL_mark_site_opposed = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = 294 compare = equals } }
		294 = { SHL_mark_site_opposed = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_demand_site value = 295 compare = equals } }
		295 = { SHL_mark_site_opposed = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_quarter_demand_course = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 10 }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_demand_loyalty value = -15 }
	SHL_change_demand_house_loyalty = yes
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
	SHL_close_house_demand = yes
}

# ----- Neighbour revanche -----
# Scheduled 365 days after a won limited war against KYZ or GLP; Mazar fights
# only as Keizan's partner and cedes nothing. Neighbour codes: 1 KYZ, 2 GLP.
# Only land still held by Shahrabad is claimed.

SHL_resolve_revanche = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_revanche_neighbour value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_revanche_kyz_ready = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_revanche_neighbour value = 1 }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_revanche_due_KYZ
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SHL_revanche_glp_ready = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_revanche_neighbour value = 2 }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_revanche_due_GLP
	}
	SHL_revanche_claims = yes
}

# STATE scope helpers.
SHL_revanche_claim_kyz = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_direct_or_administration_land = yes }
		add_claim_by = KYZ
	}
}

SHL_revanche_claim_glp = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_direct_or_administration_land = yes }
		add_claim_by = GLP
	}
}

SHL_revanche_claims = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_revanche_neighbour value = 1 compare = equals } }
		280 = { SHL_revanche_claim_kyz = yes }
		284 = { SHL_revanche_claim_kyz = yes }
		285 = { SHL_revanche_claim_kyz = yes }
		286 = { SHL_revanche_claim_kyz = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_revanche_neighbour value = 2 compare = equals } }
		297 = { SHL_revanche_claim_glp = yes }
		298 = { SHL_revanche_claim_glp = yes }
		299 = { SHL_revanche_claim_glp = yes }
		708 = { SHL_revanche_claim_glp = yes }
	}
}

SHL_revanche_drop_claims = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_revanche_neighbour value = 1 compare = equals } }
		280 = { remove_claim_by = KYZ }
		284 = { remove_claim_by = KYZ }
		285 = { remove_claim_by = KYZ }
		286 = { remove_claim_by = KYZ }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_revanche_neighbour value = 2 compare = equals } }
		297 = { remove_claim_by = GLP }
		298 = { remove_claim_by = GLP }
		299 = { remove_claim_by = GLP }
		708 = { remove_claim_by = GLP }
	}
}

# The revision buys the claim back for 150, or 90 through the border
# settlement office.
SHL_revanche_revision = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_revanche_price value = 150 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_border_settlement_office }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_revanche_price value = 90 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_revanche_neighbour value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_revanche_price compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_revanche_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_revanche_price }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_revanche_drop_claims = yes
		set_variable = { var = SHL_revanche_neighbour value = 0 }
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_revanche_price
}

SHL_revanche_refuse = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_revanche_neighbour value = 0 }
}

# ----- Integration of satellites (postwar epilogue) -----
# COUNTRY satellite. Each course pays SHL_integration_price per annexed
# satellite; the regional tags are the only possible subjects.

SHL_integrate_satellite = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = yes
			is_subject_of = SHL
			SHL = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_integration_price compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			# Junior partners chosen by a postwar focus keep their satellite status.
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					tag = KDR
					SHL = { has_completed_focus = SHL_r_kadir_junior }
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					tag = GLP
					SHL = { has_completed_focus = SHL_h_veyr_junior_partner }
				}
			}
		}
		SHL = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_integration_price }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_integration_price }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_integrated_count value = 1 }
			annex_country = {
				target = PREV
				transfer_troops = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

SHL_integrate_all_satellites = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_integrated_count value = 0 }
	NAM = { SHL_integrate_satellite = yes }
	EFL = { SHL_integrate_satellite = yes }
	AZH = { SHL_integrate_satellite = yes }
	WEF = { SHL_integrate_satellite = yes }
	KDR = { SHL_integrate_satellite = yes }
	RHM = { SHL_integrate_satellite = yes }
	SDR = { SHL_integrate_satellite = yes }
	MZR = { SHL_integrate_satellite = yes }
	KYZ = { SHL_integrate_satellite = yes }
	GLP = { SHL_integrate_satellite = yes }
}

SHL_finish_integration = {
	clear_variable = SHL_integration_price
	clear_variable = SHL_integrated_count
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# Houses: buyout for treasury.
SHL_annex_integration_houses = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_price value = 150 }
	SHL_integrate_all_satellites = yes
	SHL_finish_integration = yes
}

# Cities: voluntary accession by consent.
SHL_annex_integration_cities = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_price value = 0 }
	SHL_integrate_all_satellites = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_integrated_count value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		add_stability = 0.05
	}
	SHL_finish_integration = yes
}

# Directorate: occupied districts under a general governorate.
SHL_annex_integration_directorate = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_price value = 0 }
	SHL_integrate_all_satellites = yes
	SHL_form_furnace_administrations = yes
	SHL_finish_integration = yes
}

# Regency: fiefs and debts, 100 cash and 200 debt per satellite.
SHL_annex_integration_regency = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_price value = 0 }
	SHL_integrate_all_satellites = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_integrated_count value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_cash value = SHL_integrated_count }
		multiply_variable = { var = SHL_integration_cash value = 100 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_integration_cash }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_debt_added value = SHL_integration_cash }
		multiply_variable = { var = SHL_integration_cash value = 2 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_debt value = SHL_integration_cash }
		ADISCORD_economy_reprice_debt = yes
		clear_variable = SHL_integration_cash
	}
	SHL_finish_integration = yes
}

# Commune: the councils merge.
SHL_annex_integration_commune = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_price value = 0 }
	SHL_integrate_all_satellites = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_integrated_count value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 10 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_city_support value = 5 }
		SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
	}
	SHL_finish_integration = yes
}

# ----- News for the neighbours -----

# COUNTRY SHL. A ratified course is regional news once; the course value
# selects the page and show_major picks the readers.
SHL_announce_course_news = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_course_news_sent } }
		set_country_flag = SHL_course_news_sent
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 1 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL_news.1 hours = 6 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 2 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL_news.2 hours = 6 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 3 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL_news.3 hours = 6 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 4 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL_news.4 hours = 6 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL_news.5 hours = 6 }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. The first limited war is news once, whichever neighbour it is.
SHL_announce_first_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_first_war_announced } }
		set_country_flag = SHL_first_war_announced
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL_news.6 hours = 6 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Keizan's march on the Zarkhan district has been repelled.
SHL_announce_keizan_repelled = {
	news_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL_news.8 hours = 6 }
}

# Ratification and accession can follow the military settlement.
SHL_update_ruler_portrait = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_hegemon_SHL
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_unopposed }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_leader = { character = SHL_Jalil_Nur ruling_only = yes } }
			set_portraits = {
				character = SHL_Jalil_Nur
				civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_SHL_Jalil_Nur_victory }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_leader = { character = SHL_Darius_Safir ruling_only = yes } }
			set_portraits = {
				character = SHL_Darius_Safir
				civilian = {
					large = GFX_portrait_SHL_Darius_Safir
					small = GFX_portrait_SHL_Darius_Safir_small
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_leader = { character = SHL_Mariam_Arbin ruling_only = yes } }
			set_portraits = {
				character = SHL_Mariam_Arbin
				civilian = {
					large = GFX_portrait_SHL_Mariam_Arbin_victory
					small = GFX_portrait_SHL_Mariam_Arbin_small
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_leader = { character = SHL_Salima_Darzi ruling_only = yes } }
			set_portraits = {
				character = SHL_Salima_Darzi
				civilian = {
					large = GFX_portrait_SHL_Salima_Darzi_victory
					small = GFX_portrait_SHL_Salima_Darzi_small
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_leader = { character = SHL_Jalil_Nur ruling_only = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_portraits = {
				character = SHL_Jalil_Nur
				civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_SHL_Jalil_Nur_tenth_house }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_portraits = {
				character = SHL_Jalil_Nur
				civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_SHL_Jalil_Nur_directorate }
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. The Crown of the South, announced to the whole region.
SHL_announce_crown_of_the_south = {
	news_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL_news.7 hours = 6 }
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Metal contracts of the Golden Gates. Buyers are the six fixed tags of the
# SHL_metal_contract_offer decision, so no settlement scans the world. The
# buyer carries SHL_metal_client and SHL_metal_arrears; Shahrabad counts
# SHL_metal_clients. Each buyer pays its agreed rate on the Conclave's cycle.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY buyer, ROOT of ADISCORD_SHL.130. Both sides are rechecked at the answer.
SHL_metal_establish_contract = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = SHL_metal_offer_pending
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_metal_client }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_metal_offer_declined }
			NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
			SHL = {
				SHL_country_operable = yes
				SHL_metal_export_furnace_free = yes
				check_variable = { var = SHL_metal_clients value = SHL_metal_max_clients compare = less_than }
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = SHL_metal_offer_pending
		set_country_flag = SHL_metal_client
		clr_country_flag = SHL_metal_final_pour
		set_variable = { var = SHL_metal_arrears value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_metal_efficiency_growth value = 0.15 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_metal_efficiency_cap value = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_metal_resource_penalty value = -0.10 }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_metal_supply }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL = {
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_metal_clients value = 1 }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.131 hours = 6 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = SHL_metal_offer_pending
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = SHL_metal_offer_lapsed
			value = 1
			days = 7
		}
		SHL = {
			if = {
				limit = { SHL_country_operable = yes }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.132 hours = 6 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Start only the accepted term; a second answer cannot reset its countdown.
SHL_metal_sign_contract = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = SHL_metal_offer_pending
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_metal_client }
		}
		SHL_metal_establish_contract = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_metal_client }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_metal_cycle_price value = 20 }
			activate_mission = SHL_metal_contract_year
			SHL = { SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes }
		}
	}
	else = { SHL_metal_establish_contract = yes }
}

SHL_metal_sign_contract_two_years = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = SHL_metal_offer_pending
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_metal_client }
		}
		SHL_metal_establish_contract = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_metal_client }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_metal_cycle_price value = 15 }
			activate_mission = SHL_metal_contract_two_years
			SHL = { SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes }
		}
	}
	else = { SHL_metal_establish_contract = yes }
}

# COUNTRY buyer, ROOT of ADISCORD_SHL.130.
SHL_metal_decline_contract = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = SHL_metal_offer_pending
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_metal_client }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_metal_offer_declined }
		}
		clr_country_flag = SHL_metal_offer_pending
		set_country_flag = SHL_metal_offer_declined
		SHL = {
			if = {
				limit = { SHL_country_operable = yes }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.132 hours = 6 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY client. Ends the contract on both sides without a report; the
# SHL_metal_contract_end decision and the annexation cleanup use it directly.
SHL_metal_drop_client = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_metal_client }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_metal_client
		clr_country_flag = SHL_metal_final_pour
		clear_variable = SHL_metal_arrears
		clear_variable = SHL_metal_cycle_price
		clear_variable = SHL_forecast_min_treasury
		clear_variable = SHL_forecast_contract_receipt
		clear_variable = SHL_metal_efficiency_growth
		clear_variable = SHL_metal_efficiency_cap
		clear_variable = SHL_metal_resource_penalty
		remove_mission = SHL_metal_contract_year
		remove_mission = SHL_metal_contract_two_years
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_metal_supply } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_metal_supply }
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_metal_clients value = 1 }
			clamp_variable = { var = SHL_metal_clients min = 0 max = 6 }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# Buyer decisions and term expiries settle between pours. Losses detected during
# production share the refresh after all six accounts have settled.
SHL_metal_close_account = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_metal_client }
		SHL_metal_drop_client = yes
		SHL = { SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes }
	}
}

# COUNTRY client. Involuntary end: the Conclave is told in ADISCORD_SHL.133.
SHL_metal_end_contract = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_metal_client }
		SHL_metal_drop_client = yes
		SHL = {
			if = {
				limit = { SHL_country_operable = yes }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.133 hours = 6 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY client. The brokers' premium is credited only after the buyer pays.
SHL_metal_settle_client = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_metal_client }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					exists = no
					has_war_with = SHL
				}
			}
			SHL_metal_end_contract = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_metal_cycle_price compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_metal_cycle_price }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_metal_cycle_price }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_metal_arrears value = 0 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			SHL = {
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = PREV.SHL_metal_cycle_price }
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = PREV.SHL_metal_cycle_price }
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_last_cycle_income value = PREV.SHL_metal_cycle_price }
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_metal_brokers }
					add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 15 }
					add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 15 }
					add_to_variable = { var = SHL_last_cycle_income value = 15 }
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_metal_arrears value = 1 compare = less_than } }
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_metal_arrears value = 1 }
		}
		else = { SHL_metal_end_contract = yes }
		# Native contract timers and the 28-day pour have different origins.
		# Only on a pour is the interval to the next scheduled payment known.
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = SHL_metal_client
				OR = {
					AND = {
						has_active_mission = SHL_metal_contract_year
						check_variable = { var = days_mission_timeout@SHL_metal_contract_year value = 28 compare = less_than }
					}
					AND = {
						has_active_mission = SHL_metal_contract_two_years
						check_variable = { var = days_mission_timeout@SHL_metal_contract_two_years value = 28 compare = less_than }
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = SHL_metal_final_pour
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL, inside SHL_produce_period while SHL_metal_clients > 0.
SHL_metal_settle_clients = {
	VAL = { SHL_metal_settle_client = yes }
	STP = { SHL_metal_settle_client = yes }
	STS = { SHL_metal_settle_client = yes }
	RUS = { SHL_metal_settle_client = yes }
	GLP = { SHL_metal_settle_client = yes }
	NAM = { SHL_metal_settle_client = yes }
}

# COUNTRY SHL, annexation cleanup. Every buyer loses the flag and the modifier
# with Shahrabad's other receipts; the count is reset for a later restoration.
SHL_metal_clear_clients = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_metal_clients value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		VAL = { SHL_metal_drop_client = yes }
		STP = { SHL_metal_drop_client = yes }
		STS = { SHL_metal_drop_client = yes }
		RUS = { SHL_metal_drop_client = yes }
		GLP = { SHL_metal_drop_client = yes }
		NAM = { SHL_metal_drop_client = yes }
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_metal_clients value = 0 }
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Conclave levers. Decisions opened by the main-tree and course focuses. Site
# levers reuse the furnace escrow (SHL_operation on the site, settled by
# SHL_cancel_operation), the pick helpers and the tension scales. Measures
# (SHL_measure_*) hold the Conclave's attention through the timed country flag
# SHL_political_action and deliver their result in remove_effect only.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# ----- Shared site helpers -----

# STATE scope. Applies the country inputs SHL_site_wear_delta and SHL_site_stock_delta.
SHL_apply_site_change = {
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = SHL.SHL_site_wear_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear min = 0 max = 100 }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = SHL.SHL_site_stock_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock min = 0 max = 3 }
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Applies the pending site change to the site chosen by SHL_pick_worst_site.
SHL_apply_pick_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 287 compare = equals } }
		287 = { SHL_apply_site_change = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 288 compare = equals } }
		288 = { SHL_apply_site_change = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 289 compare = equals } }
		289 = { SHL_apply_site_change = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 290 compare = equals } }
		290 = { SHL_apply_site_change = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 291 compare = equals } }
		291 = { SHL_apply_site_change = yes }
	}
	if = {
