		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 292 compare = equals } }
		292 = { SHL_apply_site_change = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 293 compare = equals } }
		293 = { SHL_apply_site_change = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 294 compare = equals } }
		294 = { SHL_apply_site_change = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 295 compare = equals } }
		295 = { SHL_apply_site_change = yes }
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_site_wear_delta
	clear_variable = SHL_site_stock_delta
}

# COUNTRY SHL. One packet for the lit furnace with the smallest stock among the
# furnaces of SHL_pick_owner (9 = any owner).
SHL_stock_lowest_site = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_training_below value = 3 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_skip_opposed value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_by_stock value = 1 }
	SHL_pick_worst_site = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_site_wear_delta value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_site_stock_delta value = 1 }
		SHL_apply_pick_site = yes
	}
}

# ----- Quarter charters -----

# STATE furnace site, inside SHL_refresh_furnaces. A charter lapses with the
# district or with a state takeover of its furnace; only lit charters feed
# the cycle rule in SHL_update_tensions.
SHL_reconcile_charter_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = SHL_quarter_charter }
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_charter_site_valid = yes }
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_charters_active value = 1 } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
					check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
				}
				SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_charters_running value = 1 } }
			}
		}
		else = { clr_state_flag = SHL_quarter_charter }
	}
}

# FROM is the furnace site. Operation 9: the charter is granted after 21 days.
SHL_begin_charter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_charters_active value = 3 compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			FROM = { SHL_charter_site_ready = yes }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 60 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 60 }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 9 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_deposit value = 60 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_pp value = 0 }
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_charter = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 9 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_charters_active value = 3 compare = less_than }
				FROM = {
					SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
					SHL_charter_site_valid = yes
					NOT = { has_state_flag = SHL_quarter_charter }
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = SHL_quarter_charter
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_deposit
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_pp
				clear_variable = SHL_operation
			}
			SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_operation = yes }
	}
}

# ----- Dispatch order -----

# FROM is the donor site. Operation 11: the packet leaves the donor at once
# and reaches the lit furnace with the smallest stock after 7 days. A lost
# donor cannot take it back, so cancellation delivers the same way.
SHL_begin_dispatch = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			FROM = { SHL_dispatch_donor_ready = yes }
		}
		FROM = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 11 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_deposit value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_pp value = 0 }
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_dispatch = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 11 compare = equals } } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_owner value = 9 }
		SHL_stock_lowest_site = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 0 compare = equals }
				FROM = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes }
			}
			FROM = {
				add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock min = 0 max = 3 }
			}
		}
		SHL_clear_pick = yes
		FROM = {
			clear_variable = SHL_operation_deposit
			clear_variable = SHL_operation_pp
			clear_variable = SHL_operation
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# ----- Convening, arbitration and audit -----

# STATE scope. A lit house furnace receives one packet from the convened Conclave.
SHL_convene_site = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 0 compare = equals }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock min = 0 max = 3 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. The native 40 PP price is charged by the decision.
SHL_convene_conclave_effect = {
	287 = { SHL_convene_site = yes }
	288 = { SHL_convene_site = yes }
	289 = { SHL_convene_site = yes }
	290 = { SHL_convene_site = yes }
	291 = { SHL_convene_site = yes }
	292 = { SHL_convene_site = yes }
	293 = { SHL_convene_site = yes }
	294 = { SHL_convene_site = yes }
	295 = { SHL_convene_site = yes }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 5 }
	SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
	SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
}

# COUNTRY SHL. The native 15 PP price is charged by the decision.
SHL_arbitration_act_effect = {
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 6 }
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
}

# FROM is the furnace site. Operation 12: the counting chamber inspects for
# 21 days; the native 30 PP price returns through the escrow if the site is lost.
SHL_begin_audit = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			FROM = { SHL_furnace_site_idle = yes }
		}
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 12 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_deposit value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_operation_pp value = 30 }
		}
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_audit = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = SHL_operation value = 12 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes } }
			FROM = {
				subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 10 }
				clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear min = 0 max = 100 }
				set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_shortages value = 0 }
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_deposit
				clear_variable = SHL_operation_pp
				clear_variable = SHL_operation
				# The audited house resents the counting chamber.
				SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = -15 } }
				SHL_apply_house_loyalty = yes
			}
			SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
			SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_operation = yes }
	}
}

# ----- Desert embassy -----

# FROM is the neighbour. One mission travels at a time; the deposit returns if
# the neighbour vanishes or goes to war with Shahrabad before it arrives.
SHL_begin_embassy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_embassy_deposit }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			FROM = { SHL_embassy_target_open = yes }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 50 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 50 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_embassy_deposit value = 50 }
		FROM = { set_country_flag = SHL_embassy_pending }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_embassy = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_embassy_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					exists = yes
					has_country_flag = SHL_embassy_pending
					NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				clr_country_flag = SHL_embassy_pending
				set_country_flag = SHL_embassy
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = SHL modifier = ADISCORD_SHL_embassy }
			}
			clear_variable = SHL_embassy_deposit
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_embassy = yes }
	}
}

SHL_cancel_embassy = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_embassy_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_embassy_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_embassy_deposit }
		clear_variable = SHL_embassy_deposit
		FROM = { clr_country_flag = SHL_embassy_pending }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

# ----- Measures of the Conclave -----
# Every measure runs 60 days; the lockout flag expires with it and is cleared
# again on completion and cancellation.

SHL_begin_measure = {
	set_country_flag = {
		flag = SHL_political_action
		value = 1
		days = 60
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# COUNTRY SHL. Input: temp SHL_measure_price. Treasury-priced measures debit
# once here; the native PP price of the others is charged by the decision.
SHL_begin_paid_measure = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_measure_price compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_measure_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_measure_price }
		SHL_begin_measure = yes
	}
}

SHL_end_measure = {
	clr_country_flag = SHL_political_action
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

SHL_finish_crest_patronage = {
	SHL_end_measure = yes
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.05 }
	SHL_balance_shift = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_loyalty_delta value = 5 }
	SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
}

# STATE scope. A lit furnace selling abroad earns the fair bonus.
SHL_fair_site = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 compare = equals }
		}
		SHL = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 30 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 30 }
		}
	}
}

SHL_finish_golden_fair = {
	287 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
	288 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
	289 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
	290 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
	291 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
	292 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
	293 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
	294 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
	295 = { SHL_fair_site = yes }
	SHL_end_measure = yes
}

SHL_finish_quarter_assembly = {
	SHL_end_measure = yes
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.05 }

	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
}

SHL_finish_open_books = {
	SHL_end_measure = yes

	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
}

SHL_finish_directorate_decree = {
	SHL_end_measure = yes

	SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 3 }
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_owner value = 2 }
	SHL_stock_lowest_site = yes
	SHL_clear_pick = yes
	SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
}

SHL_finish_war_bonds = {

	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_debt_added value = 200 }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_debt value = 300 }
	ADISCORD_economy_reprice_debt = yes
}

SHL_finish_crest_court = {

	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
}

SHL_finish_fief_grant = {

	SHL_change_all_loyalty = yes
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = -0.03 }
	SHL_balance_shift = yes
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
}

SHL_finish_common_assembly = {
	SHL_end_measure = yes
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_balance_shift value = 0.05 }
	SHL_balance_shift = yes

	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
}

SHL_finish_shift_reserve = {
	SHL_end_measure = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_reserve_stock value = 2 }
	SHL_redraw_furnace_transaction = yes
}

# ----- War orders for every furnace -----

# STATE scope. Sites under a quota, an obligation or another order are skipped.
# The chosen recipient is kept so SHL_produce_site restores it after the cycle.
SHL_war_order_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_war_order_site_open = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_war_orders_prev_recipient value = SHL_furnace_recipient }
		set_state_flag = SHL_quota_site
		set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 1 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. The native 40 PP price is charged by the decision.
SHL_issue_war_orders_all = {
	287 = { SHL_war_order_site = yes }
	288 = { SHL_war_order_site = yes }
	289 = { SHL_war_order_site = yes }
	290 = { SHL_war_order_site = yes }
	291 = { SHL_war_order_site = yes }
	292 = { SHL_war_order_site = yes }
	293 = { SHL_war_order_site = yes }
	294 = { SHL_war_order_site = yes }
	295 = { SHL_war_order_site = yes }
	SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
}

# ----- Integration of one satellite -----

# FROM is the satellite. The course price is paid at once and held for the
# 30-day accession in SHL_integration_deposit / SHL_integration_deposit_pp;
# the annexation itself goes through SHL_integrate_satellite at zero price.
SHL_begin_integration = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_integration_deposit }
			SHL_integration_affordable = yes
			FROM = { SHL_integration_target = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_deposit value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_deposit_pp value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_deposit value = 300 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_deposit_pp value = 100 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 4 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_deposit value = 200 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 5 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_deposit_pp value = 50 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_integration_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_integration_deposit }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_pp_debit value = 0 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_integration_pp_debit value = SHL_integration_deposit_pp }
		add_political_power = SHL_integration_pp_debit
		clear_variable = SHL_integration_pp_debit
		FROM = { set_country_flag = SHL_integration_pending }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

SHL_finish_integration_decision = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_integration_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					exists = yes
					is_subject_of = SHL
					has_country_flag = SHL_integration_pending
				}
			}
			FROM = { clr_country_flag = SHL_integration_pending }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_integration_price value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_integrated_count value = 0 }
			FROM = { SHL_integrate_satellite = yes }
			clear_variable = SHL_integration_deposit
			clear_variable = SHL_integration_deposit_pp
			SHL_finish_integration = yes
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_integration = yes }
	}
}

SHL_cancel_integration = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_integration_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_integration_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_integration_deposit }
		add_political_power = SHL_integration_deposit_pp
		clear_variable = SHL_integration_deposit
		clear_variable = SHL_integration_deposit_pp
		FROM = { clr_country_flag = SHL_integration_pending }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# ----- Rewards for the course trees -----

# COUNTRY SHL. Repairs the most worn lit furnace by 30.
SHL_repair_worst_site = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_owner value = 9 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_training_below value = 3 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_skip_opposed value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_pick_by_stock value = 0 }
	SHL_pick_worst_site = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_pick_site value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_site_wear_delta value = -30 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_site_stock_delta value = 0 }
		SHL_apply_pick_site = yes
	}
	SHL_clear_pick = yes
	SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
}

# STATE scope.
SHL_stock_city_site = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_owner value = 1 compare = equals }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock min = 0 max = 3 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. One packet for every city furnace Shahrabad controls.
SHL_stock_city_sites = {
	287 = { SHL_stock_city_site = yes }
	288 = { SHL_stock_city_site = yes }
	289 = { SHL_stock_city_site = yes }
	290 = { SHL_stock_city_site = yes }
	291 = { SHL_stock_city_site = yes }
	292 = { SHL_stock_city_site = yes }
	293 = { SHL_stock_city_site = yes }
	294 = { SHL_stock_city_site = yes }
	295 = { SHL_stock_city_site = yes }
	SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
}

# COUNTRY SHL. The first batch of the chosen programme ending (SHL_prog_ending):
# houses 11/12 take the export advance, cities 21 build in the capital and 22
# arm the quarters, the directorate 31/32 arms the regiments, the regency
# 41/42 collects the pledge and the commune 51/52 repairs its worst furnace.
SHL_prog_first_batch = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 11 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 12 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 90 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 90 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 21 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				699 = {
					is_owned_by = SHL
					is_controlled_by = SHL
					infrastructure < 5
				}
			}
			699 = { add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 22 compare = equals } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 750
			producer = SHL
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 31 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 32 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 750
			producer = SHL
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0
			amount = 5
			producer = SHL
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 41 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 42 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 150 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 51 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_prog_ending value = 52 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		SHL_repair_worst_site = yes
	}
}

# Route-specific names belong only to non-story party representatives.
SHL_party_representatives_house = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SHL
			check_variable = { var = SHL_political_course value = 1 compare = equals }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character = SHL_party_representative_humanism
					NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = SHL_party_representative_humanism } }
				}
				set_character_name = {
					character = SHL_party_representative_humanism
					name = SHL_party_representatives_house_humanism
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character = SHL_party_representative_utilitarism
					NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = SHL_party_representative_utilitarism } }
				}
				set_character_name = {
					character = SHL_party_representative_utilitarism
					name = SHL_party_representatives_house_utilitarism
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character = SHL_party_representative_chauvinism
					NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = SHL_party_representative_chauvinism } }
				}
				set_character_name = {
					character = SHL_party_representative_chauvinism
					name = SHL_party_representatives_house_chauvinism
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character = SHL_party_representative_technocracy
					NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = SHL_party_representative_technocracy } }
				}
				set_character_name = {
					character = SHL_party_representative_technocracy
					name = SHL_party_representatives_house_technocracy
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character = SHL_party_representative_etatism
					NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = SHL_party_representative_etatism } }
				}
				set_character_name = {
					character = SHL_party_representative_etatism
					name = SHL_party_representatives_house_etatism
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Second row of shared branches: the four districts, foreign affairs and the
# Book of Furnaces. The shared focuses call these effects; the decisions of the
# "Four districts and foreign affairs" section keep their own escrows.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY SHL. Input: temp SHL_income_delta (may be negative). The running
# total is paid once per cycle by SHL_produce_period in country scope.
SHL_add_cycle_income = {
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_cycle_bonus_income value = SHL_income_delta }
	SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
}

# ----- District garrisons -----

SHL_ensure_border_watch_template = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_template = "Border Watch" } }
		division_template = {
			name = "Border Watch"
			override_model = SHL_ADISCORD_militia_entity
			template_counter = 80
			division_names_group = SHL_GAR_01
			is_locked = yes
			force_allow_recruiting = no
			regiments = {
				infantry = {
					x = 0
					y = 0
				}
				infantry = {
					x = 0
					y = 1
				}
				ADISCORD_militia = {
					x = 1
					y = 0
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# STATE scope, owner PREV. Rifles come from the owner's stockpile; the caller
# has already settled the treasury price.
SHL_create_border_watch_unit = {
	create_unit = {
		division = "division_template = \"Border Watch\" start_experience_factor = 0.1 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
		owner = PREV
		allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. One garrison division in the Loran sands (731); a district that
# is not held sends the patrol to the capital instead.
SHL_create_sand_patrol = {
	SHL_ensure_border_watch_template = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			731 = {
				is_owned_by = SHL
				is_controlled_by = SHL
			}
		}
		731 = { SHL_create_border_watch_unit = yes }
	}
	else = { capital_scope = { SHL_create_border_watch_unit = yes } }
}

# COUNTRY SHL. One garrison division on the Veyran border (733), same fallback.
SHL_create_border_watch = {
	SHL_ensure_border_watch_template = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			733 = {
				is_owned_by = SHL
				is_controlled_by = SHL
			}
		}
		733 = { SHL_create_border_watch_unit = yes }
	}
	else = { capital_scope = { SHL_create_border_watch_unit = yes } }
}

# ----- Border watch order (SHL_raise_border_watch) -----

SHL_begin_border_watch_order = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_border_watch_deposit }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 60 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 60 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_border_watch_deposit value = 60 }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_cancel_border_watch_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_border_watch_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_border_watch_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_border_watch_deposit }
		clear_variable = SHL_border_watch_deposit
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

# A border district lost during the thirty days refunds the order.
SHL_finish_border_watch_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_border_watch_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = {
				SHL_country_operable = yes
				733 = {
					is_owned_by = SHL
					is_controlled_by = SHL
				}
			}
			SHL_create_border_watch = yes
			clear_variable = SHL_border_watch_deposit
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_border_watch_order = yes }
	}
}

# ----- Bayrat prospecting (SHL_prospect_copi) -----

# COUNTRY SHL. The deposit blocks a second expedition until settlement; the
# counter SHL_copi_prospected caps the programme at two deliveries.
SHL_begin_copi_prospecting = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_country_operable = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_copi_deposit }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_copi_prospected value = 2 compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 120 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 120 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 120 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_copi_deposit value = 120 }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

SHL_cancel_copi_prospecting = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_copi_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_copi_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_copi_deposit }
		clear_variable = SHL_copi_deposit
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
	}
}

# The chromium is booked only while Shahrabad still holds the Bayrat mines.
SHL_finish_copi_prospecting = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_copi_deposit }
		if = {
			limit = {
				SHL_country_operable = yes
				732 = {
					is_owned_by = SHL
					is_controlled_by = SHL
				}
			}
			732 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = chromium
					amount = 3
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_copi_prospected value = 1 }
			clear_variable = SHL_copi_deposit
		}
		else = { SHL_cancel_copi_prospecting = yes }
	}
}

# ----- Eflor deposits and accord (ADISCORD_SHL.150-152) -----

# COUNTRY SHL. Eflor answers in ADISCORD_SHL.150 (FROM is Shahrabad); a missing
# or hostile Eflor is an immediate refusal.
SHL_eflor_offer_investment = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			EFL = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
			}
		}
		EFL = {
			ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.150 days = 3 }
		}
	}
	else = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.152 days = 3 } }
}

# COUNTRY EFL, option a of ADISCORD_SHL.150. The Safir site is rechecked at
# delivery; a full state receives the slot the investor pays for.
SHL_eflor_accept_investment = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
			SHL = { SHL_country_operable = yes }
		}
		288 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size = 1
							include_locked = no
						}
					}
				}
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 60 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 60 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY SHL. The 80 wait in SHL_accord_deposit until Eflor answers .151;
# every refusal path returns them through SHL_eflor_refuse_accord.
SHL_eflor_offer_accord = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_accord_deposit }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 80 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			EFL = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
			}
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 80 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 80 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_accord_deposit value = 80 }
		SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
		EFL = {
			ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.151 days = 3 }
		}
	}
	else = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.152 days = 3 } }
}

# COUNTRY EFL, option a of ADISCORD_SHL.151. The deposit becomes Eflor's fee;
# the two Safir factories are queued in Shahrabad's construction line.
SHL_eflor_accept_accord = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
			SHL = {
				SHL_country_operable = yes
				has_variable = SHL_accord_deposit
			}
		}
		730 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size = 1
							include_locked = no
						}
					}
				}
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size = 2
							include_locked = no
						}
					}
				}
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 2
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL.SHL_accord_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL.SHL_accord_deposit }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = SHL
			modifier = ADISCORD_SHL_eflor_partner
		}
		SHL = {
			clear_variable = SHL_accord_deposit
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = EFL
				modifier = ADISCORD_SHL_eflor_partner
			}
		}
	}
	else = { SHL_eflor_refuse_accord = yes }
}

# COUNTRY EFL (option b) or SHL. Returns the accord deposit once.
SHL_eflor_refuse_accord = {
	SHL = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = SHL_accord_deposit }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_accord_deposit }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_accord_deposit }
			clear_variable = SHL_accord_deposit
			SHL_refresh_forecast_if_changed = yes
		}
	}
}

# ----- Fuel treaty with Svetlogorye (ADISCORD_SHL.153-155) -----

# COUNTRY SHL. Svetlogorye answers in ADISCORD_SHL.153 (FROM is Shahrabad).
SHL_nam_offer_fuel_treaty = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_nam_fuel_treaty }
			NAM = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
			}
		}
		NAM = {
			ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.153 days = 3 }
		}
	}
	else = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.154 days = 3 } }
}

# COUNTRY NAM, option a of ADISCORD_SHL.153. The flag is read by
# SHL_refresh_packet_price; the embargo and a war suspend it without clearing it.
SHL_nam_accept_fuel_treaty = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = SHL }
			SHL = { SHL_country_operable = yes }
		}
		SHL = {
			set_country_flag = SHL_nam_fuel_treaty
			SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
			add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.155 }
		}
	}
	else = { SHL = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.154 } } }
}

# ----- Voice of the South and the Book of Furnaces -----

# COUNTRY SHL. The Voice of the South, announced to the region.
SHL_announce_voice_of_the_south = {
	news_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL_news.9 hours = 6 }
}

# COUNTRY SHL. The second page of the first chapter opens at once while the
# 90-day dispatcher has not reached chapter one (SHL_book_next = 1). After that
# ADISCORD_shl_book.1 offers the page itself and hides it once the focus is done.
SHL_open_second_volume = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = no
			check_variable = { var = SHL_book_next value = 2 compare = less_than }
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_book.11 }
	}
}

# ----- Territorial furnace administrations -----
# COUNTRY SHL. Annex callbacks run before the generic handler's final state
# writes; a single delayed dispatch consumes all acquisitions in that tick.
SHL_queue_furnace_administrations = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_furnace_administrations_enabled = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_furnace_administrations_pending }
		}
		set_country_flag = SHL_furnace_administrations_pending
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_shl_fire.12 hours = 1 }
	}
}

# STATE. The recipient is local to this dispatch, never a persistent global target.
SHL_transfer_furnace_administration_land = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_furnace_administration_land = yes }
		add_core_of = event_target:SHL_furnace_recipient
		event_target:SHL_furnace_recipient = { transfer_state = PREV }
	}
}

# COUNTRY recipient. Repeated land grants preserve politics, armies and autonomy.
SHL_finish_furnace_administration = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = yes
			is_subject = no
		}
		SHL = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = PREV
				autonomy_state = autonomy_SHL_furnace_administration
				freedom_level = 0.0
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = yes }
		ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_south_final_active = yes }
			ADISCORD_south_bind_to_champion = yes
		}
	}
}
