# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Under the namestnik's seal: the chapter after a Svetlogorye victory in the
# South. Country scope SHL unless a comment says otherwise.
# SHL_seal_chapter_open is the lifecycle marker and is never cleared. A leased
# furnace carries recipient 4; the terms are read from the native autonomy
# state, never stored. SHL_cold_rising is 1 during the war of the rising, 2
# after its victory and 3 after its defeat. All recurring work runs from
# SHL_produce_period through SHL_seal_cycle.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# ----- Opening and terms -----

# Called once from the southern settlement after the puppet and its autonomy
# exist. A capitulated subject can keep its land under the victor's control,
# so control of Shahrabad's own states is restored before the lease starts.
SHL_seal_open_chapter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_hegemon_NAM
			has_country_flag = SHL_furnaces_initialized
			is_subject_of = NAM
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_seal_chapter_open }
		}
		set_country_flag = SHL_seal_chapter_open
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_controlled_by = SHL } }
			set_state_controller_to = SHL
		}
		SHL_seal_lease_by_terms = yes
		SHL_seal_refresh_terms = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		NAM = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.200 days = 2 }
	}
}

# Leases the districts named by the autonomy terms in a fixed order: Safir,
# Bayrat and Sharim under every term, Arbin from the oil charter, Nur and
# Veyran from the plain puppet, the rest under the namestnik's office.
SHL_seal_lease_by_terms = {
	288 = { SHL_seal_lease_site = yes }
	291 = { SHL_seal_lease_site = yes }
	293 = { SHL_seal_lease_site = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_NAM_economic_contract } }
		287 = { SHL_seal_lease_site = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_NAM_oil_charter } }
			290 = { SHL_seal_lease_site = yes }
			295 = { SHL_seal_lease_site = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet } }
				289 = { SHL_seal_lease_site = yes }
				292 = { SHL_seal_lease_site = yes }
				294 = { SHL_seal_lease_site = yes }
			}
		}
	}
}

# STATE furnace site. The house keeps the furnace and its vote; only the output
# changes hands. Orders and quotas that claimed the furnace are settled first.
SHL_seal_lease_site = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = SHL
			has_variable = SHL_furnace_recipient
			NOT = { SHL_seal_site_leased = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_obligation_here = yes }
			SHL = { SHL_cancel_obligation = yes }
		}
		clr_state_flag = SHL_quota_site
		clear_variable = SHL_war_orders_prev_recipient
		set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 4 }
	}
}

# Share, buy-back price and the number of leased furnaces of the current terms.
SHL_seal_refresh_terms = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_lease_share value = 0.80 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_buyback_price value = 420 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_NAM_economic_contract }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_lease_share value = 0.40 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_buyback_price value = 240 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_NAM_oil_charter }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_lease_share value = 0.50 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_buyback_price value = 300 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_lease_share value = 0.65 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_buyback_price value = 360 }
	}
	# SHL_redeem_by_coin sets the temporary marker for the refresh in its own
	# reward, where the focus is not yet completed.
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = SHL_redeem_by_coin
				check_variable = { var = SHL_seal_coin_discount_now value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		multiply_variable = { var = SHL_seal_buyback_price value = 0.8 }
	}
	# The AI keeps 200 of its treasury after a buy-back.
	set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_ai_reserve value = SHL_seal_buyback_price }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_seal_ai_reserve value = 200 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_leased_count value = 0 }
	287 = { SHL_seal_count_leased_site = yes }
	288 = { SHL_seal_count_leased_site = yes }
	289 = { SHL_seal_count_leased_site = yes }
	290 = { SHL_seal_count_leased_site = yes }
	291 = { SHL_seal_count_leased_site = yes }
	292 = { SHL_seal_count_leased_site = yes }
	293 = { SHL_seal_count_leased_site = yes }
	294 = { SHL_seal_count_leased_site = yes }
	295 = { SHL_seal_count_leased_site = yes }
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
}

# STATE furnace site; PREV is SHL.
SHL_seal_count_leased_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_seal_site_leased = yes }
		SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_seal_leased_count value = 1 } }
	}
}

# ----- The lease in the furnace cycle -----

# STATE furnace site; called only from SHL_deliver_site_heat after the stock
# was used. The namestnik's share leaves SHL_period_cash before
# SHL_collect_site_income books the remainder. A furnace serving its
# redemption pays its whole output and counts down one producing cycle.
SHL_seal_settle_lease_site = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_lease_due value = SHL_period_cash }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SHL_seal_service_cycles } }
		multiply_variable = { var = SHL_seal_lease_due value = SHL.SHL_seal_lease_share }
	}
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_period_cash value = SHL_seal_lease_due }
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = SHL_seal_cold_shift }
		# The cooled pour goes to the caches; the namestnik receives nothing.
		clr_state_flag = SHL_seal_cold_shift
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = 6 }
		SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_rifles value = 300 } }
	}
	else = {
		SHL = {
			set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_lease_due value = PREV.SHL_seal_lease_due }
			SHL_seal_pay_namestnik = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = SHL_seal_service_cycles }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_seal_service_cycles value = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_seal_service_cycles value = 0 compare = less_than_or_equals } }
				SHL_seal_redeem_site = yes
				SHL = { SHL_seal_after_redemption = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_seal_lease_due
}

# The tenth furnace follows the lease of district 294; its income pays the same
# share, or all of it while furnace VIII serves its redemption.
SHL_seal_settle_lease_tenth = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_lease_due value = SHL_tenth_period_income }
	if = {
		limit = { 294 = { NOT = { has_variable = SHL_seal_service_cycles } } }
		multiply_variable = { var = SHL_seal_lease_due value = SHL_seal_lease_share }
	}
	subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_tenth_period_income value = SHL_seal_lease_due }
	SHL_seal_pay_namestnik = yes
}

# Input SHL_seal_lease_due. Furnace cash is the only channel from Shahrabad's
# treasury to Svetlogorye's; the autonomy transfers move factories, trade and
# manpower. SHL_produce_period releases the lease of a country without the
# overlord before the pour, so the clear below only drops a zero or stray due.
SHL_seal_pay_namestnik = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_subject_of = NAM
			check_variable = { var = SHL_seal_lease_due value = 0 compare = greater_than }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_seal_cycle_lease value = SHL_seal_lease_due }
		NAM = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = PREV.SHL_seal_lease_due }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = PREV.SHL_seal_lease_due }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_seal_lease_due
}

# STATE furnace site. The furnace returns to export; the player reassigns it.
SHL_seal_redeem_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_seal_site_leased = yes }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_recipient value = 0 }
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_seal_service_cycles
	clr_state_flag = SHL_seal_cold_shift
}

# The last redeemed furnace closes the lease and eases the autonomy one step.
SHL_seal_after_redemption = {
	SHL_seal_refresh_terms = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_seal_leased_count value = 0 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			is_subject_of = NAM
		}
		SHL_seal_close_lease = yes
		SHL_seal_refresh_terms = yes
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

SHL_seal_close_lease = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_NAM_economic_contract }
		NAM = { end_puppet = SHL }
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_faction_with = NAM }
			leave_faction = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_NAM_oil_charter }
		NAM = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = SHL
				autonomy_state = autonomy_NAM_economic_contract
				freedom_level = 0.60
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		NAM = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = SHL
				autonomy_state = autonomy_NAM_oil_charter
				freedom_level = 0.40
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
		}
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	NAM = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
	mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
}

# Every lease ends when Shahrabad leaves Svetlogorye's rule; an open petition
# is refunded once.
SHL_seal_release_all_leases = {
	287 = { SHL_seal_redeem_site = yes }
	288 = { SHL_seal_redeem_site = yes }
	289 = { SHL_seal_redeem_site = yes }
	290 = { SHL_seal_redeem_site = yes }
	291 = { SHL_seal_redeem_site = yes }
	292 = { SHL_seal_redeem_site = yes }
	293 = { SHL_seal_redeem_site = yes }
	294 = { SHL_seal_redeem_site = yes }
	295 = { SHL_seal_redeem_site = yes }
	SHL_seal_refund_petition = yes
	SHL_seal_refresh_terms = yes
}

# Independence through Svetlogorye's own decision, its disappearance or a new
# overlord releases the lease; the rising releases it itself. Called before the
# furnaces pour in SHL_produce_period and again in SHL_seal_cycle.
SHL_seal_release_without_overlord = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_seal_release_due = yes }
		SHL_seal_release_all_leases = yes
	}
}

# Once per furnace cycle, at the end of SHL_produce_period.
SHL_seal_cycle = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_seal_chapter_open }
		SHL_seal_release_without_overlord = yes
		# Freed before its rising, the underground reaches its goal without a
		# war: the result of a won rising, with the cached rifles delivered.
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_subject_of = NAM }
				has_variable = SHL_underground_heat
				NOT = { has_variable = SHL_cold_rising }
			}
			set_variable = { var = SHL_cold_rising value = 2 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_rifles value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = SHL_cold_cache_rifles
					producer = SHL
				}
			}
			SHL_cold_clear_pledges = yes
			SHL_cold_close_rising = yes
		}
		SHL_seal_refresh_terms = yes
		# The guarantor houses' loan: 25 for each of twelve cycles.
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_seal_bond_cycles value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 25 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 25 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_last_cycle_income value = 25 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_seal_bond_cycles value = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_seal_bond_cycles value = 0 compare = less_than_or_equals } }
				clear_variable = SHL_seal_bond_cycles
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = SHL_underground_heat
				NOT = { has_variable = SHL_cold_rising }
				is_subject_of = NAM
			}
			SHL_cold_grow_heat = yes
			SHL_cold_roll_raid = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_cold_rising value = 1 compare = equals }
				NAM = { exists = no }
			}
			SHL_cold_rising_victory = yes
		}
	}
}

# ----- Focus results of the lease route -----

# STATE furnace site; PREV is SHL.
SHL_seal_stock_free_site = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_furnace_site_controlled = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_running value = 1 compare = equals }
			NOT = { SHL_seal_site_leased = yes }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock min = 0 max = 3 }
	}
}

# One packet for every lit furnace Shahrabad controls outside the lease. The
# caller refreshes the furnaces.
SHL_seal_stock_free_sites = {
	287 = { SHL_seal_stock_free_site = yes }
	288 = { SHL_seal_stock_free_site = yes }
	289 = { SHL_seal_stock_free_site = yes }
	290 = { SHL_seal_stock_free_site = yes }
	291 = { SHL_seal_stock_free_site = yes }
	292 = { SHL_seal_stock_free_site = yes }
	293 = { SHL_seal_stock_free_site = yes }
	294 = { SHL_seal_stock_free_site = yes }
	295 = { SHL_seal_stock_free_site = yes }
}

# STATE furnace site.
SHL_seal_maintain_leased_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_seal_site_leased = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear value = -20 }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_wear min = 0 max = 100 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock value = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_furnace_stock min = 0 max = 3 }
	}
}

# The namestnik's engineers repair every leased furnace. The caller refreshes
# the furnaces.
SHL_seal_maintain_leased_sites = {
	287 = { SHL_seal_maintain_leased_site = yes }
	288 = { SHL_seal_maintain_leased_site = yes }
	289 = { SHL_seal_maintain_leased_site = yes }
	290 = { SHL_seal_maintain_leased_site = yes }
	291 = { SHL_seal_maintain_leased_site = yes }
	292 = { SHL_seal_maintain_leased_site = yes }
	293 = { SHL_seal_maintain_leased_site = yes }
	294 = { SHL_seal_maintain_leased_site = yes }
	295 = { SHL_seal_maintain_leased_site = yes }
}

# Attach the route spirit on first use, then recalculate it.
SHL_refresh_redeem_spirit = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_redeem_lease_dynamic } } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_redeem_lease_dynamic }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# The spirit lives only until the rising is decided: SHL_cold_close_rising
# removes it at every result, and a Cold Fire focus completed later must not
# attach it again.
SHL_refresh_cold_spirit = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SHL_cold_rising } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_cold_fire_dynamic } } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_cold_fire_dynamic }
		}
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# ----- Petitions to the namestnik -----
# One country escrow serves both forms: SHL_seal_petition_site names the
# furnace, _deposit holds the buy-back price (0 for service) and _kind is 1
# for coin or 2 for service; the 25 political power of the petition is fixed.
# ADISCORD_SHL.202 writes _answer (1 consent, 2 refusal). The decision's
# remove_effect settles it, its cancel_effect and the release of the lease
# refund it; each path clears the whole escrow.

# STATE furnace site.
SHL_seal_record_petition_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { state = 287 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_site value = 287 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 288 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_site value = 288 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 289 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_site value = 289 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 290 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_site value = 290 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 291 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_site value = 291 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 292 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_site value = 292 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 293 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_site value = 293 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 294 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_site value = 294 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { state = 295 }
		SHL = { set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_site value = 295 } }
	}
}

# FROM is the selected leased furnace; the price is re-read before the debit.
SHL_seal_begin_buy_back = {
	SHL_seal_refresh_terms = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_seal_petition_possible = yes
			SHL_seal_buyback_affordable = yes
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_seal_buyback_price }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_seal_buyback_price }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_deposit value = SHL_seal_buyback_price }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_kind value = 1 }
		SHL_seal_open_petition = yes
	}
}

SHL_seal_begin_service = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_seal_petition_possible = yes
			SHL_seal_service_affordable = yes
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_deposit value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_kind value = 2 }
		SHL_seal_open_petition = yes
	}
}

SHL_seal_open_petition = {
	add_political_power = -25
	clear_variable = SHL_seal_petition_answer
	FROM = { SHL_seal_record_petition_site = yes }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	NAM = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.202 hours = 6 } }
}

# FROM is the petitioned furnace. Consent transfers the deposit and the furnace
# in one step (coin) or starts four producing cycles of full service.
SHL_seal_settle_petition = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = SHL_seal_petition_site
			FROM = { SHL_seal_petition_here = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_answer value = 1 compare = equals }
				is_subject_of = NAM
				FROM = {
					is_owned_by = SHL
					SHL_seal_site_leased = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_kind value = 1 compare = equals } }
				NAM = {
					add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = PREV.SHL_seal_petition_deposit }
					add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = PREV.SHL_seal_petition_deposit }
					ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
				}
				SHL_seal_clear_petition = yes
				FROM = { SHL_seal_redeem_site = yes }
				SHL_seal_after_redemption = yes
			}
			else = {
				FROM = { set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_service_cycles value = 4 } }
				SHL_seal_clear_petition = yes
			}
			SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_seal_petition_answer value = 2 compare = equals } }
				FROM = {
					set_state_flag = {
						flag = SHL_seal_petition_refused
						value = 1
						days = 180
					}
				}
			}
			SHL_seal_refund_petition = yes
		}
	}
}

# FROM is the petitioned furnace (cancel_effect of the petition decisions).
SHL_seal_cancel_petition = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { SHL_seal_petition_here = yes } }
		SHL_seal_refund_petition = yes
	}
}

SHL_seal_refund_petition = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_seal_petition_site }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_seal_petition_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_seal_petition_deposit }
		add_political_power = 25
		SHL_seal_clear_petition = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

SHL_seal_clear_petition = {
	clear_variable = SHL_seal_petition_site
	clear_variable = SHL_seal_petition_deposit
	clear_variable = SHL_seal_petition_kind
	clear_variable = SHL_seal_petition_answer
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
}

# The guarantor houses lend 250 at once; SHL_seal_cycle collects 25 for twelve cycles.
SHL_seal_take_bond = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SHL_seal_bond_cycles } }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 250 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 250 }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_seal_bond_cycles value = 12 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL_refresh_selected_furnace = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

# ----- Cold Fire: the underground meter -----
# SHL_underground_heat (0-100) exists from the Cold Fire focus until the
# rising ends. Every writer goes through SHL_cold_add_heat.

# Input: temp SHL_cold_heat_delta.
SHL_cold_add_heat = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_underground_heat }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_underground_heat value = SHL_cold_heat_delta }
		clamp_variable = { var = SHL_underground_heat min = 0 max = 100 }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_gui_dirty value = 1 }
	}
}

# 2 to 7 per furnace cycle: base 2, one each for the cells and the couriers,
# two under the namestnik's office (one as a plain puppet) and one when the
# quarters are restless.
SHL_cold_grow_heat = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_cold_growth value = 2 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_cold_ash_cells }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_cold_growth value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_cold_quarter_couriers }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_cold_growth value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_cold_growth value = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_NAM_oil_charter }
			NOT = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_NAM_economic_contract }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_cold_growth value = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_cold_growth value = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cold_heat_delta value = SHL_cold_growth }
	clear_variable = SHL_cold_growth
	SHL_cold_add_heat = yes
}

# The modifiers scale only the raid weight against a fixed 85: about 15% per
# cycle from 30 heat, 23% from 50 and 29% from 70 under an ordinary overlord.
# Svetlogorye's military rule raises it and its local offices lower it. The
# martyrs focus spares 40 of every 100 raids in a second roll, so its -40% is
# exact at every heat. ADISCORD_SHL.203 sets the 84-day cooldown in its
# immediate block.
SHL_cold_roll_raid = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_underground_heat value = 30 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SHL_cold_raid_cooldown }
		}
		random_list = {
			85 = { }
			15 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.67
					check_variable = { var = SHL_underground_heat value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.4
					check_variable = { var = SHL_underground_heat value = 70 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.4
					SHL_seal_overlord_hardline = yes
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.7
					SHL_seal_overlord_conciliatory = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_cold_raid_martyrs }
					random_list = {
						40 = { }
						60 = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.203 hours = 6 } }
					}
				}
				else = {
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.203 hours = 6 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.203.a: the caches are handed over.
SHL_cold_raid_surrender = {
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_cells value = -1 }
	clamp_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_cells min = 0 max = 6 }
	multiply_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_rifles value = 0.5 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cold_heat_delta value = -10 }
	SHL_cold_add_heat = yes
}

# ADISCORD_SHL.203.b: the cells go deeper.
SHL_cold_raid_go_deeper = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cold_heat_delta value = -20 }
	SHL_cold_add_heat = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_unrest value = 10 }
	SHL_clamp_tensions = yes
}

# ----- Cold Fire: cells, caches and allies -----

# STATE furnace site.
SHL_cold_mark_shift_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_cold_shift_site_open = yes }
		set_state_flag = SHL_seal_cold_shift
	}
}

# The next pour of every lit leased furnace Shahrabad controls goes to the caches.
# Once the rising is decided the caches have no consumer, so the leases that a
# crushed rising restores are never cooled.
SHL_cold_mark_shifts = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SHL_cold_rising } }
		287 = { SHL_cold_mark_shift_site = yes }
		288 = { SHL_cold_mark_shift_site = yes }
		289 = { SHL_cold_mark_shift_site = yes }
		290 = { SHL_cold_mark_shift_site = yes }
		291 = { SHL_cold_mark_shift_site = yes }
		292 = { SHL_cold_mark_shift_site = yes }
		293 = { SHL_cold_mark_shift_site = yes }
		294 = { SHL_cold_mark_shift_site = yes }
		295 = { SHL_cold_mark_shift_site = yes }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# FROM is the selected leased furnace (SHL_cold_forge).
SHL_cold_begin_forge = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_cold_rising }
			FROM = { SHL_cold_shift_site_open = yes }
		}
		FROM = { set_state_flag = SHL_seal_cold_shift }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cold_heat_delta value = 4 }
		SHL_cold_add_heat = yes
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# A hidden cache holds its price in SHL_cold_cache_deposit until it is dug.
SHL_cold_begin_cache = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_cold_cache_deposit }
			NOT = { has_variable = SHL_cold_rising }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_cells value = 6 compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_political_power < 30 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 150 }
		add_political_power = -30
		set_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_deposit value = 150 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

SHL_cold_finish_cache = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_cold_cache_deposit }
		clear_variable = SHL_cold_cache_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_cells value = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_cold_heat_delta value = 2 }
		SHL_cold_add_heat = yes
	}
}

SHL_cold_cancel_cache = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = SHL_cold_cache_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_cold_cache_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL_cold_cache_deposit }
		add_political_power = 30
		clear_variable = SHL_cold_cache_deposit
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# The letters go to every independent neighbour outside Svetlogorye's alliance.
SHL_cold_send_pledges = {
	EFL = { SHL_cold_send_pledge = yes }
	AZH = { SHL_cold_send_pledge = yes }
	KYZ = { SHL_cold_send_pledge = yes }
	GLP = { SHL_cold_send_pledge = yes }
	MZR = { SHL_cold_send_pledge = yes }
}

# COUNTRY neighbour; Shahrabad is the sender of ADISCORD_SHL.204.
SHL_cold_send_pledge = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_cold_pledge_candidate = yes }
		ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = SHL_cold_pledge_pending
			value = 1
			days = 30
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.204 hours = 6 }
	}
}

# ----- Cold Fire: the rising -----
# SHL_cold_rising = 1 opens the war; the principals hold major status until
# either result, so an allied capitulation cannot end it. Only a principal's
# capitulation decides it (09_ADISCORD_scripted_peace_on_actions.txt); a war
# ended by a third party is decided by the controller of Khazar.

# COUNTRY principal of the war of the rising.
SHL_cold_reserve_principal = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = yes
			OR = {
				is_major = no
				has_country_flag = RUS_crisis_added_major
				RUS_crisis_other_major_required = yes
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = SHL_cold_added_major
		set_major = yes
	}
}

SHL_cold_release_principal = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_cold_added_major }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_added_major
		RUS_crisis_release_other_major = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY neighbour that promised rifles and can still keep its word.
SHL_cold_join_rising = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_cold_pledge_valid = yes }
		ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = no
				SHL = { has_country_flag = SHL_cold_faction }
			}
			SHL = { add_to_faction = PREV }
		}
		add_to_war = {
			targeted_alliance = SHL
			enemy = NAM
			hostility_reason = asked_to_join
		}
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledged
	}
}

# A cell becomes a levy in Khazar for 3000 real recruits; without them its
# rifles go to the arsenal.
SHL_cold_raise_cell = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_manpower < 3000 } }
		add_manpower = -3000
		699 = { SHL_create_levy = yes }
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 2700
			producer = SHL
		}
	}
}

# The reward of SHL_cold_night_of_fire. Shahrabad's own wars end before the
# marker is set, so their peace callbacks cannot settle the rising.
# Svetlogorye's other wars may continue; SHL_cold_close_external ignores their
# peace while Shahrabad is still at war with Svetlogorye.
SHL_cold_raise_rising = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_cold_rising_ready = yes }
		every_enemy_country = { white_peace = PREV }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_cold_rising value = 1 }
		NAM = { end_puppet = SHL }
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
				dismantle_faction = yes
			}
			else = { leave_faction = yes }
		}
		SHL_seal_release_all_leases = yes
		SHL_cold_reserve_principal = yes
		NAM = { SHL_cold_reserve_principal = yes }
		SHL_ensure_levy_template = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_cells value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			SHL_cold_raise_cell = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_cells value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			SHL_cold_raise_cell = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_cells value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			SHL_cold_raise_cell = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_cells value = 4 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			SHL_cold_raise_cell = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_cells value = 5 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			SHL_cold_raise_cell = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_cells value = 6 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			SHL_cold_raise_cell = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cold_cache_rifles value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment
				amount = SHL_cold_cache_rifles
				producer = SHL
			}
		}
		clear_variable = SHL_cold_cache_cells
		clear_variable = SHL_cold_cache_rifles
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = no
				OR = {
					EFL = {
						SHL_cold_pledge_valid = yes
						is_in_faction = no
					}
					AZH = {
						SHL_cold_pledge_valid = yes
						is_in_faction = no
					}
					KYZ = {
						SHL_cold_pledge_valid = yes
						is_in_faction = no
					}
					GLP = {
						SHL_cold_pledge_valid = yes
						is_in_faction = no
					}
					MZR = {
						SHL_cold_pledge_valid = yes
						is_in_faction = no
					}
				}
			}
			create_faction_from_template = {
				template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
				name = SHL_cold_fire_faction
			}
			set_country_flag = SHL_cold_faction
		}
		declare_war_on = {
			target = NAM
			type = independence_wargoal
		}
		EFL = { SHL_cold_join_rising = yes }
		AZH = { SHL_cold_join_rising = yes }
		KYZ = { SHL_cold_join_rising = yes }
		GLP = { SHL_cold_join_rising = yes }
		MZR = { SHL_cold_join_rising = yes }
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL_news.11 hours = 6 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		NAM = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY member of the rising: peace with Svetlogorye and its side, once.
SHL_cold_end_member_wars = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = yes
			OR = {
				tag = SHL
				has_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledged
			}
		}
		every_enemy_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = NAM
					is_subject_of = NAM
					is_in_faction_with = NAM
				}
			}
			white_peace = PREV
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledged
	clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledge_pending
}

SHL_cold_end_rising_wars = {
	SHL = { SHL_cold_end_member_wars = yes }
	EFL = { SHL_cold_end_member_wars = yes }
	AZH = { SHL_cold_end_member_wars = yes }
	KYZ = { SHL_cold_end_member_wars = yes }
	GLP = { SHL_cold_end_member_wars = yes }
	MZR = { SHL_cold_end_member_wars = yes }
}

# Shared cleanup of both results: the coalition of this campaign, the
# principals' major status, the route spirit and the underground.
SHL_cold_close_rising = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SHL_cold_faction }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_faction
		if = {
			limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
			dismantle_faction = yes
		}
	}
	SHL_cold_release_principal = yes
	NAM = { SHL_cold_release_principal = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_cold_fire_dynamic } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SHL_cold_fire_dynamic }
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_cld_stability
	clear_variable = SHL_cld_war_support
	clear_variable = SHL_cld_org
	clear_variable = SHL_cld_conscription
	clear_variable = SHL_cld_defence
	clear_variable = SHL_cld_attack
	clear_variable = SHL_cld_dig_in
	clear_variable = SHL_underground_heat
	clear_variable = SHL_cold_cache_cells
	clear_variable = SHL_cold_cache_rifles
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	NAM = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
	mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
}

# Svetlogorye fell or vanished: Shahrabad stays independent with its furnaces.
SHL_cold_rising_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cold_rising value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_cold_rising value = 2 }
		SHL_cold_end_rising_wars = yes
		SHL_cold_close_rising = yes
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL_news.11 hours = 6 }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# Shahrabad fell: the namestnik's office returns with all nine furnaces leased.
SHL_cold_rising_crushed = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_cold_rising value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_cold_rising value = 3 }
		SHL_cold_end_rising_wars = yes
		SHL_cold_close_rising = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NAM = { exists = yes } }
			NAM = {
				puppet = {
					target = SHL
					end_wars = no
					end_civil_wars = no
				}
				set_autonomy = {
					target = SHL
					autonomy_state = autonomy_NAM_namestnik_office
					freedom_level = 0.05
					end_wars = no
					end_civil_wars = no
				}
			}
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_controlled_by = SHL } }
				set_state_controller_to = SHL
			}
			SHL_seal_lease_by_terms = yes
			SHL_seal_refresh_terms = yes
		}
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = SHL_cold_ashes_idea
			days = 730
		}
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL_news.11 hours = 6 }
		SHL_refresh_furnaces = yes
	}
}

# From shahrabad:on_peace. A war of the rising closed by another script is
# decided by the controller of Khazar at that moment.
SHL_cold_close_external = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = yes
			check_variable = { var = SHL_cold_rising value = 1 compare = equals }
			NOT = { has_war_with = NAM }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SHL_cold_capital_held_by_rising = yes }
			SHL_cold_rising_victory = yes
		}
		else = { SHL_cold_rising_crushed = yes }
	}
}

# From shahrabad:on_annex when Shahrabad itself is annexed during the rising.
SHL_cold_close_annexed = {
	NAM = { SHL_cold_release_principal = yes }
	SHL = { SHL_cold_release_principal = yes }
	SHL_cold_clear_pledges = yes
}

# The neighbours' promises lapse once the rising can no longer be fought.
SHL_cold_clear_pledges = {
	EFL = {
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledged
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledge_pending
	}
	AZH = {
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledged
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledge_pending
	}
	KYZ = {
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledged
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledge_pending
	}
	GLP = {
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledged
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledge_pending
	}
	MZR = {
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledged
		clr_country_flag = SHL_cold_pledge_pending
	}
}

SHL_send_party_invoice = {
	set_country_flag = { flag = SHL_party_order_cooldown days = 30 }
	STP = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.205 } }
}

# A negative stockpile effect selects a producer. Measure each withdrawal before releasing payment or cargo.
SHL_road_measure_stock = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_stock value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_stock value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 3 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_stock value = num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 4 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_stock value = num_equipment@motorized_equipment }
	}
}

SHL_road_move_equipment = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 1 compare = equals } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = SHL_road_debit producer = PREV }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 2 compare = equals } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = SHL_road_debit producer = PREV }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 3 compare = equals } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = SHL_road_debit producer = PREV }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 4 compare = equals } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = SHL_road_debit producer = PREV }
	}
}

SHL_road_take_equipment = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_due value = SHL_road_price }
	SHL_road_measure_stock = yes
	set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_before value = SHL_road_stock }
	every_possible_country = {
		limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_due value = 0 compare = greater_than } } }
		PREV = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_debit value = SHL_road_due }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_debit value = -1 }
			SHL_road_move_equipment = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_removed value = SHL_road_stock }
			SHL_road_measure_stock = yes
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_removed value = SHL_road_stock }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_due value = SHL_road_removed }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = SHL_road_due value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = SHL_road_before value = SHL_road_stock compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_debit value = SHL_road_before }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_debit value = SHL_road_stock }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 1 compare = equals } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = SHL_road_debit producer = THIS }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 2 compare = equals } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = SHL_road_debit producer = THIS }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 3 compare = equals } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = SHL_road_debit producer = THIS }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 4 compare = equals } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = SHL_road_debit producer = THIS }
		}
	}
}

SHL_road_start = {
	clear_variable = SHL_road_deposit_refund
	clear_variable = SHL_road_home_cash
	clear_variable = SHL_road_home_rifles
	clear_variable = SHL_road_home_kits
	set_variable = { var = SHL_road_rifles value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_road_kits value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_road_cash value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_road_trucks value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_road_guns value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = SHL_road_fuel value = 0 }
	clear_variable = SHL_road_wine
	add_fuel = -400
	subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 10 }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 10 }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	set_variable = { var = SHL_road_itinerary value = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_northern_road }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_road_itinerary value = 3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_chancery_of_the_gate }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_road_itinerary value = 2 }
	}
	clear_variable = SHL_road_market_state
	set_variable = { var = SHL_road_leg value = 1 }
	activate_mission = SHL_road_to_sevir
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_sand_patrols }
		add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = SHL_road_to_sevir days = -6 }
	}
}

SHL_road_dispatch_rifles = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_road_ready = yes
			SHL_road_can_load_rifles = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_price value = 10000 }
		SHL_road_take_equipment = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_due value = 0 compare = equals } }
			SHL_road_start = yes
			set_variable = { var = SHL_road_rifles value = 10000 }
		}
	}
}

SHL_road_dispatch_kits = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_road_ready = yes
			SHL_road_can_load_kits = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 2 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_price value = 2000 }
		SHL_road_take_equipment = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_due value = 0 compare = equals } }
			SHL_road_start = yes
			set_variable = { var = SHL_road_kits value = 2000 }
		}
	}
}

SHL_road_open_market = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_road_market_open = yes }
		ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = SHL_road_offer
			days = 7
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_SHL.206 }
	}
}

SHL_road_deliver_lot = {
	clr_country_flag = SHL_road_offer
	if = {
		limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_rifles value = 2500 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } }
		SHL = { subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_road_rifles value = 2500 } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 2500 producer = SHL }
	}
	else = {
		SHL = { subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_road_kits value = 500 } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 500 producer = SHL }
	}
}

SHL_road_sell_for_cash = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_road_can_pay_cash = yes }
		SHL_road_deliver_lot = yes
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 75 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 75 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_road_cash value = 75 } }
	}
}

SHL_road_exchange = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_road_can_barter = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_market_state value = 280 compare = equals } } }
			SHL_road_deliver_lot = yes
			add_fuel = -2700
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_road_fuel value = 2700 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_market_state value = 267 compare = equals } } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_price value = 18 }
			SHL_road_take_equipment = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_due value = 0 compare = equals } }
				SHL_road_deliver_lot = yes
				SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_road_trucks value = 18 } }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_market_state value = 28 compare = equals } } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_price value = 12 }
			SHL_road_take_equipment = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_due value = 0 compare = equals } }
				SHL_road_deliver_lot = yes
				SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_road_guns value = 12 } }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_market_state value = 24 compare = equals } } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_item value = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_price value = 24 }
			SHL_road_take_equipment = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_due value = 0 compare = equals } }
				SHL_road_deliver_lot = yes
				SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_road_trucks value = 24 } }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_market_state value = 175 compare = equals } } }
			SHL_road_deliver_lot = yes
			add_fuel = -4500
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_road_fuel value = 4500 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_market_state value = 70 compare = equals } } }
			SHL_road_deliver_lot = yes
			add_fuel = -3600
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_road_fuel value = 3600 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_market_state value = 688 compare = equals } } }
			SHL_road_deliver_lot = yes
			add_fuel = -5400
			SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_road_fuel value = 5400 } }
		}
	}
}

# At home the private buyers bid on the outward cargo. Barter proceeds and wine
# belong to separate accounts and are unloaded in full.
SHL_road_home_market = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_completed_focus = SHL_chancery_of_the_gate
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = SHL_road_rifles value = 0 compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = SHL_road_kits value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_cash value = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_quantity value = SHL_road_rifles }
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_capacity value = 5000 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = SHL_road_wine }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_capacity value = 50000 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_kits value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_quantity value = SHL_road_kits }
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_capacity value = 1000 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_quarter value = SHL_road_home_capacity }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_quarter value = 0.5 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_quantity value = SHL_road_home_quarter compare = less_than_or_equals } }
			random = {
				chance = 80
				SHL_road_take_home_bid = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_quantity value = SHL_road_home_capacity compare = less_than_or_equals } }
			random = {
				chance = 60
				SHL_road_take_home_bid = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			random = {
				chance = 40
				SHL_road_take_home_bid = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

SHL_road_take_home_bid = {
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = SHL_road_home_quantity
		min = 0
		max = SHL_road_home_capacity
	}
	set_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_cash value = SHL_road_home_quantity }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_kits value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_kits value = SHL_road_home_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_road_kits value = SHL_road_home_quantity }
		divide_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_cash value = 10 }
	}
	else = {
		# Unloading takes the retained quantity from the head of the model manifest.
		# The remaining models go to the private buyers when the manifest closes.
		set_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_rifles value = SHL_road_home_quantity }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_road_rifles value = SHL_road_home_quantity }
		divide_variable = { var = SHL_road_home_cash value = 50 }
	}
	add_to_variable = { var = SHL_road_cash value = SHL_road_home_cash }
}

SHL_road_unload = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL = { has_variable = SHL_road_leg } }
		SHL = {
			clear_variable = SHL_road_leg
			clear_variable = SHL_road_market_state
			clear_variable = SHL_road_itinerary
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
		if = {
			limit = { SHL = { has_variable = SHL_road_wine } }
			SHL = { set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_order value = SHL_road_rifles } }
			SHL_road_release_rifles = yes
			SHL = {
				clear_array = SHL_road_rifle_models
				set_variable = { var = SHL_road_deposit_refund value = SHL_road_wine }
				multiply_variable = { var = SHL_road_deposit_refund value = 20 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL.SHL_road_deposit_refund }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL.SHL_road_deposit_refund }
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = SHL.SHL_road_rifles producer = SHL }
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = SHL.SHL_road_kits producer = SHL }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = SHL.SHL_road_trucks }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = SHL.SHL_road_guns }
		add_fuel = SHL.SHL_road_fuel
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL.SHL_road_cash }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = SHL.SHL_road_cash }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

SHL_road_settle_annexation = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL = { has_variable = SHL_road_leg } }
		SHL_road_unload = yes
		SHL = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_road_to_fada }
				remove_mission = SHL_road_to_fada
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_road_to_irim }
				remove_mission = SHL_road_to_irim
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_road_to_loren }
				remove_mission = SHL_road_to_loren
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_road_to_svetlogorsk }
				remove_mission = SHL_road_to_svetlogorsk
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_road_to_home }
				remove_mission = SHL_road_to_home
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_road_to_sevir }
				remove_mission = SHL_road_to_sevir
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_road_to_barak }
				remove_mission = SHL_road_to_barak
			}
			clear_variable = SHL_road_wine
			clear_variable = SHL_road_deposit_refund
			clear_array = SHL_road_rifle_models
			if = {
				limit = { has_active_mission = SHL_road_to_maren }
				remove_mission = SHL_road_to_maren
			}
			clear_variable = SHL_road_rifles
			clear_variable = SHL_road_kits
			clear_variable = SHL_road_cash
			clear_variable = SHL_road_trucks
			clear_variable = SHL_road_guns
			clear_variable = SHL_road_fuel
		}
	}
}

# PREV is the producer during loading and Shahrabad during delivery. The manifest keeps each rifle model separately.
SHL_road_move_rifle_model = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_model value = 0 compare = equals } } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = SHL.SHL_road_debit producer = PREV }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_model value = 1 compare = equals } } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_infantry_equipment_2156 amount = SHL.SHL_road_debit producer = PREV }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_model value = 2 compare = equals } } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_infantry_equipment_2163 amount = SHL.SHL_road_debit producer = PREV }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_model value = 3 compare = equals } } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_infantry_equipment_2168 amount = SHL.SHL_road_debit producer = PREV }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_model value = 4 compare = equals } } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_infantry_equipment_2170 amount = SHL.SHL_road_debit producer = PREV }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_model value = 5 compare = equals } } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_infantry_equipment_2178 amount = SHL.SHL_road_debit producer = PREV }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_model value = 6 compare = equals } } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_infantry_equipment_2183 amount = SHL.SHL_road_debit producer = PREV }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_model value = 7 compare = equals } } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_infantry_equipment_2193 amount = SHL.SHL_road_debit producer = PREV }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { SHL = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_model value = 8 compare = equals } } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_infantry_equipment_2200 amount = SHL.SHL_road_debit producer = PREV }
	}
}

SHL_road_release_rifles = {
	SHL = {
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = 9
			value = SHL_road_model
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_order value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_debit value = SHL_road_rifle_models^SHL_road_model }
				clamp_temp_variable = {
					var = SHL_road_debit
					min = 0
					max = SHL_road_order
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_debit value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					PREV = { SHL_road_move_rifle_model = yes }
					subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_road_rifle_models^SHL_road_model value = SHL_road_debit }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_order value = SHL_road_debit }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

SHL_road_dispatch_wholesale = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SHL_road_ready = yes
			SHL_road_can_load_wholesale = yes
		}
		clear_array = SHL_road_rifle_models
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = 9
			value = SHL_road_model
			add_to_array = {
				array = SHL_road_rifle_models
				value = 0
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_order value = 100000 }
		every_possible_country = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_order value = 0 compare = greater_than } } }
			PREV = {
				for_loop_effect = {
					end = 9
					value = SHL_road_model
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_order value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_stock value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
						set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_debit value = SHL_road_order }
						multiply_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_debit value = -1 }
						SHL_road_move_rifle_model = yes
						subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_stock value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
						add_to_variable = { var = SHL_road_rifle_models^SHL_road_model value = SHL_road_stock }
						subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_order value = SHL_road_stock }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_order value = 0 compare = equals } }
			SHL_road_start = yes
			set_variable = { var = SHL_road_rifles value = 100000 }
			set_variable = { var = SHL_road_wine value = 5 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 100 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 100 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_order value = 100000 }
			SHL_road_release_rifles = yes
			clear_array = SHL_road_rifle_models
		}
	}
}

SHL_road_buy_ten = {
	if = {
		limit = { SHL_road_can_buy_ten = yes }
		clr_country_flag = SHL_road_offer
		SHL = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_order value = 10000 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_road_rifles value = 10000 }
		}
		SHL_road_release_rifles = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_payment value = 300 }
		SHL_road_book_sale = yes
	}
}

SHL_road_book_sale = {
	subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = SHL_road_payment }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = SHL_road_payment }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	SHL = { add_to_variable = { var = SHL_road_cash value = PREV.SHL_road_payment } }
}

SHL_road_head_home = {
	set_variable = { var = SHL_road_leg value = 8 }
	activate_mission = SHL_road_to_home
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_itinerary value = 1 compare = equals } }
		add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = SHL_road_to_home days = -55 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_sand_patrols }
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = SHL_road_to_home days = -6 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = SHL_road_itinerary value = 2 compare = equals } }
		add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = SHL_road_to_home days = -25 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_sand_patrols }
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = SHL_road_to_home days = -12 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = SHL_sand_patrols }
		add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = SHL_road_to_home days = -17 }
	}
}

SHL_road_deliver_twenty = {
	SHL = {
		set_temp_variable = { var = SHL_road_order value = 20000 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_road_rifles value = 20000 }
	}
	SHL_road_release_rifles = yes
}

SHL_road_deliver_wine = {
	SHL = { subtract_from_variable = { var = SHL_road_wine value = 1 } }
	add_timed_idea = {
		idea = SHL_cellar_wines
		days = 90
	}
}

		SHL_road_book_sale = yes
	}
}

		add_to_variable = { var = SHL_cartel_consumer_goods value = -0.05 }
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
